TL;DR: 本文报告了在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6中，针对跨城市目标检测任务，在气隙的Training-as-a-Service平台（仅返回聚合COCO-style AP）上对单个RF-DETR-Large检测器的存档配置进行分析的结果。冻结1120×1120推理（训练于704×704）无需参数更新即达到最高聚合AP（0.3272→0.3654,+0.0382），其中小目标相对增益最大、中目标绝对增益最大。热启动1120px微调达到0.3470但域内验证AP上升（0.767→0.789），表明在仅聚合反馈的跨城市场景下，域内验证不可靠。由于该运行使用了更高的置信度阈值（0.05 vs 0.01），其结果仅作为描述性存档而非受控结论。灰色世界归一化无实质影响，矩形运行经审计发现使用了非预期的竖屏方向。所有结论附有原版命令、配置快照和明确证据边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在仅能获得隐藏的源城市+目标城市混合图像聚合AP反馈（无法访问数据或进行正常验证）的极端受限条件下，如何有效配置检测器以实现跨城市泛化，并评估域内验证指标在此场景下的可靠性。

Method: 使用单个RF-DETR-Large检测器，在气隙Training-as-a-Service平台中逐一提交配置，平台仅返回聚合COCO-style AP。评估冻结推理分辨率提升（704训练/1120推理）、热启动1120px微调、灰色世界颜色归一化及图像方向等配置因素，每次配置仅提交一次，依据隐藏服务器返回的聚合AP进行筛选。

Result: 冻结1120×1120推理在无参数更新的情况下达到最高聚合AP 0.3654（较0.3272提升+0.0382），小目标相对增益最大、中目标绝对增益最大，输入像素量为2.53倍。热启动1120px微调达到0.3470，但其域内验证AP上升（0.767→0.789）与聚合AP表现不一致，证明域内验证是可靠的模型选择信号。灰色世界归一化无实质影响；矩形运行因竖屏方向问题无效。

Conclusion: 在聚合反馈受限条件下，纯推理端的输入分辨率提升是一种无需训练即可改善跨城市检测性能的有效手段，且优于微调方案。域内验证AP在聚合反馈场景下不可靠。所有结果均为探索性审计发现，基于各自单次提交，不能确立对特定目标城市的改进。

Abstract: Cross-city object detection requires a detector trained in one city to generalize to an unlabeled target city. In AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6, we analyze archived configurations of a single RF-DETR-Large detector inside an air-gapped Training-as-a-Service platform whose server returns only an aggregate COCO-style AP over a hidden mixture of source- and target-city images. Frozen 1120 x 1120 inference of a checkpoint trained at 704 x 704 achieved the highest aggregate AP among the evaluated configurations (0.3272 -> 0.3654, +0.0382) without any parameter update, with the largest relative gain on small objects and the largest absolute gain on medium objects, at 2.53x the input pixels. A warm-start 1120px fine-tuning recipe reached 0.3470 while its in-domain validation AP rose (0.767 -> 0.789), a caution that in-domain validation is an unreliable model-selection signal under aggregate-only cross-city feedback. Because that run's evaluation used a higher confidence threshold than the inference-only runs (0.05 vs. 0.01), we treat its score as a descriptive archived outcome rather than a controlled verdict on fine-tuning. Gray-world normalization did not meaningfully change the frozen-1120 result, and a rectangular run was found by audit to have used an unintended portrait orientation. We release verbatim platform commands, configuration snapshots, and an explicit evidence boundary for every claim. Each configuration was submitted once and the best was selected on the hidden server, so these are exploratory, audited findings about the aggregate mixture; they do not establish target-city-specific improvement.

</details>


### [42] [From Routes to Steps: Separating Semantic Progress from Local Execution in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03143)
*Xiangyun Huang,Xiangchen Wang,Runfeng Lin,Yihao Xu,Kangyu Huang,Jiang Hengchen,Xiwang Dong,Lin Jiarong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Route2Step 是一个将语义进度跟踪与动作生成解耦的框架，通过显式的步骤级接口（Instruction Analysis Module 预测进度状态，Action Generation Module 生成局部动作块）来解决 VLN 中进度跟踪错误与执行错误难以区分的问题，并利用 E-SPA 步骤对齐程序在无人工时序标注的情况下监督进度状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 VLM 的导航器通常仅通过下一动作预测来监督两种能力（进度跟踪和动作执行），导致当智能体偏离路线时，无法区分是选错了子指令还是未正确执行正确的子指令，从而可能持续在错误的进度状态下做决策。

Method: Route2Step 框架包含两个模块：Instruction Analysis Module 从全局指令和视觉历史预测语义进度状态；Action Generation Module 基于预测状态和最近观测生成局部动作块。E-SPA 步骤对齐程序将子指令与路线级演示的对应部分关联，实现对错误进度估计的状态监督，而直接动作监督仅保留给在正确活动子指令下反复失败的 rollout 组。

Result: 在 R2R-CE 数据集上，Route2Step 将 SR 从 48.1% 提升至 55.3%，SPL 从 43.3% 提升至 48.2%，使用 190K 个状态级修正样本，同时仅需 11.5K 个直接动作监督状态。在真实世界室内和室外环境中的实验也验证了其实际适用性。

Conclusion: 通过解耦语义进度跟踪与动作生成并引入步骤级接口，Route2Step 能够有效区分进度跟踪错误和执行错误，提升 VLN 智能体的导航性能，且在实际场景中具有实用性。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to follow a route-level instruction by executing its constituent steps from egocentric visual observations. Existing VLM-based navigators typically supervise both capabilities through next-action prediction alone, making progress-tracking errors difficult to distinguish from execution errors. When an agent deviates from the route, a corrective action label may recover the next movement but does not indicate whether the agent selected the wrong sub-instruction or failed to execute the correct one. Consequently, the agent may continue making decisions from an erroneous progress state. To resolve this ambiguity, we propose \textbf{Route2Step}, a framework that decouples semantic progress tracking from action generation through an explicit step-level interface. The Instruction Analysis Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IA}}$) predicts this state from the global instruction and visual history. Conditioned on the predicted state and recent observations, the Action Generation Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{AG}}$) generates local action chunks. To supervise the progress state without manual temporal labels, E-SPA, a step-alignment procedure, associates sub-instructions with their corresponding portions of route-level demonstrations. These alignments enable state supervision for incorrect progress estimates, while direct action supervision is reserved for rollout groups that repeatedly fail under the correct active sub-instruction. On R2R-CE, Route2Step improves SR from 48.1\% to 55.3\% and SPL from 43.3\% to 48.2\%, using 190K state-level corrective samples while requiring only 11.5K directly action-supervised states. Experiments in real-world indoor and outdoor environments further demonstrate the practical applicability of Route2Step. The project page is: https://sisyphus-hxy.github.io/Route2Step/.

</details>


### [43] [CROSS: Cascaded Distillation and Dual-Constraint Grounding for Remote Sensing Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03147)
*Tingzhang Luo,Ruizhong Liu,Yichao Liu,Cheng Fan,Yu Liu,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CROSS提出了一种紧密集成的RRSIS范式，通过语言引导级联蒸馏（LGCD）将SAM的像素级几何结构先验注入VLM中间层以解决架构弱耦合问题，并通过透视-空间对比学习（PSCL）挖掘掩模过滤的欺骗性干扰物和空间语言反事实作为硬负样本，以消除以对象为中心的语义偏见，从而在遥感图像指代分割任务上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRSIS方法虽然借助VLM和SAM的预训练能力取得了进展，但存在两个基本缺陷：一是架构弱耦合，单向信息流迫使模型依赖粗粒度的VLM提示，浪费了SAM的像素级结构指导，导致定位漂移；二是以对象为中心的语义偏见，模型过度关注主导对象的语义而忽视了RRSIS中至关重要的空间推理能力。

Method: CROSS包含两个核心模块：(1) 语言引导级联蒸馏（LGCD），将SAM的几何亲和力作为软正则化器蒸馏到VLM中间层，注入密集的结构先验以精化定位；(2) 透视-空间对比学习（PSCL），通过挖掘掩模过滤的欺骗性干扰物和空间-语言反事实作为硬负样本，施加跨锚定约束，以显式打破语义捷径并强制实现真正的逻辑一致性。

Result: 在RRSIS基准上的大量实验表明，CROSS取得了最先进的性能，并且在严重的空间描述扰动下仍能保持精确的定位能力。

Conclusion: CROSS作为一种鲁棒的新范式，有效解决了RRSIS中的架构弱耦合和对象中心语义偏见问题，为遥感图像指代分割提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation (RRSIS) has achieved significant progress through the integration of VLMs and the Segment Anything Model (SAM). However, this progress largely relies on strong pre-trained capabilities, while leaving two fundamental limitations insufficiently addressed: (1) Architectural Weak-Coupling, where the unidirectional flow forces reliance on coarse VLM prompts and wastes SAM's pixel-level structural guidance, causing localization drift; and (2) Object-Centric Semantic Bias, where models overemphasize dominant object semantics while remaining insensitive to spatial reasoning crucial for RRSIS. Motivated by these observations, we propose CROSS, a tightly integrated paradigm for RRSIS. First, we introduce Linguistic-Guided Cascaded Distillation (LGCD) to bridge the architectural gap, which distills SAM's geometric affinities as soft regularizers into VLM intermediate layers, injecting dense structural priors to refine localization. Second, Perspective-Spatial Contrastive Learning (PSCL) imposes cross-anchored constraints by mining mask-filtered deceptive distractors and spatial-linguistic counterfactuals as hard negatives, explicitly shattering semantic shortcuts to enforce genuine logical consistency. Extensive experiments on RRSIS benchmarks demonstrate that CROSS achieves state-of-the-art performance and maintains precise localization even under severe spatial description perturbations, standing as a robust new paradigm for RRSIS.

</details>


### [44] [Surface Keypoint Representation for Multi-Object and Articulated Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03158)
*Xiaogang Peng,Zeyu Han,Zichong Meng,Yiming Xie,Jihua Zhu,Gang Hua,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表面关键点轨迹和时空接触距离场的人-物交互（HOI）生成方法，支持多物体协调和多样关节机制，无需显式指定关节类型，并通过三阶段流水线（物体运动生成、接触距离场预测、全身运动合成）在多个基准数据集上取得优于或持平现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI生成方法大多假设交互对象为单一刚性物体，难以扩展到可变数量的物体或具有多种关节机构的铰接物体场景。日常活动中人类需要协调全身动作与周围物体的运动，因此需要一种能够同时处理多物体协调和任意关节机制的统一的物体运动表示。

Method: 1) 提出表面关键点轨迹作为物体运动表示：对每个刚性组件（独立物体或铰接部件）跟踪少量非共线表面点；2) 引入时空接触距离场，将基于距离的接触建模扩展到全身、多物体和铰接场景；3) 将HOI生成分解为三阶段：从文本或路点生成物体运动、预测接触距离场、通过接触引导优化合成全身运动。

Result: 在ParaHome、HIMO、ARCTIC和OMOMO四个基准数据集上进行了实验验证，在单物体、多物体和铰接交互场景下均取得优于或与现有方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 表面关键点轨迹作为一种通用的物体运动表示，能够统一处理多物体协调和多样关节机制；结合时空接触距离场，可以在不依赖显式关节类型标注的情况下实现高质量的全身HOI生成。

Abstract: Daily activities require humans to coordinate whole-body motion with the motion of surrounding objects. Despite recent progress in human-object interaction (HOI) generation, most existing methods assume interactions with a single rigid object and do not extend well to scenarios involving a variable number of objects or articulated objects with diverse joint mechanisms. We propose surface keypoint trajectories as an object motion representation: for each rigid component, whether a standalone object or one part of an articulated assembly, we track a small set of non-collinear surface points over time. This representation handles multi-object coordination and diverse articulation mechanisms directly from point dynamics without requiring explicit joint-type specification. To model when and where each body region contacts each object, we introduce a spatio-temporal contact distance field that extends distance-based contact modeling to whole-body, multi-object, and articulated settings. We factorize HOI generation into three stages: generating object motions from text or waypoints, predicting the contact distance field, and synthesizing whole-body motion with contact-guided optimization. Experiments on ParaHome, HIMO, ARCTIC, and OMOMO demonstrate better or comparable performance to existing methods across single-object, multi-object, and articulated interaction settings.

</details>


### [45] [Frequency-Decorrelated Temporal Ensembles for EEG--fNIRS Imagined-Handwriting Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03176)
*Xiao Fan,Hongbin Guo,Yubo Han,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FRED是一个面向跨被试想象手写轨迹四分类任务的多模态脑机接口系统，通过多尺度时间卷积网络在三个EEG频率视图上训练，结合跨频段集成、转导伪标签训练和基于块配额的匈牙利匹配推理，在公开/私有/总体测试集上分别取得0.8076/0.7242/0.7492的准确率，完整系统在私有划分上排名第四。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想象手写为神经解码提供了时间信息丰富的范式，但头皮EEG噪声大且内部生成的手写序列因人而异，导致跨被试（subject-independent）识别仍然困难，需要设计任务自适应的解码系统。

Method: FRED系统将想象手写建模为多秒运动序列，训练紧凑的多尺度时间网络处理三个互补的EEG频率视图。每个视图使用三个随机种子，跨频段集成相比同频段复制产生更低相关性的错误，形成九成员集成。提交管线进一步包含转导伪标签训练、三个EEG-Conformer成员、后验聚合和范式感知解码器。由于每个12试次随机化块包含每类三个实例，最终预测通过匈牙利分配在已知块配额约束下获得。

Result: 九成员集成的公开/私有/总体测试集准确率分别为0.8076/0.7242/0.7492。在固定后验池上，独立、会话约束和块约束解码分别为0.7600/0.7758/0.7952总体准确率。完整系统达到0.8498/0.7718/0.7952，私有划分排名第四。fNIRS单独解码仅为偶然水平（0.2511），向EEG添加fNIRS仅提升+0.0025准确率。

Conclusion: 频率多样化的时间EEG建模和协议匹配的结构化推理是稀疏导联EEG-fNIRS设置中性能的主要来源；fNIRS模态在此任务中贡献极小，几乎与随机水平无异。

Abstract: Imagined handwriting offers a temporally rich paradigm for non-invasive neural decoding, yet reliable recognition across unseen participants remains difficult because scalp EEG is noisy and internally generated stroke sequences vary across individuals. The Multimodal Brain-Computer Interface Grand Challenge provides synchronized EEG and fNIRS for four-class subject-independent handwriting-trajectory classification. We propose FRED, a task-adapted system that models imagined handwriting as a multi-second motor sequence and trains a compact multi-scale temporal network on three complementary EEG frequency views. With three seeds per view, cross-band members produce substantially less-correlated errors than same-band replicas, yielding a clean nine-member ensemble accuracy of 0.8076/0.7242/0.7492 on the public/private/overall test partitions without test-set adaptation or output constraints. The submitted pipeline further incorporates transductive pseudo-label training, three EEG-Conformer members, posterior aggregation, and a paradigm-aware decoder. Because every 12-trial randomization block contains three instances of each class, the final predictions are obtained by Hungarian assignment under the known block quota. On one fixed posterior pool, independent, session-constrained, and block-constrained decoding achieve 0.7600, 0.7758, and 0.7952 overall accuracy, respectively. The complete system reaches 0.8498/0.7718/0.7952, ranking fourth on the private split. A modality audit finds fNIRS-only decoding at chance (0.2511 overall), while adding fNIRS to EEG changes accuracy by only +0.0025. These results identify frequency-diverse temporal EEG modeling and protocol-matched structured inference as the principal sources of performance in this sparse-montage EEG--fNIRS setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/XiuFan719/EEG-fNIRS-fuse-method-for-MM-challenge.

</details>


### [46] [EditFlow3D: Automated Local Editing of 3D Assets with Trajectory Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03179)
*Rui Nie,Chuang Wang,Haitao Zhou,Jiahe Song,Buyu Li,Sheng Wang,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditFlow3D是一个免训练的局部3D编辑框架，通过VLM驱动的视觉引导和3D掩码生成，结合掩码引导差分流和逐步轨迹保持，实现对目标区域的精确编辑同时保留非目标区域的结构与外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D编辑方法缺乏简单而精确的3D掩码获取方式，难以在实现目标编辑的同时忠实保持非目标区域的结构和外观，需要一种既精准又能在局部编辑中保持整体一致性的方法。

Method: EditFlow3D采用免训练的流程：1) 利用VLM驱动的流程解释编辑意图并自动构建视觉引导图像和经过细化的3D编辑掩码；2) 在预训练3D生成模型的原始表示空间中进行局部编辑；3) 通过掩码引导的差分流（mask-guided differential flow）将编辑聚焦在目标区域；4) 使用逐步轨迹保持（step-wise trajectory preservation）在非目标区域与源资产之间保持一致性，而非直接替换中间特征。此外还引入了补充基准EditFlow-Bench。

Result: 在现有Edit3D-Bench和新提出的EditFlow-Bench两个基准上进行评估，定量结果、定性比较及用户研究表明EditFlow3D比现有3D编辑方法在目标区域编辑的精确性和非目标区域保真度上表现更好。

Conclusion: EditFlow3D通过免训练的VLM驱动框架和差分流引导策略，实现了更精确的局部3D编辑，同时较好保留了非目标区域，为3D局部编辑任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Controllable local editing of 3D assets requires precise target localization and appropriate visual guidance. However, existing methods lack a simple yet accurate way to obtain 3D masks and struggle to achieve the desired edit while faithfully preserving the structure and appearance of non-target regions. To address these challenges, we present EditFlow3D, a training-free framework for local 3D editing. Given a source asset and an edit instruction, a VLM-driven workflow interprets the editing intent and automatically constructs a visual guidance image and a refined 3D editing mask, enabling localized editing in the native representation space of a pretrained 3D generative model. Specifically, mask-guided differential flow focuses the edit on the target region, while step-wise trajectory preservation maintains consistency between non-target regions and the source asset without directly replacing intermediate features. Since the existing Edit3D-Bench covers only a limited range of local editing categories, we further introduce EditFlow-Bench as a complementary benchmark encompassing a broader variety of structural and appearance edits, and evaluate EditFlow3D on both benchmarks. Quantitative results, qualitative comparisons, and a user study demonstrate that EditFlow3D achieves more accurate target-region editing and better preserves non-target regions than existing 3D editing methods.

</details>


### [47] [CRIL-U-Net: Compact Ratio-Interaction Learning for Focal Cortical Dysplasia Segmentation from T1w and FLAIR MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03185)
*Soumen Ghosh,Amit Soni Arya,Tilottama Goswami,Subhojit Mandal,John Phamnguyen,Rajat Vashistha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRIL-U-Net，一种集成紧凑比率交互学习模块（CRIL）的3D U-Net，用于自动分割局灶性皮层发育不良（FCD）II型病灶。在85名FCD患者和25名健康对照上，使用五折交叉验证并与传统3D U-Net及注意力U-Net对比，CRIL-U-Net在Focal Tversky-Focal (FTF)损失下取得最高平均Dice分数（0.196），并在44/85例中产生非零病灶重叠，显著优于对比架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCD II型是药物难治性局灶性癫痫的重要结构性病因，但其病灶体积小、外观异质性强且MRI特征细微，导致自动分割极具挑战性。传统的多模态网络通常简单拼接T1w和FLAIR图像，依赖后续层隐式学习跨模态关系，缺乏显式且紧凑的跨模态交互机制。

Method: 提出CRIL-U-Net，在3D U-Net中嵌入紧凑比率交互学习模块（Compact Ratio-Interaction Learning module），该模块结合局部空间特征提取、体素级跨模态混合以及双向比率启发的交互机制。采用Dice-BCE和Focal Tversky-Focal (FTF)两种损失函数，在85例FCD患者和25例健康对照上执行五折交叉验证，并与传统3D U-Net和输入自注意力U-Net进行独立训练对比。

Result: 在FTF损失下，CRIL-U-Net获得最高平均Dice分数（0.196 ± 0.262），优于传统U-Net（0.136 ± 0.224）和注意力U-Net（0.135 ± 0.214）；在85例中44例产生非零病灶重叠，高于U-Net的36例；经假发现率校正后，CRIL-U-Net显著优于两个对比架构。

Conclusion: 紧凑的跨模态表示学习结合不平衡感知损失函数，能够在受控U-Net框架下改善FCD分割性能。然而48.2%的零重叠率表明该方法仍需进一步验证和方法学改进。

Abstract: Focal cortical dysplasia (FCD) type II is an important structural cause of drug-resistant focal epilepsy, but its small size, heterogeneous appearance, and subtle MRI characteristics make automated segmentation challenging. Conventional multimodal networks commonly concatenate T1-weighted (T1w) and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) images, requiring subsequent layers to learn useful cross-modal relationships implicitly. We propose CRIL-U-Net, a 3D U-Net incorporating a Compact Ratio-Interaction Learning module that combines local spatial features, voxel-wise cross-modal mixing, and bidirectional ratio-inspired interactions. CRIL-U-Net was compared with a conventional 3D U-Net and an input self-attention U-Net using five-fold cross-validation on 85 FCD subjects and 25 healthy controls. Each architecture was trained independently using Dice-binary cross-entropy (Dice-BCE) and Focal Tversky-Focal (FTF) losses. With FTF, CRIL-U-Net achieved the highest mean Dice score (0.196 +/- 0.262), compared with 0.136 +/- 0.224 for the U-Net and 0.135 +/- 0.214 for the attention comparator. It produced nonzero lesion overlap in 44 of 85 cases, compared with 36 for the U-Net. Under FTF, CRIL-U-Net significantly outperformed both comparison architectures after false-discovery-rate correction. These findings suggest that compact cross-modal representation learning can improve FCD segmentation within a controlled U-Net setting when combined with an imbalance-aware objective, although the remaining zero-overlap rate of 48.2% highlights the need for further validation and methodological development.

</details>


### [48] [Bridging Online and Offline Handwriting via Differentiable Physical Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03198)
*Seonmi Park,Seunghyun Shin,Vihaan Misra,Dongmin Shin,Ukcheol Shin,Jean Oh,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一在线-离线手写文本生成的物理画笔框架，通过可微分画笔渲染桥接笔迹轨迹与像素级外观，实现结构保真与视觉保真的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手写文本生成分为在线方法（捕捉结构时序但缺乏精细纹理）和离线方法（生成逼真图像但丢弃笔画顺序）两个独立范式，二者统一面临三大挑战：缺乏连接笔运动学与像素外观的显式物理模型、缺乏配对的轨迹-图像数据集、以及需要跨运动和外观域的可微分渲染过程来实现端到端学习。

Method: 提出一个紧凑的物理画笔模型（桥接笔迹动力学与视觉外观）和一个可微分渲染模块（将笔画轨迹转换为风格化图像），并集成四大核心模块：1) 文本到笔画生成器，2) 画笔参数观测器，3) 可微分画笔渲染器，4) 基于扩散模型的零样本图像精化器。

Result: 通过大量实验和真实机器人书法演示验证了方法的有效性，同时实现了结构保真（笔画顺序、时序动力学）和视觉保真（纹理、外观）的双重目标。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功弥合了在线与离线手写生成范式之间的鸿沟，为端到端的可微分手写生成提供了一条可行路径，并已在真实机器人书法场景中得到验证。

Abstract: Realistic handwritten text generation plays an important role in numerous applications, such as font design, biometric authentication, and robotic calligraphy. Existing methods are typically divided into two independent paradigms: online approaches that estimate handwriting trajectories and offline approaches that synthesize realistic handwriting images. While online models capture structural and temporal dynamics, they often lack fine-grained textures, whereas offline models reproduce realistic appearance but discard stroke order. However, unifying online and offline models remains challenging due to (1) the lack of an explicit physical model linking stroke kinematics to pixel-level appearance and (2) the absence of paired trajectory-image datasets. Moreover, enabling end-to-end learning requires a differentiable rendering process across motion and appearance domains. To address these challenges, we propose a compact physical brush model that bridges stroke dynamics and visual appearance, together with a differentiable rendering module that converts stroke trajectories into stylized images. By integrating these components, we propose a unified online-offline handwriting generation framework via differentiable brush rendering. The proposed framework consists of four core modules: 1) a text-to-stroke generator that predicts the target stroke conditioned on the given text and style image, 2) a brush parameter observer that extracts brush model parameters from style references, 3) a differentiable brush renderer that maps a stroke sequence and physical brush parameters into a handwritten image, and 4) a zero-shot image refiner that refines rendered images via diffusion models. Extensive experiments and real-world robotic calligraphy demonstrations validate our approach, achieving both structural and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [49] [DRIFT: Derailing Denoising Trajectories of Flow-Matching VLAs with Adversarial Patch Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03207)
*Hoseong Tae,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了流匹配视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型（如pi0）在对抗性攻击下的鲁棒性问题，发现其看似鲁棒的行为实际上是假象，源于先前攻击忽略了多步去噪ODE。作者提出DRIFT（通过输入扰动重定向去噪流匹配轨迹），这是一种测试时通用对抗补丁，放置在机器人夹爪上攻击去噪速度场。关键发现是：仅攻击第一步去噪比攻击更宽的步骤窗口更强且更经济，这源于输入空间优化中特有的梯度冲突。在pi0和pi0.5上跨四个LIBERO套件，DRIFT以单个小补丁打破几乎所有原本可解的任务，远超动作空间和嵌入空间攻击基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作声称流匹配VLA模型（如pi0）对对抗扰动具有鲁棒性，但本文作者认为这种鲁棒性大多是假象——之前的攻击方法没有考虑到多步去噪ODE，因此未能有效攻击这类模型。作者旨在揭示这一假象并开发真正有效的攻击方法。

Method: 提出DRIFT（Denoising Redirection via Input perturbation of the Flow-matching Trajectory），一种测试时通用对抗补丁攻击方法，直接攻击去噪速度场。研究发现攻击仅第一步去噪效果最好，并通过梯度冲突机制解释了这一反直觉现象，该机制与训练时后门攻击场景中的梯度行为完全相反。

Result: 在pi0和pi0.5模型上跨LIBERO的四个评估套件，DRIFT使用单个小补丁打破了几乎所有原本可解决的任务，性能远超动作空间和嵌入空间攻击基线。

Conclusion: 流匹配VLA模型的对抗鲁棒性是假象；通过正确攻击去噪轨迹的第一步即可轻松破解这类模型，这对流匹配生成模型在机器人策略中的安全性提出了严重警示。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action (VLA) models such as pi0 generate robot actions by integrating a learned denoising velocity field, and have been reported to resist adversarial perturbations that readily fool autoregressive VLAs. We show that this robustness is largely illusory: it stems from prior attacks ignoring the multi-step denoising ODE. We introduce DRIFT (Denoising Redirection via Input perturbation of the Flow-matching Trajectory), a test-time universal adversarial patch placed on the robot's gripper that attacks the denoising velocity field of an off-the-shelf policy. Our central finding is counterintuitive: attacking only the first denoising step is both stronger and cheaper than attacking a wider window of steps, which we explain through a gradient conflict unique to input-space optimization and which is exactly opposite to the training-time backdoor regime. On pi0 and pi0.5 across four LIBERO suites, DRIFT breaks essentially all originally-solvable tasks with a small single patch, far exceeding action- and embedding-space attack baselines.

</details>


### [50] [CrossScope: A Role-Asymmetric World Model for Joint Dual-Scope Surgical Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03211)
*Wanhao Liu,Jinsong Lin,Rulin Zhou,Chi Kit Ng,Wenbin Pan,Zhiqing Tang,Dongyue Li,Liwei Luo,Yanshen Wu,Panshuo Li,Zhiyong Xiong,Huxin Gao,Tamas Haidegger,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出CrossScope，一种针对母子镜ERCP场景中角色不对称的双镜视觉世界模型，通过几何引导的残差交互实现跨视角选择性证据传递，在同步双镜基准上优于现有手术视频生成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉世界模型从单一观察流学习未来动态，难以建模具有多个独立移动观察者的协同系统。在母子镜ERCP中，两个柔性内镜提供互补但角色依赖的视图，且缺乏标定的立体关系，传统多视角融合假设对称信息交换，不适用于此场景。

Method: 提出CrossScope，一个双流手术世界模型：保留各视角专属专家网络，通过几何引导的残差交互实现目标特定的证据路由。学习两个互补的通信方向——Mother视图的几何运动线索引导Child视图未来动态；Child视图的位姿对齐外观仅在建立有效空间对应时才支持Mother视图预测，从而避免损害各视角的专属表征。

Result: 建立了包含同步幻影与真实世界ERCP片段的配对双镜基准，从视觉保真度、结构保持、目标定位和运动一致性四方面评估。实验表明CrossScope一致优于强手术视频生成基线。

Conclusion: 验证了角色感知的证据路由对多观察者视觉世界建模的重要性，CrossScope能有效利用双镜互补信息进行未来预测。

Abstract: Visual world models typically learn future dynamics from a single observation stream, limiting their ability to model cooperative systems with multiple independently moving observers. We investigate this challenge in Mother--Child endoscopic retrograde cholangiopancreatography (ERCP), where two flexible scopes provide complementary yet role-dependent views without a calibrated stereo relationship. Unlike conventional multi-view fusion that assumes symmetric information exchange, we formulate \textbf{role-asymmetric dual-scope future prediction}, where cross-view evidence is selectively transferred according to the prediction target and its underlying spatial requirements. We propose \textbf{CrossScope}, a dual-stream surgical world model that preserves view-specific experts while enabling target-specific evidence routing through geometry-guided residual interactions. CrossScope learns two complementary communication directions: geometric motion cues from the Mother view guide Child-view future dynamics, while pose-aligned Child appearance supports Mother-view prediction only when valid spatial correspondence is established. This design allows each scope to contribute task-relevant evidence without compromising its view-specific representation. To evaluate this problem, we establish a paired dual-scope benchmark comprising synchronized phantom and real-world ERCP episodes, with evaluations assessing visual fidelity, structural preservation, target localization, and motion consistency. Experiments demonstrate that CrossScope consistently outperforms strong surgical video generation baselines, validating the importance of role-aware evidence routing for multi-observer visual world modeling.

</details>


### [51] [SLAMFormer-$\infty$: Infinite SLAM Transformer for Unbounded Frontend and Backend Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03429)
*Zhijian Fang,Weicheng Zheng,Yijun Yuan,Weibang Wang,Zhuoguang Chen,Chang Sun,Junhao Huang,Kenan Li,Minghui Qin,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了无限SLAM Transformer（SLAMFormer-∞），这是首个无需显式距离界限即可支持长距离前端和后端处理的几何transformer。通过采用记忆条件而非首帧锚定框架，实现了灵活的坐标系和尺度定义，并能在超过17公里长的轨迹上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于几何transformer的SLAM系统通常依赖首帧锚定的公式化表述，这限制了其对长距离轨迹的建模能力和全局一致性，难以处理超出显式距离界限的场景。

Method: 采用记忆条件（memory conditions）替代首帧锚定方案来定义灵活的坐标系和输入帧尺度；前端保持高效的局部计算，后端则联合优化长距离轨迹和场景几何，实现全局一致的处理。

Result: 在大规模数据集上，SLAMFormer-∞在轨迹估计和场景重建任务中均达到了优越或极具竞争力的性能，并能成功处理超过17公里的超长轨迹序列。

Conclusion: SLAMFormer-∞证明了通过记忆条件驱动的几何transformer可以有效摆脱显式距离限制，实现兼具长距离前端高效性和后端全局一致性的SLAM系统，为大规模、超长距离场景下的导航与重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce the Infinite SLAM Transformer (SLAMFormer-$\infty$), the first geometric transformer capable of supporting both long-range frontend and backend processing without an explicit distance bound. Instead of relying on a first-frame-anchored formulation, SLAMFormer-$\infty$ employs memory conditions to define flexible coordinate systems and scales for input frames, enabling more expressive structural conditioning. Built upon this formulation, the frontend preserves efficient local computation, while the backend jointly optimizes long-range trajectories and scene geometry in a globally consistent manner. Experimental results demonstrate that SLAMFormer-$\infty$ achieves superior or highly competitive performance in both trajectory estimation and scene reconstruction across large-scale datasets. Notably, SLAMFormer-$\infty$ generalizes to extremely long trajectories, successfully operating on sequences exceeding $17\mathrm{km}$.

</details>


### [52] [iFAN: Inference-Aware Learning for Plain Mask Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03216)
*Fang Li,Yu He,Haoyang Tong,Lichen Ma,Jingling Fu,Wenxiao Fan,Tongxuan Liu,Luohang Liu,Ke Zhang,Junshi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iFAN是一个面向查询式掩码Transformer的通用训练框架，通过调整概率-掩码排序(APMR)和对齐跨层自蒸馏(CLSD)来解决训练与推理之间的失配问题，仅增加极小的参数与推理开销即可在COCO、ADE20K和Cityscapes上显著提升全景、实例和语义分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询式掩码Transformer在推理时通过最终层查询预测间的像素级竞争来聚合分割输出，但这一推理过程并未在训练中被显式优化。论文识别出两个关键失配：具有最高概率-掩码分数的查询不一定产生最准确的掩码，且最终层解码可能丢弃中间层更优的预测。

Method: iFAN包含两个核心组件：1) APMR（调整概率-掩码排序），将查询竞争与预测掩码质量对齐，抑制高置信但不准的竞争者；2) CLSD（跨层自蒸馏），将中间层更强的预测知识迁移到最终层。两者均为纯训练目标，推理时仍保持高效的最终层解码。

Result: 在COCO、ADE20K和Cityscapes上，iFAN在全景、实例和语义分割任务以及不同架构、骨干网络规模和输入分辨率下均取得一致提升，平均提升1.20 PQ、1.30 AP和0.63 mIoU，且额外参数、FLOPs和推理延迟可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 通过显式对齐训练与推理过程，iFAN成功弥补了查询式掩码Transformer中训练推理失配问题，成为无需修改推理流程即可提升分割性能的通用且高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Query-based mask transformers assemble segmentation outputs through pixel-wise competition among query predictions of the final layer, yet this inference process is not explicitly optimized during training. We identify two key mismatches: the query with the highest probability-mask score does not necessarily produce the most accurate mask, and final-layer decoding may discard superior predictions from intermediate layers. To address these issues, we propose Inference-Aware Learning (iFAN), a general training framework for plain mask transformers. iFAN introduces Adjusted Probability-Mask Ranking (APMR), which aligns query competition with predicted mask quality and suppresses high-confidence but inaccurate competitors. We further employ Cross-Layer Self-Distillation (CLSD) to transfer stronger intermediate predictions to the final layer. The ranking and distillation objectives are training-only, while inference retains efficient final-layer decoding. Experiments on COCO, ADE20K, and Cityscapes demonstrate consistent improvements across panoptic, instance, and semantic segmentation, as well as across different architectures, backbone scales, and input resolutions. Overall, iFAN improves performance by an average of 1.20 PQ, 1.30 AP, and 0.63 mIoU, with negligible additional parameters, FLOPs and inference latency.

</details>


### [53] [Self-Supervised Representation-Guided Generative Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03218)
*Mingzhuo Li,Guang Li,Linfeng Ye,Jiafeng Mao,Takahiro Ogawa,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRG框架，利用自监督表示空间的几何结构（原型对齐、类间区分、类内分配）引导扩散模型的去噪过程，用于生成数据集蒸馏，提升压缩合成集的判别性和跨编码器的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法大多针对随机初始化网络，而现代视觉系统常基于冻结的预训练编码器。蒸馏样本应保留预训练表示空间的判别几何结构，但现有生成式目标函数未显式考虑这一点，因此缺乏表示空间感知的蒸馏生成方法。

Method: SRG框架分两步：(1) 从真实图像的SSL表示中构建类级原型；(2) 通过三个SSL空间目标（原型对齐、类间判别、类内分配）进行扩散引导。采样时采用阶段式引导策略：早期去噪锚定到SSL表示最接近指定原型的真实图像潜码，后期用SSL空间目标引导，兼顾视觉真实性与表示空间的代表性/判别性。

Result: SRG在多个数据集和不同每类图像数（IPC）设置下始终优于所评估的生成式基线；跨编码器评估表明其可在不同预训练表示空间之间迁移。

Conclusion: 表示引导的生成式方法对基于预训练SSL模型的数据集蒸馏是有效的，验证了将SSL几何结构融入扩散引导的实用价值。

Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses a large training set into a compact synthetic set while retaining its downstream utility. Most existing methods target randomly initialized networks, whereas modern vision systems often adapt frozen pretrained encoders with lightweight modules. Distilled samples should therefore preserve the discriminative geometry of the pretrained representation space, which existing generative objectives do not explicitly consider. We propose self-supervised representation-guided generative dataset distillation (SRG), a framework that translates the SSL geometry into diffusion guidance. Specifically, SRG constructs class-wise prototypes from real-image SSL representations and performs guidance through three SSL-space objectives for prototype alignment, inter-class discrimination, and intra-class assignment. During diffusion sampling, it adopts a stage-wise guidance strategy: early denoising is anchored to the latent of the real image whose SSL representation is nearest to the assigned prototype, whereas later denoising is guided by the SSL-space objectives. This division preserves the visual realism provided by the generative prior while progressively steering samples toward representative and class-discriminative regions of the SSL representation space. SRG consistently outperforms the evaluated generative baselines across multiple datasets and IPC settings. A cross-encoder evaluation further indicates transfer across pretrained representation spaces. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of representation-guided generation for dataset distillation with pretrained SSL models.

</details>


### [54] [Open-Linguistic Concept Unified Learning for Cross-Site Interpretable Dermatology Image Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03225)
*Chengyu Wu,Junpeng Tan,Wanxiang Luo,Yaqi Wang,Yandong Wen,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCon是一个开放语言驱动的统一概念学习框架，用于多模态可解释视觉-语言诊断，通过共享概念原型码本、开放语言多面语义规范和可靠性门控瓶颈聚合机制，实现跨站点、跨模态的无缝概念协调与可迁移的临床干预能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念型模型虽提供了透明推理和临床可干预性，但其数据集特异的刚性适应方式限制了跨站点泛化；在皮肤镜图像与临床照片等异构模态间，概念分类体系在可用性、粒度和语义上存在差异，导致基础视觉-语言模型（FVLMs）适配需要昂贵的标签工程和重复后训练，现有干预机制僵化绑定预定义概念，无法支持可扩展的皮肤科计算机辅助诊断部署。

Method: UniCon包含三项核心贡献：(1) 统一概念原型码本构建共享语义表示空间，无缝协调跨模态的异构概念系统，无需数据集特定重训练；(2) 基于开放语言的多面语义规范，缓解稀疏文本标签限制，提升不确定临床情境中的边界敏感性；(3) 可靠性门控瓶颈聚合驱动的跨站点可调干预接口，实现一致推理与可迁移的临床医师修正。

Result: 大量实验表明，UniCon在保持顶尖诊断精度的同时，成功弥合了不同临床分类体系之间的鸿沟，实现了前所未有的跨站点干预能力。

Conclusion: UniCon通过开放语言统一概念学习框架有效解决了概念型模型在多模态、跨站点皮肤科诊断中的适配瓶颈，在诊断精度与可解释性、可干预性之间取得了卓越平衡，为可扩展的临床CAD部署提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Human-interpretable computer-aided diagnosis is crucial for clinical decision making. Concept-based models excel by providing transparent reasoning and enabling post-hoc, clinician-in-the-loop interventions. However, their rigid dataset-specific adaptation inherently restricts cross-site generalization. Applying them across diverse modalities, such as dermoscopic and clinical photographs, is challenging due to heterogeneous concept taxonomies varying in availability, granularity, and semantics across cohorts. Consequently, adapting Foundation Vision-Language Models (FVLMs) demands costly label engineering and repeated post-training. Existing intervention mechanisms remain rigidly tied to predefined concepts, lacking adaptability and hindering scalable dermatology CAD deployment. To address these bottlenecks, we propose UniCon, an open-linguistic unified concept learning framework for multimodal interpretable vision-language diagnosis. UniCon resolves these challenges through three contributions: (1) A shared semantic representation space via a unified concept prototype codebook, seamlessly coordinating heterogeneous concept systems across modalities without dataset-specific retraining. (2) Open-linguistic based multi-faceted semantic specifications to overcome sparse textual label limitations, improving boundary sensitivity in uncertain clinical contexts. (3) A robust, cross-site adjustable intervention interface powered by reliability-gated bottleneck aggregation, enabling consistent reasoning and transferable clinician corrections. Extensive experiments demonstrate that beyond securing top-tier diagnostic accuracy, UniCon successfully bridges disparate clinical taxonomies, unlocking unprecedented cross-site intervention capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/wuchengyu123/UniCon.

</details>


### [55] [CIGTSurv: Clinical Information Guided Tri-modal Survival Prediction with Local Prototype Association and Global Feature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03247)
*Jing Dai,Qibin Zhang,Weiwei Zhou,Mingde Xu,Jingsong Liu,Jingdong Zhang,Hongming Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CIGTSurv是一个临床信息引导的三模态生存预测框架，通过将临床表格数据转换为高维token嵌入，并引入基于交叉注意力的局部原型关联模块和基于MMD的全局特征对齐损失，实现病理图像、基因组和临床数据的跨模态融合，在五个TCGA癌种数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生存预测中，病理图像和基因组数据被广泛利用，但临床信息因其离散、稀疏、低维的特性而未被充分利用；同时，模态间固有的异质性使得跨模态交互建模面临显著挑战。

Method: 1) 设计整体文本模板，利用预训练基础模型将临床表格数据转化为高维token化嵌入；2) 以临床信息为锚点，提出双层级交互机制——基于交叉注意力的局部原型关联(LPA)模块显式学习跨模态token级对应关系，以及基于最大均值差异(MMD)的全局特征对齐(GFA)损失隐式增强跨模态分布一致性。

Result: 在五个TCGA癌症队列上的大量实验表明，CIGTSurv取得了最先进的生存预测性能，代码已公开。

Conclusion: 通过充分利用临床信息并设计双层级交互机制有效缓解模态异质性问题，验证了临床信息在三模态生存预测中的关键锚定作用。

Abstract: Multimodal learning has significantly advanced survival prediction by integrating pathology images with genomic data. However, clinical information, despite its critical role in reflecting a patient' s overall health, remains underutilized due to its discrete, sparse, and low-dimensional nature. Furthermore, the inherent heterogeneity across these modalities pose significant challenges in modeling cross-modal interactions. In this paper, we propose CIGTSurv, a Clinical Information Guided Tri-modal framework for Survival prediction. Specifically, we first design a holistic text template and use pretrained foundation models to transform clinical tabular data into high-dimensional tokenized embeddings. Using clinical information as an anchor, we then introduce a dual-level interaction mechanism: 1) a local prototype association (LPA) module based on cross-attention to explicitly learn token-level correspondences between different modalities, and 2) a global feature alignment (GFA) loss based on Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) to implicitly enhance cross-modal distribution consistency. Extensive experiments on five TCGA cancer cohorts demonstrate that CIGTSurv achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) survival prediction performance. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Daijing-ai/CIGT-Surv.git.

</details>


### [56] [Clarity Contrast and Similarity Selection for Multi-Focus Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03252)
*Yicheng Zhang,Haoyou Deng,Zhiqiang Li,Wenti Yin,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的清晰度对比与相似度选择网络（CSNet），用于多聚焦图像融合（MFIF）。通过清晰度对比注意力模块（CCAM）增强源图像间的直接信息交互，利用相似度选择策略（SSS）处理散焦扩展效应（DSE）导致的边界模糊区域，最终生成全聚焦图像，在定量和定性上均达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的多聚焦图像融合方法大多缺少源图像之间的显式交互，限制了融合性能与可解释性。此外，散焦扩展效应（DSE）会在聚焦/离焦边界处使所有源图像中的像素退化，现有方法难以有效处理这一模糊区域。

Method: 提出CSNet，包含两个核心模块：(1) 清晰度对比注意力模块（CCAM）：对比源图像间的清晰度差异，相互增强清晰特征并抑制模糊特征，从而精确识别各源的聚焦区域并定位聚焦-离焦边界；(2) 相似度选择策略（SSS）：从源图像重建初始清晰图像，通过比较像素间的相似性选择最优像素，以细化边界附近受DSE影响的模糊区域。

Result: 大量实验表明，所提出的方法在定量和定性指标上均取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: CSNet通过显式的源图像交互机制，有效保留了聚焦区域并恢复自然的边界过渡，最终生成高质量的全聚焦融合图像。代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-focus image fusion (MFIF) aims to generate an all-in-focus image from multiple images of the same scene focused at different regions. Most existing deep learning-based methods lack explicit interaction between the source images, which limits their performance and interpretability. This paper presents a novel Clarity Contrast and Similarity Selection Network (CSNet), to bridge direct information exchange for MFIF. Specifically, by contrasting the clarity differences between source images within our proposed Clarity Contrast Attention Module (CCAM), we mutually enhance sharp features while suppressing blurry ones. This allows us to identify the exactly focused regions in each source and locate the focused-defocused boundaries. Moreover, the Defocus Spread Effect (DSE) degrades pixels in all source images around the boundaries. To further refine these ambiguous areas, we introduce a Similarity Selection Strategy, which reconstructs an initial clear image from source images and selects optimal pixels by comparing the similarity among them. Through this interactive approach, CSNet effectively preserves focused regions as well as recovering natural boundaries to fuse an all-in-focus output. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance both quantitatively and qualitatively. Our code is available on Github: https://github.com/ZYC-HUST/CSNet.

</details>


### [57] [NanoMorph-3D: An End-to-End Physics-Driven Unrolling Framework for Nanomaterial Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03257)
*Beiyuan Zhang,Hesong Li,Ziqi Wu,Ruiwen Shao,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NanoMorph-3D是一个统一的端到端框架，基于纳米形态分类学、大规模仿真数据集和物理驱动的展开网络，结合分层注意力机制与双域策略（正弦注意力+无监督双流机制），以克服电子断层扫描中的缺失楔问题、噪声干扰和仿真到现实差距，实现高保真、高速的三维纳米材料重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准电子断层扫描受缺失楔问题限制，传统算法产生严重几何畸变且难以应对噪声干扰。现有学习方法要么依赖物理盲目的后处理，要么采用受限于局部感受野的端到端架构，无法捕获复杂的三维拓扑结构。

Method: 提出NanoMorph-3D：1) 基于全面的纳米形态分类学构建大规模仿真数据集，显式建模非线性电子衰减；2) 设计物理驱动的展开网络，将近端梯度下降映射为可学习架构；3) 提出带物理归一化的分层注意力机制，用于长程三维依赖和尺度不变性；4) 采用双域策略，通过正弦注意力显式建模物理投影轨迹，强制执行正弦图一致性以缓解缺失楔伪影；5) 引入无监督双流机制弥合仿真到现实的差距。

Result: 实验表明NanoMorph-3D能够以优越的保真度和速度重建多种拓扑结构的纳米材料。

Conclusion: NanoMorph-3D通过物理机制与深度学习的深度融合，有效解决了电子断层扫描中缺失楔问题带来的几何畸变，为三维纳米材料的高精度、高效率表征提供了统一且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Precise 3D characterization of nanomaterials is essential for unlocking structure-property relationships. However, standard electron tomography is fundamentally limited by the missing wedge problem. Consequently, conventional algorithms suffer from severe geometric distortions, a challenge further complicated by pervasive noise interference. Current learning-based methods either rely on physics-blind post-processing or employ end-to-end architectures constrained by local receptive fields, failing to capture complex 3D topologies. We propose NanoMorph-3D, a unified end-to-end framework grounded in a comprehensive Nanomorphological Taxonomy. Powered by a large-scale synthetic dataset explicitly modeling non-linear electron attenuation, we design a Physics-Driven Unrolled Network mapping proximal gradient descent into a learnable architecture. To capture complex internal topologies, we formulate a hierarchical attention mechanism with Physics-Normalization for long-range 3D dependencies and scale invariance. Crucially, our Dual-Domain strategy leverages Sinusoidal Attention to explicitly model physical projection trajectories, enforcing strict sinogram consistency to mitigate missing wedge artifacts. Finally, an unsupervised dual-stream mechanism bridges the simulation-to-reality gap. Experiments demonstrate NanoMorph-3D reconstructs diverse topologies with superior fidelity and speed.

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Video Dataset Distillation via Cluster-Guided Prototype Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03269)
*Chongle Ren,Guang Li,Wenbo Huang,Naoki Saito,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ProtoBlend框架，通过选择-分配-混合三步流程，无需基于梯度的迭代优化即可构建高质量的视频蒸馏数据集，在四个动作识别基准上取得了具有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频数据集蒸馏方法依赖于迭代优化来合成紧凑视频，其计算成本随时间维度放大。作者希望探索一种无需对存储视频进行梯度优化的构建式蒸馏方法，以规避昂贵的优化开销。

Method: ProtoBlend采用三阶段框架：(1) 教师引导的时间片段选择，从每个源视频中保留高置信度片段；(2) 聚类引导的原型分配，在教师特征空间中对所选片段进行划分，为每个类内簇分配一个蒸馏槽位；(3) 原型混合，将每个原型与簇内锚点混合，并使用相同系数组合它们的教师预测以提供混合源监督。

Result: 在四个trimmed动作识别基准上，ProtoBlend在无需对蒸馏视频进行迭代优化的情况下取得了具有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过精心设计的构建式策略（而非迭代优化），就能高效生成有效的视频蒸馏数据集，为视频数据集蒸馏提供了一条更高效的路径。

Abstract: Video dataset distillation aims to compress a large video dataset into a compact surrogate set that preserves its training utility. Most existing approaches synthesize condensed videos through iterative optimization, whose cost is amplified by the temporal dimension. Rather than further reducing the number of optimized variables, we investigate whether effective distilled videos can be constructed without gradient-based optimization of the stored videos. Such a construction-based approach must address three challenges: selecting informative temporal segments, covering diverse intra-class variations under a limited videos-per-class budget, and increasing the information carried by each stored sample. To this end, we propose ProtoBlend, an efficient select-allocate-blend framework. First, teacher-guided temporal clip selection retains a high-confidence segment from each source video. Second, cluster-guided prototype allocation partitions the selected clips in the teacher feature space and assigns one distilled slot to each intra-class cluster. Third, each prototype is blended with an in-cluster anchor, while their teacher predictions are combined using the same coefficient to provide mixture-source supervision. Experiments on four trimmed action-recognition benchmarks demonstrate that ProtoBlend achieves a competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-off without iterative optimization of the distilled videos.

</details>


### [59] [GUI-Lens: Coarse-to-Fine Cropping for GUI Grounding with General-Purpose VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03270)
*Zichuan Fu,Shirong Wang,Wenlin Zhang,Guojing Li,Yimin Deng,Jingtong Gao,Junjia Qi,Hanyu Yan,Yefeng Zheng,Xiaopeng Li,Wanyu Wang,Xian Wu,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUI-Lens提出了一种从粗到细的GUI接地框架，通过主动视觉观察和逐步放大裁剪视图，让通用视觉语言模型能够精确定位UI元素。在多个基准测试上，该方法相比现有方法准确率最高提升24.9个百分点，并在GPT-5.5后端上达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI接地（将自然语言指令映射到点击位置）对可靠的GUI代理至关重要。现有方法虽然提供各种形式的定位辅助，但仍依赖直接点击预测，导致视觉歧义或不准确的初始估计会传播到最终结果。在高分辨率、密集界面上，视觉语言模型可能识别到目标控件却无法精确定位到可交互的坐标。

Method: GUI-Lens采用粗到细的接地框架：(1) 从截图提取OCR文本和检测到的UI组件，将其位置作为坐标参考；(2) 基于指令、当前视图和这些参考，让VLM选择下一视图的区域和缩放级别；(3) 裁剪并放大所选区域以提供更精细的视觉细节；(4) 在连续聚焦视图上重复此过程直至确定目标；(5) 整个过程持续与指令核对所提出的裁剪和点击，最终将局部位置映射回原始屏幕坐标。

Result: 在四个GUI接地基准测试和三个通用VLM后端上，GUI-Lens将整体接地准确率提升了最高24.9个百分点，并在使用GPT-5.5作为后端时达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过主动视觉观察和逐步聚焦的方式，替代直接点击预测，可以有效缓解高分辨率密集界面上的GUI接地定位误差累积问题，显著提升通用VLM在GUI接地任务上的表现。

Abstract: GUI grounding maps natural-language instructions to click locations and is essential for reliable GUI agents. The task remains difficult on high-resolution, densely populated interfaces because a vision-language model (VLM) may recognize a requested control without locating it precisely enough for interaction. Most existing methods provide various forms of localization assistance, but still rely on a direct click prediction, allowing visual ambiguity or an inaccurate initial estimate to propagate to the final result. In this paper, we introduce GUI-Lens, a coarse-to-fine grounding framework that allows a general-purpose VLM to determine the target through active visual observations. Specifically, GUI-Lens extracts OCR text and detected UI components from the screenshot and presents their positions as coordinate references. Using the instruction, the current view, and these references, the VLM selects the region and scale of the next view, which is cropped and enlarged to provide finer visual details. This process continues over successively focused views until the target is determined. Proposed crops and clicks are checked against the instruction throughout the process, and the final local position is mapped back to the original screen coordinates. Experiments on four GUI grounding benchmarks and three general-purpose VLM backends show that GUI-Lens improves overall grounding accuracy by up to 24.9 percentage points and achieves state-of-the-art performance with GPT-5.5.

</details>


### [60] [3DGSI-Assessor: A Large-Scale Dataset and An LMM-based Method for 3D Gaussian Splatting Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03279)
*Yuke Xing,Jiarui Wang,William Gordon,Zhu Li,Guangtao Zhai,Yiling Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3DGS-IEval-15K+，一个大规模、多维度的压缩3D高斯泼溅质量评估数据集，包含15,200张图像、45,600个跨整体/几何/颜色三维度的平均意见分数；并基于此提出3DGSI-Assessor，一种集成全局语义和维度特定局部特征的大多模态模型，可单次前向预测三个质量维度且达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS已成为实时新视角合成的代表性表示，但其存储占用使得压缩必不可少。3DGS训练与压缩会引入浮点伪影和表面散射等表示特有的失真，传统IQA指标无法捕捉；同时几何和颜色属性的独立压缩会导致解耦的维度特定失真需单独诊断，而现有指标仅报告单一总体分数。

Method: 1) 构建3DGS-IEval-15K+数据集：涵盖10个多样场景、6种代表性3DGS算法、系统设计的压缩级别、20个战略选择视角（含训练视角和挑战性的新视角），共15,200张图像，并标注45,600个覆盖整体、几何、颜色三维度的MOS。2) 提出3DGSI-Assessor框架：在大语言-多模态模型（LMM）中集成全局语义特征和维度特定的局部特征，单次前向传播同时预测三个质量维度。

Result: 3DGSI-Assessor在3DGS-IEval-15K+数据集上达到最先进（SOTA）性能，并在其他新视角合成基准上展现出具有竞争力的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文填补了压缩3DGS质量评估在表示特有失真和多维度诊断方面的空白，通过大规模数据集的构建和LMM驱动的评估框架，实现了对3DGS整体、几何和颜色质量的高效统一评估，并开源数据集和代码以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a dominant representation for real-time novel view synthesis (NVS), yet its storage footprint makes compression indispensable for practical deployment. 3DGS training and compression introduce representation-specific distortions such as floating artifacts and surface scattering, which conventional image quality assessment (IQA) metrics fail to capture. Moreover, the independent compression of geometric and color attributes may lead to decoupled dimension-specific distortions that must be diagnosed separately, yet existing metrics report only a single overall score. To address these gaps, we present 3DGS-IEval-15K+, a large-scale, multi-dimensional IQA dataset for compressed 3DGS, comprising 15,200 images from 10 diverse scenes, produced by 6 representative 3DGS algorithms at systematically designed compression levels and rendered from 20 strategically selected viewpoints spanning both training views and challenging novel views, annotated with 45,600 mean opinion scores (MOSs) across overall, geometry, and color quality. Based on 3DGS-IEval-15K+, we propose 3DGSI-Assessor, an all-in-one 3DGS IQA framework that integrates global semantic and dimension-specific local features within a large multimodal model (LMM), predicting all three dimensions in a single forward pass. 3DGSI-Assessor achieves state-of-the-art performance on 3DGS-IEval-15K+, and exhibits competitive generalization on other NVS benchmarks. Dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/YukeXing/3DGSI-Assessor.

</details>


### [61] [Test-Time Scaling for Safe Text-Guided Image Generation via Intermediate Clean Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03284)
*Jinya Sakurai,Shueicheng Yan,Xun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对文本到图像扩散模型的推理时安全防御方法，利用生成过程中的中间干净图像和稀疏边际目标检测违规内容，并通过在文本条件空间中优化结构化低秩残差进行即时干预，在不改变模型权重的前提下实现高效、可扩展的安全生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型面临安全合规挑战，良性或对抗性提示可触发违规内容（如裸露、知识产权侵权）。训练式消除方法计算开销大且易灾难性干扰通用能力；现有测试时防御主要依赖修改文本提示，忽略视觉检测信号。

Method: 利用扩散生成过程中估计的中间干净图像，采用稀疏边际目标检测被禁止概念；检测到违规时，通过截断反向传播在文本条件空间中优化结构化低秩残差进行即时干预。该方法保持权重不变，设计允许检测和干预在推理时进行。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4和v3.5上针对裸露移除、知识产权保护和风格擦除的实验表明，该方法在抑制、保真度和保留性方面均优于现有权重保持基线；非违规推理延迟几乎不随最大预算增加而变化，并支持通过测试时缩放灵活调整安全性能。

Conclusion: 提供了一种可扩展、灵活的安全生成部署方案，兼顾了安全性、生成质量和通用能力保持之间的平衡。

Abstract: Ensuring safety and policy compliance in text-to-image diffusion models remains a critical challenge, as benign or adversarial prompts can often elicit prohibited content, e.g. nudity and protected intellectual property. While training-based unlearning methods are effective, they are computationally expensive and prone to catastrophic interference with general capabilities. Conversely, existing test-time defenses are primarily prompt-centric, relying on modifying textual descriptions only, and overlook the visual signals for detection. In this paper, we propose to leverage the intermediate clean image estimated during the generation process and employ a sparse margin objective to detect prohibited concepts. When a violation is detected, we immediately intervene by optimizing a structured low-rank residual in the text-conditioning space via truncated backpropagation. This design allows weight-preserving detection, keeps non-violating inference latency nearly unchanged as the maximum budget increases, and offers flexibility in safety performance via test-time scaling. Extensive experiments on Stable Diffusion v1.4 and v3.5 across nudity removal, IP protection, and style erasure demonstrate superior performance across suppression, fidelity and preservation compared to prior weight-preserving baselines, providing a scalable and flexible solution for safe generative deployment.

</details>


### [62] [Recurrent Contrastive Learning for Imbalanced Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03304)
*Zhiyuan Zhu,Xinling Meng,Junxuan Yu,Jiongquan Chen,Qiongying Ni,Tuhang Shao,Yuhao Huang,Luping Zhou,Ruiyang Huang,Yuxue Wang,Rongliang Zhang,Xue Wang,Tianhong Tang,Likun Wang,Junbo Chen,Yong Jiang,Yongping Lu,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出递归对比学习（RCL）用于不平衡医学图像分类。RCL通过递归复用历史特征状态（基于DINOv3+LoRA骨干和时序记忆队列TMQ），逐步扩展尾部类别的支持区域，抑制头部类别侵占，改善类间分离。在三个不平衡医学数据集上取得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像分类常因疾病发病率差异而面临类别不平衡问题。现有方法（如类重采样和损失重加权）主要在学习已观测的特征分布内改善性能，但没有显式扩大尾部类别的潜在支持区域，导致尾部类别表征过于紧凑、容易被头部类别侵占，产生有偏的决策边界。

Method: RCL方法包含三个核心组件：(1) 采用DINOv3搭配LoRA适配器作为骨干网络，提供鲁棒的特征嵌入；(2) 设计时序记忆队列（TMQ），跨训练阶段保留语料级特征，为对比学习提供多样化全局参考；(3) 基于TMQ构建时序锚点（TARs），在尾部类别周围形成锚点区域，扩大尾部类别支持区域，抑制头部类别侵占并改善类间分离。通过跨阶段递归复用历史特征状态逐步实现上述目标。

Result: 在三个不平衡医学数据集上的大量实验表明，RCL相对于强基线方法取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: RCL通过递归复用历史特征状态来动态扩大尾部类别的表征支持区域，有效缓解了不平衡医学图像分类中头部类别对尾部类别的侵占问题，为处理医学领域固有的类别不平衡提供了一种新的对比学习策略。

Abstract: Medical image classification often suffers from class imbalance due to the inherent disparities in disease incidence. Existing approaches, such as class resampling and loss reweighting, mainly improve learning within the observed feature distribution, but do not explicitly enlarge the latent support region of tail classes. As a result, tail-class representations remain overly compact and are easily encroached upon by head classes, leading to biased decision boundaries. In this work, we propose Recurrent Contrastive Learning (RCL) for imbalanced medical image classification. RCL progressively expands the support region of tail classes by recurrently reusing historical feature states across training phases. Specifically, we adopt DINOv3 with LoRA adapters as the backbone to provide robust feature embeddings. We then devise a Temporal Memory Queue (TMQ) to preserve corpus-level features across training phases and provide diversified global references for contrastive learning. Based on TMQ, we construct Temporal Anchors (TARs) to form an anchor field around tail classes. This field enlarges the support region of tail classes, suppresses head-class encroachment, and improves inter-class separation. Extensive experiments on three imbalanced medical datasets demonstrate that RCL achieves consistent improvements over strong baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/dndins/RCL.

</details>


### [63] [LocAnyMed: Vision-Language Grounding for Multimodal Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03322)
*Zihan Wang,Tong Liu,Zhiwei Wang,Tao Huang,Wentao Jiang,Sihan Ma,Shanshan Ye,Xiaohui Yang,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了LocAnyMed-200K多模态医学视觉定位数据集（约20万条图像-查询-答案样本，覆盖CT、光学成像、超声和X射线），通过统一化异构医学定位资源为自由形式指令格式，对LocateAnything-3B模型进行全参数微调，将F1@IoU 0.50从10.64提升至85.59；同时构建LocAnyMed-CoT-20K推理增强子集提升跨源泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉定位模型主要在自然图像上训练，而现有医学定位资源分散于不同成像模态、数据集和任务形式之间，缺乏统一的医学视觉定位基准和训练资源，难以支撑可解释的医学人工智能。

Method: 1) 构建LocAnyMed-200K数据集，将异质的检测与定位资源（CT、光学医学成像、超声、X射线）统一为支持多边界框、点坐标和负查询无目标输出的自由形式指令格式；2) 对LocateAnything-3B基准模型进行全参数微调；3) 从原始数据导出LocAnyMed-CoT-20K推理增强子集，将解剖背景、视觉观察和空间结论通过结构化推理链连接。

Result: 在保留评估集上，全参数微调将F1@IoU 0.50从10.64显著提升至85.59；CoT子集微调进一步改善跨来源泛化性能。

Conclusion: 大规模领域特定监督可使通用定位模型具备有效的医学定位能力，同时推理增强训练能够提升临床可解释性；该数据集和代码为异构医学成像模态下的定位准确性与推理质量提供了统一研究基础。

Abstract: Medical visual grounding connects free-form clinical queries to spatial evidence in medical images and is an important component of interpretable medical artificial intelligence. However, general-purpose grounding models are predominantly trained on natural images, while existing medical localization resources remain fragmented across imaging modalities, datasets, and task formulations. To address this gap, we construct LocAnyMed-200K, a multimodal medical visual grounding dataset containing approximately 200K image-query-answer examples across computed tomography, optical medical imaging, ultrasound, and X-ray. We harmonize heterogeneous detection and localization resources into a unified free-form instruction format that supports one or multiple bounding boxes, point coordinates, and no-target outputs for negative queries. Full-parameter fine-tuning of LocateAnything-3B on LocAnyMed-200K improves F1@IoU 0.50 from 10.64 to 85.59 on a held-out evaluation split, demonstrating that large-scale domain-specific supervision can equip a general grounding model with effective medical localization capabilities. Beyond spatial coordinates, a clinically interpretable grounding system should also communicate the evidence supporting its prediction. We therefore derive LocAnyMed-CoT-20K, a rationale-augmented subset that connects anatomical context, visual observations, and spatial conclusions through structured reasoning and further improves cross-source generalization through fine-tuning. Together, these resources provide a unified foundation for studying both localization accuracy and rationale quality across heterogeneous medical imaging modalities. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/MiliLab/LocAnyMed.

</details>


### [64] [PolyLayout: Multi-room Manhattan Layout Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03323)
*Gustav Hanning,Shaohui Liu,Rémi Pautrat,Marc Pollefeys,Kalle Åström,Viktor Larsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PolyLayout是一种多房间室内布局估计方法，将房间布局参数化为曼哈顿3D多边形，并通过神经网络预测的优化目标在多个房间间联合优化。该方法将可学习的评分与显式的几何/相机模型分离，通过迭代的墙面分割与合并自适应细化多边形拓扑，并利用跨房间的结构线索。在引入的两个新多视点多房间基准上，PolyLayout在准确性和鲁棒性上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内布局估计方法存在两方面局限：一是对新数据集的泛化能力差，或对房间形状/相机配置有过强的几何假设；二是大多数方法独立估计各房间，未能利用建筑中共享的结构信息（如主方向、地面平面、天花板高度），导致估计结果缺乏全局一致性。

Method: PolyLayout将房间布局参数化为曼哈顿3D多边形，并跨多个房间联合优化。其核心创新在于：优化目标由神经网络基于预训练视觉特征预测，并仅以最终布局为监督端到端训练，而相机投影和多边形更新保持显式且基于模型，由此实现学习与几何的解耦以提升泛化性。优化过程中通过迭代的墙体分割与合并操作自适应细化多边形拓扑，同时利用跨房间的结构线索。此外，作者为现有数据集添加布局标注，构建了两个新的多视点多房间布局基准。

Result: 在两个新引入的多视点多房间布局基准上，PolyLayout在准确性和鲁棒性方面均优于之前的方法。

Conclusion: 通过将神经网络学习的评分函数与显式的模型化几何优化分离，PolyLayout在保持对新数据集和相机参数良好泛化能力的同时，实现了跨房间联合布局估计的精度提升，验证了该范式在多房间室内场景理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Estimating room layouts from multi-view imagery is a core task for indoor scene understanding. Existing methods are typically limited either by poor generalization to new datasets or restrictive geometric assumptions of the room shape or camera configuration. Most also estimate rooms independently, failing to exploit shared building structure such as dominant directions, ground plane or ceiling height.
  We propose PolyLayout, a multi-room layout estimation method that parameterizes room layouts as Manhattan 3D polygons and optimizes them jointly across multiple rooms. The optimization objective is predicted by a neural network on top of robust pre-trained visual features and trained end-to-end with supervision only on output room layouts. At the same time, camera projection and polygon updates remain explicit and model-based. This separation between learned scoring and geometry improves generalization to new datasets and camera parameters. During optimization, PolyLayout adaptively refines the polygon topology through iterative wall split and merge operations while jointly utilizing structural cues across rooms. We introduce two new multi-view multi-room layout benchmarks by providing layout annotations to existing datasets, and experiments show that PolyLayout outperforms prior approaches, both in terms of accuracy and robustness.
  Project page: https://ghanning.github.io/PolyLayout

</details>


### [65] [SPADE: An Input-Adaptive Sparse Attention Engine for Fast Video Diffusion Models Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03335)
*Shanghao Liu,Renze Chen,Size Zheng,Yuanqiang Liu,Yun,Liang,Hailong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SPADE是一个面向视频扩散Transformer（vDiTs）的免训练稀疏注意力引擎，通过vDiT-SSR规范、SICS运行时方案生成与低开销执行器三部分实现输入自适应稀疏化，在不损失生成质量的前提下将注意力速度提升2.26x-3.40x，端到端推理加速1.49x-1.80x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer生成高质量视频，但其二次复杂度自注意力在视频token规模下推理成本极高。关键在于实现输入自适应的稀疏性：以可忽略的开销选择关键Q/K/V token，并执行端到端加速。

Method: SPADE包含三个组件：(i) vDiT-SSR规范，定义3D分块候选并通过Summarizer/Estimator表达式形式化动态掩码；(ii) 基于SICS和头级策略的运行时方案生成；(iii) 执行器，包含低开销索引搜索、flash块稀疏注意力和内核分组。

Result: 在Hunyuan-Video和Wan 2.1/2.2的文本到视频及图像到视频任务上，SPADE在提升稀疏度和速度的同时保持生成质量，注意力加速2.26x-3.40x，端到端推理加速1.49x-1.80x。

Conclusion: SPADE证明了免训练稀疏注意力技术在视频扩散模型中的可行性，通过规范化的3D分块与动态掩码策略，实现了显著的推理加速且不损失质量，代码已开源。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (vDiTs) generate high quality but pay quadratic self-attention cost, making inference prohibitive at video-token scales. The challenge is input-adaptive sparsity: selecting critical Q/K/V tokens with negligible overhead and executing them for end-to-end gains. We present SPADE, a training-free sparse-attention engine of three parts: (i) vDiT-SSR, a specification defining 3D blocking candidates and formalizing dynamic masks via Summarizer/Estimator expressions; (ii) runtime scheme generation using SICS and a head-wise policy; and (iii) an executor with low-overhead index search, flash block-sparse attention, and kernel grouping. Across Hunyuan-Video and Wan 2.1/2.2 for text-to-video and image-to-video generation, SPADE raises sparsity and speed while preserving quality, accelerating attention by 2.26x-3.40x and end-to-end inference by 1.49x-1.80x. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/6somehow/DAC-SPADE.

</details>


### [66] [When Oracle Conditioning Misleads Deployment: Conditioning-Availability Bias in Echocardiographic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03342)
*Dang P. M. Cao,Hieu D. Pham,Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究条件分割模型在部署时辅助信号不匹配（辅助变量偏移/捷径学习）的问题，以相位条件超声心动图分割为例，提出互补差距对（deployable oracle-estimated gap 和 oracle-random gap）来评估模型在真实部署条件下的性能，并在CAMUS和EchoNet-Dynamic数据集上验证了检查点选择与相位扰动可提升部署鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件分割模型在训练和评估时可能使用比部署时更干净的辅助信号（如相位标注），导致模型在真实推理路径上性能下降，即协议层面的捷径学习与辅助变量偏移问题。现有评估方法未能量化这种训练/部署信号不匹配带来的风险。

Method: 提出互补差距对：1) deployable oracle-estimated gap衡量使用真实推理路径（估计相位）时的损失；2) oracle-random gap通过随机相位测试探测模型对错误相位的敏感性。在CAMUS和EchoNet-Dynamic数据集上进行实验，并采用部署感知的检查点选择与相位扰动进行缓解。

Result: 在CAMUS数据上，一个强循环、oracle选择的运行在使用估计相位时严重失效，且对错误相位的敏感性在三次运行中持续存在；在EchoNet-Dynamic上，当前估计器仍可用，但随机相位测试揭示强潜在敏感性。部署感知检查点选择和相位扰动在几乎不改变平均Dice的情况下缩小了两种差距。下游EF审计显示恢复分割不一定恢复EF误差或符号偏差。

Conclusion: 互补差距对测试了oracle条件性能能否在部署时实际可用的推理路径上存续。该框架揭示了条件分割模型在部署时的脆弱性，并表明分割性能的改善并不必然转化为下游临床指标（如EF）的改善。

Abstract: Conditional segmentation models may be trained and evaluated with auxiliary signals cleaner than those available at deployment. We study this protocol-level manifestation of shortcut learning and auxiliary-variable shift in phase-conditioned echocardiographic segmentation. The complementary gap pair measures loss on the deployable oracle-estimated pathway and probes sensitivity on the oracle-random pathway. On held-out CAMUS data, one strong-cyclic, oracle-selected run fails severely with estimated phase, while sensitivity to incorrect phase persists across three runs. On EchoNet-Dynamic, the current estimator remains usable, but random-phase testing reveals strong latent sensitivity. Deployment-aware checkpoint selection and phase perturbation reduce both gaps with little change in mean Dice. Exploratory subgroup analyses quantify variation across measured strata, and a downstream ejection fraction (EF) audit shows that recovering segmentation does not necessarily recover EF error or signed bias. Together, the gaps test whether oracle-conditioned performance survives the inference pathway actually available at deployment.

</details>


### [67] [Can Text-to-Image Models Draw from the Right Frame of Reference?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03357)
*Zheyuan Gu,Ruihang Li,Yong Huang,Yiqian Zhang,XIangzhao Hao,Jiaxin Niu,Jiahao Hu,Zhenyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FoR-T2I基准，专门评估文本到图像生成模型在不同参照系（相机视角vs物体内在方向）下遵循空间指令的能力。基于1,200对受控空间布局的提示词，对22个T2I模型评估发现，FoR提示的平均最终准确率比匹配的Cam提示低41.8%，即使最佳模型也仅达到44.3%的FoR准确率。文章还提出了一种基于VLM门控的重写方法，通过视觉反馈选择重写后的提示词，在相同生成预算下将平均FoR准确率从25.0%提升至29.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I基准虽揭示了许多布局失败案例，但很少隔离"模型能否在指定参照系与相机视角不一致时遵循该参照系"这一问题。方向性表达（如"左边"）可能指代观看者图像坐标或物体的内在方向，二者会产生不同的预期布局，因此需要一个专门基准来区分和评估这一能力。

Method: 构建FoR-T2I基准：包含1,200对受控空间布局的提示词对，每对包含相机视角(Cam)提示和参照系(FoR)提示描述相同目标位置。评估22个闭源和开源T2I模型，并分析不同关系类型和相机视角下的性能差距。进一步提出VLM门控重写策略，利用视觉反馈选择重写后的提示词。

Result: FoR提示的平均最终准确率比匹配的Cam提示低41.8%；最佳模型仅达到44.3%的FoR准确率。VLM门控重写方法将平均FoR准确率从25.0%提升到29.2%，在相同生成预算下实现了显著改进。

Conclusion: 当前T2I模型在通过物体朝向描述布局时远不如直接使用图像坐标描述，这表明模型在理解物体内在参照系方面存在明显不足。基于视觉反馈的提示重写策略可以部分缓解这一问题，但整体性能仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Spatial instruction following has become a crucial requirement for text-to-image (T2I) generation. A common challenge arises when directional expressions are interpreted under different frames of reference. For example, ``the left of'' may refer to the viewer's image coordinates or to the intrinsic orientation of an object, leading to different expected layouts. Existing T2I benchmarks reveal important layout failures, yet they rarely isolate whether models can follow a specified frame of reference when it differs from camera view. To mitigate this gap, we introduce FoR-T2I, a benchmark for evaluating this distinction with 1,200 prompt pairs built from controlled spatial layouts. In each pair, the camera-view (Cam) prompt states the target relation in camera view, while the frame-of-reference (FoR) prompt describes the same target placement through an oriented anchor object. Across 22 closed-source and open-source T2I models, mean final accuracy is 41.8\% lower on FoR prompts than on matched Cam prompts; even the best-performing model achieves only 44.3\% FoR accuracy. This suggests that current models struggle more when the same layout is described through an object's orientation rather than directly in image coordinates. We further analyze this gap by relation type and camera view, compare several training-free prompting and feedback-based mitigation strategies, and propose a VLM-gated rewriting approach that selects rewritten prompts using visual feedback, improving average FoR accuracy from 25.0\% to 29.2\% under the same generation budget.

</details>


### [68] [DRPFNet: Dual-domain Residual Progressive Fusion Network for RGB-Thermal Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03370)
*Zian Wang,Changchun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRPFNet，一个双域残差渐进融合网络，通过结构、特征、增强三个协同层面构建统一信息流优化系统，解决RGB-T目标检测中跨尺度知识继承缺失、缺乏双向优化和频率-空间协作不足的问题，在两个公开数据集上取得竞争性性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB-T目标检测方法通常在每个特征尺度独立进行跨模态融合，面临三大局限：1)各尺度独立融合导致跨层级知识继承缺失；2)缺乏双向优化导致噪声无法持续抑制；3)缺少频率-空间协作导致信息退化。

Method: 提出DRPFNet（Dual-domain Residual Progressive Fusion Network），从三个协同层面构建统一信息流优化系统：1)结构层面：通过自底向上的知识累积和双向增强实现跨尺度传播，确保信息流平滑；2)特征层面：通过频带分离和边缘引导，协作提取RGB高频边缘和热红外低频结构，保证表示质量；3)增强层面：通过边缘引导的双域细化增强前景-背景判别，实现精确目标定位。

Result: 在两个公开RGB-T数据集上进行了大量实验，方法取得了竞争性的性能与效率，验证了层级协作策略的有效性。

Conclusion: DRPFNet通过结构、特征、增强三个层面的层级协作策略，有效解决了现有RGB-T检测中跨尺度知识继承、双向优化和频率-空间协作方面的不足，验证了该分层协同框架的有效性。

Abstract: RGB-thermal (RGB-T) object detection aims to fuse complementary information from visible and thermal modalities to achieve robust detection under varying illumination and weather conditions. Current methods typically employ attention mechanisms or transformers to perform cross-modal fusion independently at each feature scale, directly combining RGB and thermal features in the spatial domain. However, they still face significant limitations: cross-level knowledge inheritance caused by independent fusion at each scale,suppressing noise continuously due to the lack of bidirectional optimization, and information degradation induced by the absence of frequency-spatial collaboration. To address these issues, we propose DRPFNet, a Dual-domain Residual Progressive Fusion Network that constructs a unified information flow optimization system from three synergistic levels:structure, feature, and enhancement. At the structural level, we establish cross-scale propagation through bottom-up knowledge accumulation and bidirectional enhancement,ensuring smooth information flow. At the feature level, we collaboratively extract RGB high-frequency edges and thermal low-frequency structures via frequency band separation and edge guidance, guaranteeing representation quality. At the enhancement level, we enhance foreground-background discrimination through edge-guided dual-domain refinement,achieving precise object localization.Extensive experiments on two public RGB-T datasets demonstrate that our method achieves competitive performance with competitive efficiency, validating the effectiveness of this hierarchical collaborative strategy.

</details>


### [69] [Residual Flow Matching with Dynamic Cross-Interaction for 3D Multi-Person Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03379)
*Wei Wei,Yinyuan Zhao,Ruixuan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于先验引导残差流匹配（Prior-Guided Residual Flow Matching）的3D多人运动预测框架，通过确定性粗略先验锚定运动结构、动态跨交互机制时序同步智能体间消息传递以及解耦关节运动架构，在多个数据集上达到最先进预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D多人运动预测需要同时建模个体运动学与人与人之间的交互。现有的Flow Matching方法虽然能通过多假设生成提高预测精度，但从纯噪声直接预测骨骼序列会导致结构一致性受损，且在噪声主导的早期积分步骤中产生不可靠的跨智能体交互。

Method: 1) 确定性粗略先验（DCP）：建立运动学锚点，将生成过程建模为运动残差上的条件流，简化生成目标并保持结构稳定性。2) 动态跨交互机制（DCI）：将跨智能体消息传递与积分进度进行时间同步，确保提取可靠的社会上下文。3) 解耦关节运动架构：采用双向融合以保持细粒度运动学一致性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法取得了最先进的预测精度。

Conclusion: 通过引入先验引导的残差流匹配框架，有效解决了直接生成骨骼序列时结构不一致和早期噪声阶段跨交互不可靠的问题，同时在多人运动预测精度上超越了现有方法。

Abstract: 3D multi-person motion prediction requires modeling both individual kinematics and inter-person interactions. While Flow Matching is effective for multi-hypothesis generation to improve prediction accuracy, directly predicting skeletal sequences from pure noise often compromises structural consistency and introduces unreliable cross-agent interactions during early noise-dominated integration steps. To address this, we propose a Prior-Guided Residual Flow Matching framework. First, a Deterministic Coarse Prior (DCP) establishes a kinematic anchor, formulating the generative process as a conditional flow over motion residuals to simplify the generative objective and preserve structural stability. Second, a Dynamic Cross-Interaction (DCI) mechanism temporally synchronizes inter-agent message-passing with the integration progress, ensuring the extraction of reliable social contexts and improving multi-person motion fidelity. Finally, a decoupled joint-motion architecture with bidirectional fusion effectively preserves fine-grained kinematic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art prediction accuracy across multiple datasets. Code is available at https://github.com/Wei-Wei-a/Residual-Flow-Matching-with-Dynamic-Cross-Interaction-for-3D-Multi-Person-Motion-Prediction.

</details>


### [70] [FreqAdapt: Frequency-Adaptive Processing for RAW Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03385)
*Hanxi Li,Huiling Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FreqAdapt，一种轻量级的频域自适应RAW数据增强模块，将ISP操作映射到傅里叶频域，通过域分离和自适应频域编码器实现RAW图像增强，在多种光照和天气条件下取得最优检测性能，且可无缝集成到现有检测框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测方法主要使用sRGB输入，而sRGB是经过为可视化设计的ISP从RAW传感器数据处理压缩而来。相比之下，RAW图像具有更好的噪声特性和更丰富的信息表示，在恶劣天气或低光照等挑战性条件下对物体检测至关重要。然而目前的增强方法多在空间域处理，缺乏在频域对RAW数据进行自适应增强的有效方案。

Method: FreqAdapt采用三方面创新：(1) 将ISP操作创新性地映射到傅里叶频域，基于ISP操作的物理特性进行域分离，确保每个操作在最适合的域中执行；(2) 设计自适应频域编码器，联合分析幅度谱、相位谱和RAW图像特征，为ISP参数预测提供全局上下文；(3) 使用可学习的融合机制实现自适应特征增强。

Result: 在多个涵盖不同光照和天气条件的数据集上的大量实验表明，FreqAdapt达到最先进性能，同时保持轻量级效率和良好的物理可解释性。该模块可无缝集成到现有目标检测框架中。

Conclusion: FreqAdapt为RAW域视觉感知任务提供了新颖的解决方案，通过频域自适应增强在保持轻量高效的同时显著提升了目标检测性能，尤其在挑战性环境条件下表现突出，并具备良好的物理可解释性和框架兼容性。

Abstract: Existing object detection methods predominantly utilize sRGB inputs, which are compressed from RAW sensor data using Image Signal Processors (ISP) originally designed for visualization purposes. Compared to RGB images, RAW images possess favorable noise characteristics and richer information representation, which are crucial for object detection, particularly under challenging conditions such as adverse weather or low-light environments. In this paper, we propose FreqAdapt, a lightweight module for adaptive RAW data enhancement in the frequency domain. Unlike traditional spatial domain processing methods, FreqAdapt innovatively maps ISP operations to the Fourier frequency domain and performs domain separation based on the physical properties of ISP operations, ensuring each operation is performed in its most suitable domain. Meanwhile, through an adaptive frequency domain encoder that jointly analyzes amplitude spectrum, phase spectrum, and RAW image features, we provide global context for ISP parameter prediction and employ a learnable fusion mechanism to achieve adaptive feature enhancement. Extensive experiments on multiple datasets with diverse lighting and weather conditions demonstrate that FreqAdapt achieves state-of-the-art performance while maintaining lightweight efficiency and good physical interpretability. Furthermore, our module can be seamlessly incorporated into existing object detection frameworks, providing a novel solution for visual perception tasks in the RAW domain.

</details>


### [71] [SRAP: SVD-Refined Adversarial Perturbations for Imperceptible Face-Swap Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03395)
*Sungwon Cho,Kwanghyun Ko,Myungjoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRAP，通过通道级截断SVD精细化和身份重要性掩码相结合的对抗扰动优化方法，在保持身份破坏性能的同时显著提升深伪防御中保护图像的视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深伪技术威胁面部隐私和身份安全。基于PGD的对抗扰动虽能破坏换脸模型的身份表示，但存在两个问题：扰动广泛分布在整个图像包括身份不敏感区域，以及包含视觉上突出的高频成分，导致图像质量下降。

Method: 通过身份敏感度估计和PGD扰动的奇异值分解(SVD)分析空间和频谱效率。提出SRAP，在每个优化步骤中结合：1) 每通道截断SVD精化——抑制高秩、高频残余；2) 身份重要性掩码——将扰动限制在强烈影响身份表示的位置。

Result: 在CelebA-HQ和VGGFace2-HQ数据集上的实验表明，SRAP在所有报告指标上大幅提升保护图像的保真度，同时保持具有竞争力的身份破坏性能，实现了换脸防御与视觉不可感知性之间的良好权衡。

Conclusion: SRAP通过空间（掩码）和频谱（SVD截断）双维度的扰动优化，有效解决了PGD对抗扰动视觉质量退化的问题，为深伪防御提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Deepfake technologies pose increasing threats to facial privacy and identity security, motivating proactive defenses that protect facial images before misuse. Although adversarial perturbations generated by projected gradient descent (PGD) can disrupt the identity representations used by face-swapping models, their visual quality is degraded by two characteristics: perturbations are distributed broadly over the image, including identity-insensitive regions, and they contain visually salient high-frequency components. We analyze these spatial and spectral inefficiencies through identity-sensitivity estimation and the singular-value decomposition (SVD) of PGD perturbations. Our analysis shows that later singular components contain a disproportionate amount of high-frequency energy, while the leading components preserve most of the perturbation energy and defense utility. Based on these observations, we propose SRAP, which combines per-channel truncated SVD refinement with an identity-importance mask at every optimization step. The SVD refinement suppresses high-rank, high-frequency residuals, while the mask restricts perturbations to locations that strongly influence identity representations. Experiments on CelebA-HQ and VGGFace2-HQ demonstrate that SRAP substantially improves protected-image fidelity across all reported metrics while maintaining competitive identity-disruption performance, yielding a favorable trade-off between face-swap defense and visual imperceptibility.

</details>


### [72] [Distilled Roads: Generalisable Road Network Extraction Across Sensors, Resolutions, and Region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03407)
*Sanayya,Rakshith Sathish,Ashwathi Nambiar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将全球道路提取重新定义为持续适应问题，通过跨分辨率知识蒸馏、分辨率递减课程、多传感器训练和拓扑感知监督的组合策略，训练单一模型以在0.3-1.0米分辨率的多种卫星平台图像上泛化，在公开基准上相比SOTA最高提升22个F1点和15个APLS点，同时推理速度快3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道路分割模型通常在狭窄的分辨率-区域组合下训练，对未见环境（如乡村场景、不同道路材质、新卫星平台）泛化能力差，常产生断裂或不连续的预测；适应新领域需要昂贵的重训练或微调，且存在灾难性遗忘风险。

Method: 将全球道路提取重构为持续适应问题，而非架构问题。核心方法包括三个组件：1) 分辨率递减课程上的跨分辨率知识蒸馏；2) 多传感器联合训练；3) 拓扑感知监督（如APLS相关损失）。通过这三者的组合训练一个统一模型。

Result: 在City-Scale和Global-Scale等公开基准上，模型相比SOTA最高提升22个F1点和15个APLS点，同时保持最高效率，推理速度提升3倍。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，通过有针对性的训练策略（数据课程、蒸馏、拓扑感知损失）而非日益复杂的架构，就可以显著提升多样亚米级卫星图像上的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Road network segmentation from satellite imagery remains challenging due to large geographic variation in road appearance, occlusions, and domain shifts introduced by differing resolutions and sensors. Existing models, typically trained under narrow resolution--region combinations, generalise poorly to unseen environments such as rural settings, regions with distinct road materials, or imagery from new satellite platforms, often producing broken or disconnected predictions. Adapting these models to new domains usually requires retraining or fine-tuning, which is costly and risks catastrophic forgetting.
  In this work, we reframe global road extraction as a continual adaptation problem rather than an architectural one. Our framework combines cross-resolution knowledge distillation across a resolution-decreasing curriculum, multi-sensor training, and topology-aware supervision, yielding a single model that generalises across $0.3-1.0$ m imagery from multiple satellite platforms across continents. On publicly available benchmarks, including City-Scale and Global-Scale, our model outperforms state-of-the-art results by up to $22$ F1 points and $15$ APLS points, while remaining the most efficient, with $3\times$ faster inference. Our results suggest that improved robustness across diverse sub-meter satellite imagery can be achieved through targeted training strategies, such as data curricula, distillation, and topology-aware losses, rather than increasingly complex architectures.

</details>


### [73] [Earth Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03410)
*Adam J. Stewart,Heng Fang,Isaac A. Corley,Xiao Xiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了"地球嵌入"（Earth embeddings）这一新兴方向，即从基础模型中提取的矢量特征产品，用于替代用户自行运行大模型处理原始卫星影像的传统工作流。文章涵盖嵌入类型、产品特性对比、下游应用、案例研究以及选择/评估/存储/发布的实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球观测正从用户自行运行基础模型转向嵌入产品化，将模型特征输出打包为可重用数据。本文动机在于系统梳理这一新兴领域的各类嵌入产品，比较其优劣，并评估其在下游任务中的实际表现，为研究者和从业者提供清晰的选型和使用指引。

Method: 本文为综述性章节，采用系统比较框架：按嵌入类型（隐式位置编码器、显式图像块/像素产品）分类，从覆盖范围、分辨率、维度、存储成本、许可证和可复现性等维度对比；综述陆地覆盖与作物制图、生态与灾害建模、社会经济预测及语义搜索等应用领域；并通过两个案例研究（相似性搜索和土地覆盖制图）展示实际工作流。

Result: 综述发现嵌入在部分任务上优于传统特征，但池化（pooling）、融合（fusion）或空间迁移（spatial transfer）可能限制其性能；同时识别出海洋和大气覆盖、不确定性量化及基准测试等方面的开放问题。

Conclusion: 文章最终为选择、评估、存储、压缩和发布嵌入产品提供实用指南，并指出该领域尚待解决的关键问题包括海洋/大气覆盖不足、不确定性问题以及缺乏标准化基准，为未来研究方向提出了展望。

Abstract: Earth observation is moving from foundation models that users must run themselves toward embedding products that package model feature outputs as reusable data without needing to download and process the imagery used to generate them. Earth embeddings are vectors that summarize locations, image patches, or pixels, letting users analyze compact features instead of repeatedly training or running large models on raw satellite imagery. This chapter explains the main types of Earth embeddings, from implicit location encoders to explicit patch and pixel products, and compares their coverage, resolution, dimensionality, storage cost, licenses, and reproducibility. We review their use in land cover and crop mapping, ecological and hazard modeling, socioeconomic prediction, and semantic search, with evidence on when embeddings improve on conventional features and when pooling, fusion, or spatial transfer limit performance. Two case studies show practical workflows for similarity search and land cover mapping. We close with guidance for choosing, evaluating, storing, compressing, and publishing embeddings, and with open problems in oceanic and atmospheric coverage, uncertainty, and benchmarking.

</details>


### [74] [HyperbolicDiffusion: Sharp & Scalable Tiled Generation on the Hyperbolic Plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03422)
*Hugo Caselles-Dupré*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperbolicDiffusion，一种无需训练的扩散模型方法，直接在双曲平面H2上生成有限视觉场。通过Hyperbolic Blooming Cover将窗口放置简化为可在数秒内求解的紧凑动态规划，并在不同窗口预测融合回H2后，利用几何推导的第二阶段对曲率导致的残差不一致与模糊区域进行重新加噪和修复，最终生成清晰、可重投影且跨视角一致的图像，成为Escher《Circle Limit》系列的提示驱动生成对应物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面扩散模型通过重叠窗口去噪矩形画布，但双曲平面没有此类画布，且其面积随半径指数增长。因此需要一种方法直接在双曲平面上生成有限的视觉场，以弥合标准扩散模型与双曲几何之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) Hyperbolic Blooming Cover：将窗口放置问题归约为紧凑动态规划，运行时间仅数秒，同时提供强理论保证；2) 永久表面ID构成共享潜在画布，标准扩散模型去噪局部窗口并将其预测融合回H2；3) 因曲率导致多窗口接缝处出现残差不一致和模糊，设计一个几何推导的第二阶段对这些特定区域进行重新加噪与修复。

Result: 生成的视觉场清晰锐利、可重投影，并在不同视角下保持一致，可在双曲平面上实现提示驱动的生成效果，对应Escher的Circle Limit系列艺术风格。

Conclusion: HyperbolicDiffusion成功将扩散模型扩展到双曲几何，通过高效窗口覆盖与几何感知修复策略，在无训练条件下实现了双曲平面上的高质量生成，为多视图一致的双曲视觉生成开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Planar tiled diffusion denoises overlapping windows of one rectangular canvas. The hyperbolic plane has no such canvas, and its area grows exponentially with radius.
