These results motivate future geospatial embedders that organize training and evaluation around explicit query-target relations, including semantic, cross-view, region-level, and temporal correspondence.

</details>


### [107] [LiteMVS: Efficient Multi-View Stereo with Foundation Distillation and Expert Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03851)
*Tianbao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Shuyu Wu,Fanxing Li,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenjun Wu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteMVS是一种轻量级多视角深度估计模型，通过将平面扫描几何推理与单目语义和结构先验相结合，在保持高效性的同时提升静态场景中的深度估计和3D重建质量，并为后续的时序建模与4D表示学习提供可靠的几何基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MVS方法依赖几何对应关系，在无纹理或重复区域容易失效；单目深度模型虽具备强图像先验但缺乏多视角几何约束。此外，机器人操作和具身智能场景不仅需要静态重建，还需要为一致的4D表示学习提供高质量几何基础。因此需要一种兼具几何推理能力和单目先验的高效深度估计模型。

Method: LiteMVS的核心方法是将从轻量级分割模型和大规模视觉基础模型中获取的高层单目知识高效注入MVS框架。具体包括：(1) 用语义描述符丰富代价体；(2) 利用混合专家（MoE）机制实现跨深度假设的自适应几何聚合；(3) 从视觉基础模型蒸馏几何先验以增强单目引导且不增加推理成本。

Result: 在ScanNetv2和7-Scenes数据集上的实验表明，LiteMVS在保持有竞争力的效率的同时，实现了高质量的深度预测和3D重建。

Conclusion: LiteMVS不仅提升了静态场景中的深度估计和3D重建质量，而且为后续时序建模和4D表示学习提供了更可靠的几何基础，展示了多视角几何推理与单目语义先验融合的潜力。

Abstract: Real-time 3D perception is crucial for robotics, augmented reality, and embodied intelligence applications. Existing multi-view stereo (MVS) methods primarily rely on geometric correspondences, which often fail in textureless or repetitive regions, while monocular depth models leverage strong image-level priors but lack robust multi-view geometric constraints. More importantly, in robotics and embodied manipulation scenarios, high-quality 3D geometry is not only essential for static reconstruction, but also serves as a critical foundation for learning temporally consistent 4D representations. To obtain visual representations with stronger structural awareness and greater potential for spatiotemporal extension, we present LiteMVS, a lightweight multi-view depth estimation model that integrates plane-sweep geometric reasoning with strong monocular semantic and structural priors. The central idea of LiteMVS is to efficiently inject high-level monocular knowledge, obtained from lightweight segmentation models and large-scale vision foundation models, into a multi-view stereo framework. In particular, LiteMVS enriches the cost volume with semantic descriptors and employs a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) formulation to enable adaptive geometric aggregation across depth hypotheses. Moreover, geometric priors distilled from vision foundation models further strengthen monocular guidance without increasing inference cost. Through this design, LiteMVS not only improves depth estimation and 3D reconstruction quality in static scenes, but also provides a more reliable geometric foundation for subsequent temporal modeling and 4D representation learning. Experiments on ScanNetv2 and 7-Scenes demonstrate that LiteMVS achieves high-quality depth prediction and 3D reconstruction while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [108] [CPrefix: A Combinatorial Tensor Framework for Structured Discrete Color Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03863)
*Yvan Richard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPrefix，一种基于组合可观测量的离散多通道映射表示框架，通过计数张量与离散Pascal单纯形实现映射的表示、重建与结构分析，并在ICC显示与打印配置文件上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散多通道映射通常以采样值表示，虽能提供精确评估，但难以揭示其底层结构。现有方法缺乏一种能够分离组合组织与测量值、同时支持重建和结构分析的统一表示框架。

Method: CPrefix框架基于由多项计数可观测量诱导的计数张量，其支撑集构成离散Pascal单纯形，作为潜在组合表示而非可观测量空间的约束。该框架将映射的组合组织与测量值分离，在统一的张量框架中实现表示、重建和结构分析。

Result: 在ICC显示与打印机配置文件上的验证中，通过潜在重建和感知色域传输实现了精确重建，表明颜色映射具有忠实的可观测表示；重建残差揭示了底层映射与所提表示的兼容性信息。

Conclusion: 虽然该框架在颜色变换上得到验证，但其不依赖于观测量的物理解释，可推广应用于颜色成像、光谱测量及其他离散系统中出现的结构化多通道映射。

Abstract: Discrete multi-channel mappings are typically represented through sampled values, providing accurate evaluations but limited insight into their underlying structure. We introduce CPrefix, a combinatorial observable representation for discrete mappings, realized within a unified tensor framework that enables representation, reconstruction, and structural analysis.
  The framework is based on a counting tensor induced by multinomial counting observables. Its support forms a discrete Pascal simplex, not as a constraint on the observable space, but as a latent combinatorial representation from which mappings are reconstructed. This formulation separates the combinatorial organization of a mapping from its measured values, exposing the observable structure underlying the mapping.
  The framework is validated on ICC display and printer profiles through latent reconstruction and perceptual gamut transport. Accurate reconstruction demonstrates that color mappings admit faithful observable representations, while reconstruction residuals provide insight into the compatibility of the underlying mapping with the proposed representation.
  Although demonstrated on color transformations, the framework is independent of the physical interpretation of the observables, making it applicable to structured multi-channel mappings arising from color imaging, spectral measurements and other discrete systems.

</details>


### [109] [BanglaWild: An In-the-Wild Bengali Scene Text Recognition Benchmark for OCR and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03884)
*Sadab Shiper,Tawsif Tashwar Dipto,Mir Md Inzamam,Eshat Tanzeem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BANGLAWILD基准数据集，包含2,535张孟加拉语场景文本图像，并评估了15个VLM和3个传统OCR系统。核心发现是：同一模型家族中更大模型并不优于更小模型；视觉误识别占最强系统错误的~60%，而辅音连字相关错误仅占2%以下，挑战了孟加拉语OCR研究中的长期假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外孟加拉语场景文本识别长期缺乏系统性评测：现有资源要么针对手写文档，要么局限于受限的招牌解析；只报告聚合编辑距离指标；且从未在同一野外数据上同时评估传统OCR和视觉语言模型（VLM）。这一空白阻碍了孟加拉语场景文本识别领域的可靠进展评估。

Method: 构建BANGLAWILD基准：包含2,535张孟加拉语场景文本图像，每张配有逐字黄金转录、两个分类轴、四个诊断属性，以及当图像内文本偏离规范拼写时的正字法标准形式。在三种提示策略下评估15个VLM和3个传统OCR系统，用LoRA微调6个开源模型，并用LLM作为裁判的评估方式补充编辑距离指标。

Result: 结果显示存在持续的性能差距，即同一家族中更大模型并未超越更小模型。十五类错误分类法表明视觉误识别占最强系统错误的约60%，而辅音连字相关错误贡献不足2%，挑战了孟加拉语OCR研究中长期存在的假设；这一视觉主导特征跨架构一致，包括唯一能可靠读取孟加拉语的传统基线。提示语言主要影响跨脚本漂移，LoRA能减少弱模型中的灾难性失败，但未能提升已具备能力模型的上限。

Conclusion: 视觉误识别是孟加拉语场景文本识别的主要瓶颈，而非辅音连字问题；模型规模与性能之间不存在简单正相关；提示语言和LoRA微调的作用有限。论文将公开代码和数据，为该领域提供标准化评测基准。

Abstract: In-the-wild Bengali scene text recognition is largely unmeasured: existing resources target handwritten documents or constrained sign-board parsing, report only aggregate edit-distance metrics, and evaluate either conventional OCR or VLMs, never both on the same in-the-wild data. To address this gap, we introduce BANGLAWILD, a benchmark of 2,535 Bengali scene text images, each paired with a verbatim gold transcription, two categorical axes, four diagnostic attributes, and an orthographically standard form where the in-image text deviates from canonical spelling. We evaluate fifteen VLMs and three conventional OCR systems under three prompting strategies, fine-tune 6 open-source models with LoRA, and complement edit-distance metrics with an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our results reveal a persistent gap in which larger models within the same family do not outperform smaller ones. Our fifteen-class error taxonomy shows that visual mis-recognition accounts for ~60% of errors in the strongest systems, while conjunct-related errors contribute under 2%, challenging a long-standing assumption in Bengali OCR research; the same visual dominant profile also holds across architectures, including the one conventional baseline that reads Bengali reliably. Prompt language mainly affects cross-script drift and LoRA reduces catastrophic failures in weak models without lifting the ceiling on already competent ones. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [110] [MuRA: Multi-Rank Adaptation for Efficient and Effective Test-Time Vision-Language Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03885)
*Gengyuan Liu,Nanzhou Wang,Chang Liu,Qinwen Wu,Zhenhao Wang,Jiacong Wang,Bokui Chen,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuRA是一个新颖的多秩自适应测试时适应框架，通过动态选择和融合不同容量的适配模块，并根据token级视觉复杂度进行秩配置，解决了现有TTA方法中固定秩配置导致的欠拟合和过拟合问题，在域泛化和跨数据集基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型在分布偏移下性能显著下降。虽然基于低秩适配的测试时适应（TTA）提供了参数高效的解决方案，但现有方法依赖静态秩配置，而视觉输入天然具有可变的信息密度，固定秩导致不可避免的优化折中——复杂场景下欠拟合、简单场景下过拟合。

Method: MuRA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 多秩正交分解（Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition），提供优越且知识保持的初始化；(2) 统一组件融合与连续路由器更新（Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating），可持续学习语义到秩的映射。整个机制基于token级视觉复杂度动态选择和融合不同容量的适配模块，并利用了最深层视觉层的最短梯度反向传播路径。

Result: 广泛实验表明MuRA在域泛化和跨数据集基准上达到最先进精度，同时显著降低了计算和内存开销。此外，论文提供了严格的数学证明来验证自适应机制的必要性和梯度稳定性。

Conclusion: MuRA通过动态多秩适配机制有效解决了固定秩在TTA中的瓶颈问题，证明了动态秩配置在分布偏移场景下的优越性，并通过理论和实验双重验证了该方法的有效性和效率优势。

Abstract: Vision-language models exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities but suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts. While test-time adaptation (TTA) via Low-Rank Adaptation offers a parameter-efficient solution, we identify a fundamental bottleneck in current methods: the reliance on static rank configurations. Because visual inputs inherently possess varying information densities, a fixed rank forces an inevitable optimization compromise, leading to underfitting on complex scenes and overfitting on simple ones. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-Rank Adaptation (MuRA), a novel framework that dynamically selects and fuses adaptation modules of varying capacities based on token-level visual complexity. MuRA synergizes Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition to provide a superior, knowledge-preserving initialization, and Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating to sustainably learn semantic-to-rank mappings. Furthermore, we provide rigorous theoretical justifications mathematically proving the necessity and gradient stability of this adaptive mechanism. Crucially, MuRA's dynamic design uniquely thrives at the deepest visual layer, capitalizing on the shortest gradient backpropagation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MuRA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across extensive domain generalization and cross-dataset benchmarks while significantly reducing both computational and memory overhead.

</details>


### [111] [CARE-X: Towards Clinically Useful Radiology VLMs with Auxiliary Supervision, Reward-Aligned Learning, and Tool-Augmented Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03890)
*Mercy Prasanna Ranjit,Anirban Porya,Sathvik Joel,Niharika Vadlamudi,Nikhilesh Chowdary Eathamukkala,Prasanth V,Abhyuday Kumara Swamy,Pranay Narhari Umredkar,Pradeep Narayan,Vivek Rajagopal,Tanuja Ganu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARE-X是一个用于胸部X射线(CXR)分析的视觉语言模型(VLM)，通过统一辅助判别监督（焦点损失分类头+复合损失定位头）与基于奖励的对齐生成（DAPO策略优化），同时实现可调阈值的发现分类、空间定位、报告生成和基于测量工具的诊断推理。在四个报告生成基准上达到多数指标的最先进性能，ReXVQA准确率达94.0%，并通过工具调用实现测量依赖诊断的混合推理，平均F1提升43.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸部X射线系统将报告生成与发现分类、空间定位、解剖测量视为分离问题。临床可用的CXR系统需要超越流畅的报告生成——需支持可调判别阈值的发现分类、空间定位及基于解剖测量的诊断，而当前VLMs未能统一这些能力，导致生成模型与放射科医生实际需求之间存在鸿沟。

Method: CARE-X采用三层架构设计：(1) 在生成骨干网络上增加焦点损失(focal-loss)分类头和复合损失(composite-loss)定位头，与语言建模目标协同训练，提供可调阈值的判别式诊断预测和空间定位；(2) 提出解耦裁剪与动态采样策略优化(DAPO)，利用任务特异性奖励信号对报告生成、视觉问答和空间定位进行直接优化；(3) 将Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct与原生工具调用能力耦合，调用确定性测量工具进行解剖测量，同时保留完整视觉访问的混合推理框架。

Result: 在四个报告生成基准上多数指标达到最先进性能；ReXVQA视觉问答准确率达94.0%（超过次优基线6.0个百分点）；生成式空间解码与专用检测头接近相当；在五个测量依赖条件下，混合推理平均F1比仅感知基线提升43.6个百分点。辅助监督同时改善了报告质量，验证了结构化预测与生成相互增强。

Conclusion: CARE-X证明辅助判别监督与奖励对齐生成能够相互增强——结构化预测不仅提供可调阈值和精确定位，还提升了报告质量。工具调用与VLMs的混合推理有效弥合了测量依赖诊断与纯感知模型之间的差距，为临床级CXR分析系统提供了统一框架。

Abstract: A clinically useful chest X-ray system must go beyond fluent report generation: it should classify findings with tunable decision thresholds, localize them spatially, and derive the anatomical measurements upon which many diagnoses depend. Today's Vision-Language Models (VLMs) treat these as separate problems, if they address them at all, leaving a gap between what radiologists need and what generative models provide. We introduce CARE-X, a chest X-ray VLM that narrows this gap by unifying auxiliary discriminative supervision with reward-aligned generation. CARE-X augments its generative backbone with focal-loss classification and composite-loss grounding heads, co-trained alongside the language-modeling objective. This auxiliary supervision produces discriminative diagnostic predictions with tunable decision thresholds and precise spatial localization while also improving report quality, providing evidence that structured prediction and generation reinforce one another. Building on this foundation, Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) leverages task-specific reward signals for report generation, visual question answering (VQA), and spatial grounding, directly optimizing the clinical quality metrics that matter in practice. The result is state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics across four report-generation benchmarks, 94.0% VQA accuracy on ReXVQA (+6.0 pp over the next-best baseline), and generative spatial decoding that reaches near parity with dedicated detection heads. Separately, to address measurement-dependent diagnoses, we couple Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct with native tool-calling capabilities for invoking deterministic measurement tools, while retaining full visual access to the image. This hybrid inference yields +43.6 pp average F1 over perception-only baselines across five measurement-dependent conditions.

</details>


### [112] [NCGR: Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification for Camera Extrinsic Perturbations in BEV 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03895)
*Wenbin Pan,Wanhao Liu,Liwei Luo,Panshuo Li,Yong Xu,Renquan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出噪声条件门控校正（NCGR）方法，用于解决相机外参扰动下基于鸟瞰图（BEV）的3D检测性能退化问题。NCGR通过为每个查询-相机对预测2D校正偏移，并利用相机级门控调制来补偿投影误差，无需显式估计完整的六自由度外参校正。通过训练时的计划性插值过渡，NCGR能够实现无扰动元数据的盲推理。在nuScenes数据集上，五相机动态外参扰动压力测试中NCGR达到39.69% NDS，显著优于BEVFormer（28.00%）和CAPE（33.23%），且在干净外参下保持与BEVFormer相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相机的BEV 3D检测通常假设精确且固定的相机外参，但在使用空间交叉注意力（SCA）的检测器中，外参扰动会导致BEV参考点在图像平面上的投影偏移，使查询从错误区域采样特征，从而严重降低检测性能。现有方法缺乏对此失效模式的有效补偿。

Method: NCGR为每个查询-相机对预测一个2D校正偏移，并由相机级门控调制，以在原生可变形采样之前校正基础投影。训练时，用于构建条件和门控的扰动派生量通过计划性插值，逐步替换为由相机特征预测的辅助标量生成的对应量，从而实现无需扰动元数据的盲推理。训练中采用权重共享的干净教师/扰动学生配对，并通过BEV一致性目标监督校正模块。

Result: 在nuScenes的五相机动态外参应力测试中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，分别比BEVFormer（28.00%）和CAPE（33.23%）高出11.69和6.46个百分点。在干净外参条件下，NCGR性能与BEVFormer相当。

Conclusion: NCGR通过无需显式估计完整外参校正的门控式2D投影偏移补偿机制，有效增强了BEV检测器对外参扰动的鲁棒性，同时在不牺牲干净外参条件下性能的情况下实现了盲推理能力。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye-view (BEV) 3D detection typically assumes accurate and fixed camera extrinsics. In detectors using spatial cross-attention (SCA), extrinsic perturbations displace the image-plane projections of BEV reference points, causing queries to sample features from incorrect regions and degrading detection performance. To address this failure mode, Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification (NCGR) is proposed to compensate for projection errors without explicitly estimating a full six-degree-of-freedom extrinsic correction. For each query-camera pair, a 2D rectification offset is predicted and modulated by a camera-level gate to rectify the base projection before native deformable sampling. During training, the perturbation-derived quantities used to construct the condition and gate are gradually replaced through scheduled interpolation by counterparts generated from an auxiliary scalar predicted from camera features. This transition enables blind inference without perturbation metadata. During training, a weight-shared clean-teacher/perturbed-student pair is used, and the rectification module is supervised by a BEV-consistency objective between the two branches. NCGR is evaluated on nuScenes with simulated dynamic and static extrinsic perturbations. In a five-camera dynamic stress test, NCGR achieves 39.69% NDS, compared with 28.00% for BEVFormer and 33.23% for CAPE. Under clean extrinsics, NCGR maintains performance comparable to that of BEVFormer.

</details>


### [113] [UniEvo-RS: Omni-Prompt Unified Remote Sensing Segmentation with Representative Exemplar-Driven Prototype Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03911)
*Kunquan Zhang,Peilang Li,Xikun Hu,Yunkai Yang,Yushan Zou,Zhiwei Zhang,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniEvo-RS是一个面向密集遥感分割的统一提示框架，通过多指令提示数据集和代表性样本驱动、免训练的原型演化机制，实现了文本与视觉提示的统一，并在批量标注过程中对未见类别实现渐进式精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态视觉-语言模型在新型场景、未见类别或视觉混淆背景下部署时性能严重退化；现有统一范式主要依赖图像内特定提示，缺乏灵活的任务路由能力以适应多意图的操作工作流。实际批量标注中，标注者通常先精炼少量代表性样本再处理大规模数据，这一实践未被现有方法充分利用。

Method: 1) 构建多指令提示数据集，在单一架构中统一文本驱动与视觉驱动提示，建立动态任务路由机制；2) 提出代表性反馈驱动的免训练原型演化机制，通过对比代表性样本上的手动标注与初始预测，将预测错误蒸馏为正负原型，利用固定预算聚类记忆增强LLM查询召回并抑制空间背景噪声。

Result: 实验表明UniEvo-RS在大多数设置下实现了最先进性能，统一了多样化的提示任务。仅需对少量代表性样本进行最小交互，即可在批量标注过程中对未见类别实现免训练的渐进式精度增强。

Conclusion: UniEvo-RS验证了代表性样本驱动原型演化在遥感批量标注中的有效性，为多意图、多提示类型的统一RS分割提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Prompt-driven vision-language models (VLMs) hold immense promise for accelerating dense remote sensing (RS) annotation, but static models suffer from severe performance degradation when deployed on novel scenes, unseen categories, or visually confusing backgrounds. Moreover, existing unified paradigms primarily rely on intra-image specific prompts, lacking flexible task routing to adapt to multi-intent operational workflows. In practical batch mapping, annotators typically refine a small set of representative samples before processing large datasets. Motivated by this practice, we propose UniEvo-RS, an omni-prompt unified RS segmentation framework equipped with representative exemplar-driven prototype evolution. First, we construct a multi-instruction prompt dataset that unifies text-driven and visual-driven prompts within a single architecture, establishing a dynamic task-routing mechanism for highly diverse RS annotation scenarios. Second, we introduce a representative feedback-driven, training-free prototype evolution mechanism. By contrasting manual annotations with initial predictions on exemplars, UniEvo-RS distills prediction errors into positive and negative prototypes. These prototypes enhance LLM query recall and suppress spatial background noise under a fixed-budget clustering memory. Extensive experiments show that UniEvo-RS unifies diverse prompting tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance across most settings. Crucially, with minimal interaction on a few exemplars, it enables training-free, progressive accuracy enhancement on unseen categories during batch annotation.

</details>


### [114] [StreamDAM: Presence-Aware Memory for Real-Time Streaming Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03912)
*Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向流式视频目标分割（VOS）的跟踪器方法，通过重建内存管线并引入单一学习到的存在性信号，实现在30fps实时协议下追平甚至超越离线模型的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高质量VOS跟踪器（如DAM4SAM）在离线场景下精度领先，但在真实的流式协议（30fps）下性能崩溃：其精确所需的大内存处理速度跟不上帧率，且输出无法感知目标是否仍然存在。作者将这两个失败根因归结于跟踪器的内存管线，并指出固定策略无法同时应对目标消失与目标难辨两种情形，需要逐帧自适应决策。

Method: 该方法核心是重建内存管线，使得内存机制本身在帧率内运行，通过模型内优化而非外部回退策略实现。具体由一个学习到的存在性信号统一控制四个关键决策：什么内容进入内存、跟踪器向后读取多远的记忆、何时暂停输出、以及何时重新检测目标。该信号根据帧内容动态调整，而非使用固定策略。

Result: 在四个基准数据集和五种现代基线的评估中，该方法成为最强的流式跟踪器，在时钟限制下几乎恢复了离线模型全部精度，在最具挑战性的内容上甚至超过了其构建所基于的离线模型。

Conclusion: 存在性信号的逐帧决策机制是流式VOS性能的关键：固定策略无法兼顾目标真实消失与目标难见两种相互冲突的场景，而基于学习的自适应控制在流式推理中能够弥合离线与在线精度差距。

Abstract: Quality-tier video object segmentation (VOS) trackers such as DAM4SAM top accuracy leaderboards, but they are measured offline, one frame at a time with no clock. Under an honest streaming protocol at 30 frames per second, where a frame that misses its budget is served the last mask already computed, the winner collapses: the rich memory that makes it accurate is too slow to keep up, and what it emits is blind to whether the object is even present. We trace both failures to one place, the tracker's memory pipeline, and rebuild it for streaming. \method{} makes the memory machinery itself run at frame rate through in-model optimization rather than a bolted-on fallback, and governs it with a single learned presence signal that decides what enters memory, how far back the tracker reads, when to withhold output, and when to re-detect. A mechanism analysis shows why a fixed policy cannot win: the control that helps when an object truly disappears is the one that hurts when it is merely hard to see, so the choice must be made per frame. Across four benchmarks and five modern baselines, \method{} is the strongest streaming tracker, recovers nearly all of the offline model's accuracy under the clock, and on the hardest content exceeds the offline model it is built from.

</details>


### [115] [When and Where to Look: Adaptive Visual Evidence Scheduling for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03918)
*Ke Li,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Hailong Zou,Hengyi Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EcoFrame是一个无需训练的查询自适应视觉证据调度框架，通过熵门控预算调度和注意力引导候选提议，在保持精度的同时显著提升长视频理解效率。在多个基准和VLM骨干上验证了更好的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频理解方法存在两类问题：基于相关性的方法依赖静态一次性选择和固定帧预算，缺乏自适应性；基于智能体(agent)的调度器通过昂贵的多轮推理和交互搜索实现自适应性，计算开销过大。需要一个低开销的查询自适应视觉证据调度方案。

Method: EcoFrame包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵门控预算调度——利用VLM推理输出的不确定性(熵)决定是否提前停止或逐步扩大帧预算；(2) 注意力引导候选提议——将帧级注意力转化为时间先验，在信息密集区域进行局部搜索，同时在注意力分散时保持全局覆盖。整个框架无需训练。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU基准上，EcoFrame在多个VLM骨干上实现了更好的精度-效率权衡。在Qwen2.5-VL上平均精度达到64.4，超过BOLT的63.5，同时相对AKS和BOLT提供1.85倍加速；相对基于智能体的A.I.R.，在保持相当精度的同时最高实现13.5倍推理加速。

Conclusion: EcoFrame通过利用VLM自身的推理反馈（熵与注意力）实现低开销的查询自适应帧调度，有效弥合了静态选择方法与基于智能体方法之间的效率与自适应性差距，是一种训练免费且实用的长视频理解解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires vision--language models (VLMs) to reason over a small number of frames selected as sparse visual evidence. Existing relevance-based methods rely on static one-shot selection with fixed frame budgets and candidate pools, while agent-based schedulers achieve adaptivity through costly multi-round reasoning and interactive search. We propose EcoFrame, a training-free framework for low-overhead query-adaptive visual evidence scheduling. EcoFrame leverages the VLM's inference feedback to determine when to increase the frame budget and where to search for additional candidate evidence. Specifically, entropy-gated budget scheduling uses output uncertainty to stop early when the current evidence is sufficient or progressively expand the frame budget otherwise. Meanwhile, attention-guided candidate proposal converts frame-level attention into a temporal prior, enabling dense local search in informative regions while preserving global coverage when attention is diffuse. Experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU demonstrate that EcoFrame achieves a better accuracy--efficiency trade-off across multiple VLM backbones. On Qwen2.5-VL, EcoFrame achieves an average accuracy of 64.4, surpassing BOLT at 63.5, while providing a $1.85\times$ speedup over AKS and BOLT. Compared with the agent-based A.I.R., EcoFrame maintains comparable accuracy with up to a $13.5\times$ inference speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/EcoFrame.

</details>


### [116] [Low-Dimensional High-Leverage Subspace Optimization: Beyond Full-Parameter Coupled Training for Neural Network Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03919)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang,Zheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出规范化仿射预处理（NAP）方法，识别BN仿射参数为控制量化鲁棒性的低维高杠杆子空间，通过PTQ和QAT两种模式下针对该子空间进行定向优化，以极低成本恢复低比特量化中紧凑网络的精度崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化在紧凑网络上精度严重下降，根源在于传统的全参数耦合训练范式忽略了参数子空间的异质性。紧凑网络特征冗余有限，难以吸收量化误差；现有PTQ无法提升模型固有的量化友好性，而QAT中骨干权重与校准参数的梯度耦合限制了优化效果。

Method: 提出NAP方法：1) 对PTQ，冻结骨干权重，在目标伪量化图下仅微调仿射参数，主动提升量化友好性后再进行下游重建；2) 对QAT，引入交替QAT-NAP模式，解耦特征学习与数值校准，打破联合训练的饱和上限。理论分析证明了BN仿射参数可完全抵消量化失真的通道级仿射分量，蒸馏引导的NAP被证明是方向性平坦度优化。

Result: 在ImageNet和CIFAR-100上，NAP能恢复严重崩溃的低比特量化，一致地提升基于重建的PTQ性能，并以极小的调参成本超越饱和的全参数QAT。

Conclusion: 这项工作揭示了定向低维子空间优化的原理，为高效深度学习提供了超越全参数耦合训练的新视角。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization suffers severe accuracy degradation on compact networks, rooted in the dominant full-parameter coupled training paradigm that ignores parameter subspace heterogeneity. Their limited feature redundancy leaves little room to absorb quantization errors. Conventional pipelines adopt monolithic optimization: PTQ reconstructs fixed pretrained models without improving inherent quantization friendliness; QAT updates all parameters jointly, suffering from gradient coupling between backbone weights and calibration parameters. In this paper, we identify normalization affine parameters as a low-dimensional high-leverage subspace dominating quantization robustness, and propose Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP) for targeted subspace optimization. For PTQ, NAP freezes backbone weights and fine-tunes only affine parameters under the target fake-quantization graph on full-precision models, proactively boosting quantization friendliness before downstream reconstruction. For QAT, we introduce an alternating QAT-NAP schema that decouples feature learning and numerical calibration, breaking the performance ceiling of saturated joint training. Theoretical analysis confirms BN affine parameters fully cancel the channel-wise affine component of quantization distortion, while nonlinear rounding and clipping residuals form the irreducible error boundary; distillation-guided NAP acts as directional flatness optimization, projecting teacher-student logit mismatch onto the restricted subspace. Experiments on ImageNet and CIFAR-100 show NAP recovers severely collapsed low-bit quantization, consistently boosts reconstruction-based PTQ, and outperforms saturated full-parameter QAT with negligible tuning cost. This work reveals the principle of targeted low-dimensional subspace optimization, offering a new perspective beyond full-parameter coupled training for efficient deep learning.

</details>


### [117] [GeoMAR: Unleashing Geometrically Aligned Features for Masked Autoregressive Blind Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03923)
*Lu Gan,Hanyu Yan,Chaofeng Chen,Junqi Hu,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMAR是一个盲人脸复原框架，通过双输入提取管道获得基于组件的几何描述，利用对齐几何先验注入器（KV-Q交换策略）生成几何对齐特征，并将一步映射重构为多步掩码自回归（MAR）过程进行渐进式细化。在合成和三个真实世界基准上取得了具有竞争力的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于码本的盲人脸复原方法在严重退化条件下常面临两个关键问题：一是条件特征模糊（ambiguous conditioning features），二是预测机制脆弱（fragile prediction mechanism），导致复原结果不够鲁棒。

Method: 1) 双输入提取管道：提取基于组件的几何描述（含显式、空间忠实的锚点），并与低质量特征结合；2) 对齐几何先验注入器（Aligned Geometric Priors Injector）：采用KV-Q交换策略生成几何对齐特征；3) 掩码自回归（MAR）细化：将单步映射重构为多步粗到细生成过程，基于越来越可靠的上下文逐步细化复杂面部区域。

Result: 在一个合成基准和三个真实世界基准上，GeoMAR相比现有方法取得了高度有竞争力的感知质量和连贯的视觉结构。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了GeoMAR在严重退化条件下盲人脸复原的有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Codebook-based blind face restoration (BFR) often suffers from ambiguous conditioning features and a fragile prediction mechanism under severe degradation. To address these challenges, we propose GeoMAR, a framework designed to unleash geometrically aligned features with masked autoregressive (MAR) refinement for robust face restoration. For feature conditioning, we introduce a dual-input extraction pipeline to extract component-based geometric descriptions with explicit, spatially faithful anchors. These textual priors are integrated with low-quality (LQ) features via an Aligned Geometric Priors Injector, which employs a KV-Q exchange strategy to generate geometrically aligned features. For prediction mechanism, we reformulate the one-step mapping into a multi-step MAR process. This coarse-to-fine generation progressively refines complex facial regions based on increasingly reliable context. Experiments on one synthetic and three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GeoMAR achieves highly competitive perceptual quality and coherent visual structures compared with existing methods. The code is available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/GeoMAR.git.

</details>


### [118] [Progressive Learning of a Diffusion-based Inpainting Model for Separating Overlapped Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03937)
*Noor Hussein,Anil K. Jain,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的指纹重叠分离方法，将重叠指纹分离问题建模为图像修复任务，通过多阶段渐进式训练（从预训练的Stable Diffusion出发，逐步引入指纹先验、部分指纹补全能力，最终提出基于多通道条件的重叠感知修复），在两个公开数据集上实现了高概率的指纹匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠摩擦脊线图案是犯罪现场潜在指纹和活体扫描场景中反复出现的问题，现有方法要么依赖基于规则的取向场补全（需要强领域知识），要么训练不考虑领域特定考量的端到端深度神经网络。这两种方法各有不足，需要一种能结合领域知识且性能优越的解决思路。

Method: 将重叠指纹分离问题形式化为图像修复（inpainting）任务。采用多阶段渐进式扩散模型训练流程：从预训练的Stable Diffusion模型出发，逐步融入指纹先验知识；然后增加部分指纹补全能力；最终提出overlap-aware inpainting方法，基于多通道条件（multi-channel conditioning）利用扩散修复模型分别重建每个成分指纹。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，使用所提出的基于扩散的修复方法重建出的成分指纹，能够以非常高的概率与其配对的对应指纹成功匹配。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合多阶段渐进式训练和多通道条件修复可以有效地解决重叠指纹分离问题，且该方法在重建质量上优于依赖规则或端到端训练的传统方法。

Abstract: Overlapped friction ridge patterns are a recurring problem in latent fingerprints recovered from crime scenes and in live-scan scenarios where residual fingerprints on the sensor may corrupt subsequent acquisitions. Existing approaches for separating overlapped fingerprints either rely on rule-based orientation field completion that requires strong domain knowledge or train end-to-end deep neural networks that do not account for domain-specific considerations. This work introduces a diffusion-based pipeline for separating component fingerprints from an image containing overlapping friction ridge patterns. We formulate the separation problem as an inpainting task and progressively learn a diffusion model for this task in multiple stages. Starting from a pre-trained Stable Diffusion model, we progressively incorporate a fingerprint prior, add the ability to complete partial fingerprints, and finally propose \textbf{overlap-aware inpainting} that reconstructs each component print using a diffusion inpainting model based on multi-channel conditioning. Experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that component fingerprints reconstructed using the proposed diffusion-based inpainting method can match with their mated counterparts with very high probability.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-Camera Trajectory Forecasting with Trajectory Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2108.04694)
*Olly Styles,Tanaya Guha,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多摄像头轨迹预测（MCTF）问题，通过"Which-When-Where"框架同时利用多个视角的物体相对位置，预测物体在全部视角中的未来出现位置、时间和地点。提出了轨迹张量（trajectory tensors）技术来跨多视角编码轨迹及其不确定性，并构建了包含600小时视频、15个摄像头视角的数据集进行实验验证，结果表明轨迹张量模型优于坐标轨迹模型及适配后的SCTF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法大多聚焦于单摄像头轨迹预测（SCTF），无法满足监控、交通监控等广泛使用的多摄像头场景需求；同时单摄像头视野受限，难以实现长期轨迹预测。因此需要一种能同时利用多个视角信息进行轨迹预测的框架。

Method: 提出MCTF框架，采用"Which-When-Where"策略，预测物体在哪些摄像头出现、何时出现以及出现在视角内的具体位置。核心创新是轨迹张量（trajectory tensors），一种跨多个摄像头视图编码轨迹及其不确定性的新技术；基于此开发了多种编码器-解码器MCTF模型，并构建了专门用于MCTF任务的自有数据集（600小时视频、15个摄像头视角）。

Result: 轨迹张量模型在所有实验中均优于基于坐标轨迹的MCTF模型，以及现有的SCTF方法适配到MCTF任务的表现。

Conclusion: 轨迹张量是解决多摄像头轨迹预测问题的有效编码方式，MCTF框架弥补了传统SCTF在多摄像头场景中的不足，为监控和交通管理等应用提供了更实用的长期轨迹预测方案。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of multi-camera trajectory forecasting (MCTF), which involves predicting the trajectory of a moving object across a network of cameras. While multi-camera setups are widespread for applications such as surveillance and traffic monitoring, existing trajectory forecasting methods typically focus on single-camera trajectory forecasting (SCTF), limiting their use for such applications. Furthermore, using a single camera limits the field-of-view available, making long-term trajectory forecasting impossible. We address these shortcomings of SCTF by developing an MCTF framework that simultaneously uses all estimated relative object locations from several viewpoints and predicts the object's future location in all possible viewpoints. Our framework follows a Which-When-Where approach that predicts in which camera(s) the objects appear and when and where within the camera views they appear. To this end, we propose the concept of trajectory tensors: a new technique to encode trajectories across multiple camera views and the associated uncertainties. We develop several encoder-decoder MCTF models for trajectory tensors and present extensive experiments on our own database (comprising 600 hours of video data from 15 camera views) created particularly for the MCTF task. Results show that our trajectory tensor models outperform coordinate trajectory-based MCTF models and existing SCTF methods adapted for MCTF. Code is available from: https://github.com/olly-styles/Trajectory-Tensors

</details>


### [120] [UniWorld-Design: From Pixel Generation to Layer-Native Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03971)
*Zongjian Li,Zhiyuan Yan,Chenxu Bai,Chen Chen,Haoxiang Sun,Shaodong Wang,Feize Wu,Shenghai Yuan,Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Yuwei Niu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了UniWorld-Design框架，将图像生成从平面像素合成重构为结构化视觉组合，以语义RGBA图层作为生成、理解和编辑的原子单元。该框架包含Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA)和Image-to-Layer (I2L)两个模型，分别实现从文本直接生成独立RGBA素材，以及从完整图像中联合生成有序、完整的语义RGBA图层。在Crello基准上，I2L相比Qwen-Image-Layered在逐层RGB L1误差上降低37%，Alpha Soft IoU提升34%；T2RGBA在CLIP Score上超越LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心洞察在于：像素定义图像如何被渲染，而图层定义图像如何被创建、理解和编辑。正如人类设计师通过图层而非原始像素来创建和操控视觉内容，现有生成模型缺乏图层原生的设计空间，因此需要为多模态生成模型配备图层原生的设计空间。

Method: UniWorld-Design包含两个模型：T2RGBA模型直接从文本生成独立的RGBA素材；I2L模型以完整图像、全局指令和逐层提示为条件，联合生成有序且完整的语义RGBA图层。I2L的指令接口支持顶层分解、递归分解和定向提取，使分层成为可指令寻址的编辑操作。由于I2L学习的是完整语义对象而非可见像素分区，其图层在移动或移除后仍可保持可用。

Result: 在Crello基准上，I2L将逐层RGB L1误差降低37%，Alpha Soft IoU相对Qwen-Image-Layered提升34%。T2RGBA达到最高的CLIP Score，超越LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。

Conclusion: 将语义RGBA图层作为生成、理解和编辑的原子单元能够有效重新定义图像生成范式，使多模态生成模型具备类似人类设计师的图层原生工作流，并在分解质量和文本生成素材质量上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: We introduce UniWorld-Design, a framework that redefines image generation from flat pixel synthesis to structured visual composition, with semantic RGBA layers as the atomic units of generation, understanding, and editing. Our key insight is that pixels define how an image is rendered, whereas layers define how an image is created, understood, and edited. Just as human designers create and manipulate visual content through layers rather than raw pixels, UniWorld-Design equips multimodal generative models with a layer-native design space. UniWorld-Design comprises two models. The Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA) model generates standalone RGBA assets directly from text. The Image-to-Layer (I2L) model conditions on a finished image, a global instruction and per-layer prompts, and jointly produces ordered, complete semantic RGBA layers. Its instruction interface supports top-level decomposition, recursive decomposition and targeted extraction, making layering an instruction-addressable operation for agentic editing. Because I2L learns complete semantic objects rather than visible-pixel partitions, its layers stay usable when moved or removed. On the Crello benchmark, I2L reduces per-layer RGB L1 error by 37% and achieves a 34% relative improvement in Alpha Soft IoU over Qwen-Image-Layered. Separately, T2RGBA achieves the highest CLIP Score, outperforming LayerDiffuse and OmniAlpha.

</details>


### [121] [CLIP-EBC: CLIP Can Count Accurately through Enhanced Blockwise Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09281)
*Yiming Ma,Victor Sanchez,Tanaya Guha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIP-EBC，这是首个完全基于CLIP模型的精准人群密度估计方法。通过引入增强块状分类（EBC）框架，采用整数值分箱和基于密度图的回归损失，解决了现有分类式计数框架中分箱边界标签模糊和仅关注分类误差的问题，在多个数据集上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型在图像分类等识别任务上表现出色，但由于计数本质上是回归问题而非识别任务，将CLIP用于人群计数面临挑战。现有的基于分类的人群计数框架存在两个主要问题：计数值的量化采用邻近实值分箱导致边界附近标签模糊，以及仅关注分类损失无法准确预测计数值。因此直接应用CLIP在这些框架中会导致性能不佳，需要新的框架来充分发挥CLIP的识别能力。

Method: 首先提出增强块状分类（EBC）框架，采用整数值分箱（而非实值分箱）以减少分箱边界附近的标签模糊；同时引入基于密度图的回归损失以提升计数值预测精度。EBC框架是骨干网络无关的。在此基础上，将CLIP作为骨干网络集成到EBC框架中，提出CLIP-EBC，充分利用CLIP的识别能力完成人群密度估计任务。

Result: EBC框架在UCF-QNRF数据集上可将现有分类式方法最多提升44.5%。CLIP-EBC在NWPU-Crowd测试集上取得最先进性能，MAE为58.2，RMSE为268.5，分别比此前最优方法STEERER提升8.6%和13.3%。

Conclusion: EBC框架通过整数分箱和密度图回归损失有效解决了分类式人群计数中的标签模糊和预测不准确问题；CLIP-EBC证明了CLIP模型在计数任务（虽然本质是回归问题）上通过合适的框架设计也能发挥强大作用，达到最先进水平。

Abstract: We propose CLIP-EBC, the first fully CLIP-based model for accurate crowd density estimation. While the CLIP model has demonstrated remarkable success in addressing recognition tasks such as zero-shot image classification, its potential for counting has been largely unexplored due to the inherent challenges in transforming a regression problem, such as counting, into a recognition task. In this work, we investigate and enhance CLIP's ability to count, focusing specifically on the task of estimating crowd sizes from images. Existing classification-based crowd-counting frameworks have significant limitations, including the quantization of count values into bordering real-valued bins and the sole focus on classification errors. These practices result in label ambiguity near the shared borders and inaccurate prediction of count values. Hence, directly applying CLIP within these frameworks may yield suboptimal performance.
  To address these challenges, we first propose the Enhanced Blockwise Classification (EBC) framework. Unlike previous methods, EBC utilizes integer-valued bins, effectively reducing ambiguity near bin boundaries. Additionally, it incorporates a regression loss based on density maps to improve the prediction of count values. Within our backbone-agnostic EBC framework, we then introduce CLIP-EBC to fully leverage CLIP's recognition capabilities for this task. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of EBC and the competitive performance of CLIP-EBC. Specifically, our EBC framework can improve existing classification-based methods by up to 44.5% on the UCF-QNRF dataset, and CLIP-EBC achieves state-of-the-art performance on the NWPU-Crowd test set, with an MAE of 58.2 and an RMSE of 268.5, representing improvements of 8.6% and 13.3% over the previous best method, STEERER. The code and weights are available at https://github.com/Yiming-M/CLIP-EBC.

</details>


### [122] [JoyAI-Video-Edit: Real-Time Open-Ended Video Editing with Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03974)
*Yicheng Xiao,Wenxun Dai,Xinran Qin,Lin Song,Maoquan Zhang,Hang Xu,Yukang Chen,Yitong Li,Guohui Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Xuying Zhang,Tommy Zhang,Jianlong Yuan,Peihao Li,Shuai Lu,Siming Fu,Chuyang Zhao,Xin Han,Jie Huang,Wenbo Li,Guoqing Ma,Wei Huang,Xiaojuan Qi,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoyAI-Video-Edit是一个16B参数的自回归扩散框架，用于实时开放视频编辑，通过分块自回归适配、源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（SA-DMD）和长视界自回归蒸馏技术，在无需未来帧和预定义视频长度的情况下，实现了单张Nvidia B200 GPU上720p视频约30 FPS的端到端实时编辑，性能显著优于现有流式编辑器并与强离线系统相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频编辑需要低延迟的因果生成和有限计算资源下的源保真度与长期时间一致性，而现有方法在流式编辑场景中存在训练-推理不匹配、两步生成时源信息丢失以及时间漂移累积等问题。

Method: 采用16B参数的自回归扩散框架，结合三种关键技术：1) 分块自回归适配（chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation）实现无未来帧的因果生成；2) 源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（SA-DMD）在两步生成过程中保持源保真度；3) 长视界自回归蒸馏（Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation）缓解累积时间漂移并减少训练-推理不匹配。

Result: 广泛的自动评估和人工评估表明，JoyAI-Video-Edit在短视频和长视频上均大幅优于现有流式编辑系统，并与强离线系统具有竞争力。完整系统在单张Nvidia B200 GPU上实现720p约30 FPS的端到端视频编辑。

Conclusion: JoyAI-Video-Edit通过自回归扩散架构与蒸馏策略的组合，有效解决了实时视频编辑中源保真与时间一致性的核心挑战，在实时性和质量之间取得了领先的权衡，代码已开源发布。

Abstract: Real-time video editing requires low-latency causal generation with bounded computational resources while preserving source fidelity and long-term temporal consistency. We present JoyAI-Video-Edit, a 16B-parameter autoregressive diffusion framework for real-time, open-ended video editing without access to future frames or a predefined video duration. Our method combines chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation, Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation (SA-DMD), and Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation to reduce train--inference mismatch, preserve source fidelity during two-step generation, and mitigate accumulated temporal drift. Extensive automatic and human evaluations show that JoyAI-Video-Edit substantially outperforms existing streaming editors and remains competitive with strong offline systems on both short and long videos. The complete system achieves end-to-end 720p video editing at approximately 30 FPS on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-opensource/JoyAI-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [123] [Video-DeepResearch: Towards the Next-Generation Multimodal Deepresearch Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03979)
*Zhen Fang,Yu Zeng,Wenxuan Huang,Yiming Zhao,Shiting Huang,Tianfei Ren,Qi Lu,Qingnan Ren,Qisheng Su,Lionel Z. Wang,Qingyu Yin,Shuang Chen,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Yao Hu,Shaohui Lin,Wanli Ouyang,Shaosheng Cao,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-DeepResearch（Video-DR），将多模态智能体从静态图像扩展到连续视频流，解决视频理解中需要密集时空定位与开放网络探索相结合的任务。通过解耦感知-探索流水线和阶段式工具解锁，配合SFT+GRPO两阶段训练，在自建的Video-DR-Bench基准上达到64.0%的平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet、GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态智能体在处理连续视频流时存在两个关键瓶颈：(1) 模态偏差——智能体倾向于绕过视觉工具而依赖文本搜索；(2) 参数化知识泄漏——模型依赖内部记忆而非真实的工具增强执行。这些问题阻碍了智能体在需要密集时空定位与开放网络探索相结合的任务中发挥真正能力。

Method: 提出Video-DR框架，核心包括：(1) 解耦的感知-探索流水线，配合阶段式工具解锁，强制在网页检索之前进行穷尽式跨帧视觉定位；(2) 两阶段训练流程：监督微调（SFT）后接组相对策略优化（GRPO），实现自主探索并突破模仿学习上限；(3) 构建Video-DR-Bench基准，包含200个复杂多跳VQA实例，采用人类-AI协作方式标注。

Result: Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B在基准上取得64.0%的平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet（59.0%）5.0个百分点，显著优于GPT-5（52.5%）和Gemini 2.5 Pro（57.5%）。30B-A3B变体达到59.3%，与Claude-4.5-Sonnet相当，证明了训练范式在小规模模型上的有效性。

Conclusion: Video-DR通过解耦感知-探索架构和GRPO强化学习训练，有效克服了模态偏差和参数化知识泄漏问题，在视频深度研究任务上实现了新的最优性能，且训练范式在紧凑模型规模下仍保持竞争力。代码已开源。

Abstract: We introduce Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR), extending multimodal agents from static images to continuous video streams, a setting that demands dense spatiotemporal grounding coupled with open-web exploration. Preliminary evaluations reveal two critical bottlenecks in current models: (1) modality bias, where agents bypass visual tools in favor of textual search, and (2) parametric knowledge leakage, where models rely on internal memory rather than genuine tool-augmented execution. To address these challenges, we propose Video-DR, featuring a decoupled perception-exploration pipeline with stage-wise tool unlocking that compels exhaustive cross-frame visual grounding prior to web retrieval. Our framework adopts a two-stage training recipe: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), enabling autonomous exploration that breaks the imitation-learning ceiling. Furthermore, we curate Video-DR-Bench, a human-AI collaborative benchmark comprising 200 complex, multi-hop VQA instances. Empirical results demonstrate that our Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B establishes a new state-of-the-art of 64.0% average accuracy, surpassing proprietary Claude-4.5-Sonnet (59.0%) by 5.0 points and significantly outperforming GPT-5 (52.5%) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (57.5%). The 30B-A3B variant achieves 59.3%, competitive with Claude-4.5-Sonnet and demonstrating the effectiveness of our training paradigm even at compact scale. Code: https://github.com/Osilly/Vision-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [124] [Perceptual Anchoring: Prototype-Guided Text Calibration for Training-free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03991)
*Wanli Ma,Jiangwen Lu,Qinmu Peng,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PTC（原型引导文本校准）的训练无关开放词汇语义分割方法。该方法受符号-感知对应关系启发，通过视觉原型校准文本嵌入，缩小语义鸿沟，无需额外训练即可作为即插即用模块提升现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练无关开放词汇语义分割方法通常仅关注改进视觉表示，而将编码通用类别概念的文本嵌入视为固定分类参考。这种通用概念与捕捉目标实例具体外观的视觉表示之间存在语义鸿沟，导致分割不完整和误预测。

Method: PTC包含两个阶段：（1）感知阶段：基于初始匹配分数选择可靠的视觉证据，构建类别特定的视觉原型；（2）锚定阶段：利用视觉原型校准对应的文本嵌入，校准强度根据视觉证据量自适应调整。PTC无需额外训练或外部模型，可作为即插即用模块。

Result: 在八个基准上的大量实验表明，PTC显著提升了六种代表性方法的性能，产生更完整、更准确的分割结果。

Conclusion: PTC是一种简单有效的改善视觉-文本对齐的方法，通过原型引导校准文本嵌入，在保持开放词汇泛化能力的同时实现更精确的实例级语义对应。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) partitions an image into semantically distinct regions based on arbitrary text descriptions, without learning any additional parameters. However, existing methods typically focus on improving visual representations while treating text embeddings that encode only generic category concepts as fixed classification references. The resulting semantic gap between these generic concepts and the visual representations that capture the specific appearances of target instances often causes incomplete masks and erroneous predictions in non-target regions. Inspired by the symbol-percept correspondence underlying perceptual anchoring, we propose Prototype-Guided Text Calibration (PTC) for training-free OVSS. In the Perceiving stage, PTC selects reliable visual evidence based on initial matching scores to construct category-specific visual prototypes. In the Anchoring stage, PTC uses these prototypes to calibrate their corresponding text embeddings, with the calibration strength adaptively adjusted based on the amount of visual evidence. Consequently, the calibrated text embeddings align more accurately with instance-specific visual representations while preserving generic category semantics and open-vocabulary generalization. Moreover, PTC requires neither additional training nor external models and can serve as a plug-and-play module for existing methods. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks show that PTC significantly enhances the performance of six representative methods and yields more complete and accurate segmentation results. These results validate PTC as a simple and effective approach to improving visual-text alignment.

</details>


### [125] [ParVL: Parallel Scaling and Expandable Compute Allocation for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04010)
*Yang Yang,Qinyu Zhao,Mouxiang Chen,Xiaohui Li,Lixin Gu,Wenhai Wang,Hongjie Zhang,Wenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ParVL（Parallel Vision-Language）扩展框架，通过复用现有ViT和LLM主干参数，在多个视觉和语言分支间并行分配计算，从而在不增加额外模型参数和推理延迟的前提下，实现MLLMs中视觉与语言计算分配的灵活优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM扩展策略要么扩展模型参数，要么扩展顺序推理计算，导致巨大的内存或延迟开销；更重要的是，这些方法无法改变ViT与LLM之间固定的计算分配，限制了针对特定任务的优化能力。

Method: ParVL框架通过复用既有ViT和LLM主干参数，在多个视觉与语言分支间扩展并行计算；每个并行计算流使用分支特定的前缀参数实例化到共享主干上，并在约130亿token上通过全参数监督微调进行端到端训练，系统研究视觉编码器与LLM解码器之间的计算分配权衡。

Result: ParVL在整体多模态性能上优于相同训练配方的单分支基线，且最优的视觉-语言计算分配比例随任务不同而变化。

Conclusion: 固定主干参数预算下，视觉与语言模态之间的共享主干计算分配存在最优权衡，且该最优分配因任务而异，ParVL框架实现了这种灵活分配并提升了多模态性能。

Abstract: Existing scaling strategies for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically expand either model parameters or sequential inference computation, incurring substantial memory or latency overhead. More importantly, most existing methods fail to alter the rigid, fixed computation allocation between the Vision Transformer and the Large Language Model components, limiting task-specific optimization. To address this, we introduce the Parallel Vision-Language (ParVL) scaling framework for MLLMs, which scales parallel computation by reusing the existing ViT and LLM backbone parameters across multiple vision and language branches. This framework raises a central question: given a fixed backbone parameter budget, how should additional shared-backbone computation be allocated between the vision and language modalities? We instantiate each parallel computational stream with branch-specific prefix parameters over a shared backbone, and train the entire model end-to-end via full-parameter supervised fine-tuning on roughly 13B tokens. We systematically study the computation-allocation trade-off between the ViT encoder and LLM decoder. ParVL improves overall multimodal performance over same-recipe single-branch baselines, and the best evaluated vision--language allocation varies across tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YangYangGirl/ParVL.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [ISEE: Interactive Semantic Enrichment for Database Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02604)
*Yuan Tian,Yiru Chen,Rakesh R. Menon,Zifan Liu,Ting Cai,Fei Wu,Anudeep Chimakurthi,Prashanthi Ramamurthy,Sridevi Aishwariya Ganesan,Kun Qian,Yunyao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出交互式语义增强系统（ISEE），通过对数据字段描述进行质量评分、收集领域知识并与用户协作丰富语义，以弥补LLM智能体在数据任务中因字段语义模糊或不完整导致的性能瓶颈。实验证明该系统能降低认知负荷、提升描述质量和下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在数据相关任务（如感知、探索、检索）中的性能严重依赖数据语义的清晰度和完整性。然而实际应用中许多字段描述模糊或不完整，因为大量关键上下文（如自定义字段含义）来源于用户的领域知识且很少被公开记录，这限制了智能体在下游任务（如实体链接）中的表现。

Method: 提出ISEE系统，包含三个核心模块：（1）质量评分系统——度量给定数据字段描述的语义质量；（2）领域知识收集模块——自动获取相关领域知识；（3）协作式语义增强模块——与用户互动协同完善字段语义。通过用户研究、自动化用户模拟、定量评估和案例研究进行综合验证。

Result: 实验结果显示ISEE能显著降低用户认知负荷、提高字段描述质量，并增强下游任务（如实体链接）的性能。

Conclusion: ISEE通过交互式语义增强有效弥合了数据字段上下文缺失的鸿沟，为LLM智能体在数据任务中提供更可靠的语义基础，具有实用价值。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly being deployed for data-related tasks, including data sense-making, exploration, and retrieval. However, their performance heavily depends on the clarity and completeness of data semantics. In practice, many field descriptions remain ambiguous or incomplete, as much of the essential context (e.g., the meaning of a customized field) originates from users' domain knowledge and is rarely documented publicly. This gap restricts the agents' task performance in downstream tasks, such as entity-linking. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel and comprehensive Interactive SEmantic Enrichment system (ISEE). Given a data field description, ISEE measures its quality through a scoring system, gathers domain knowledge, and collaboratively enriches the semantics with users. Through a user study, automated user simulation, quantitative evaluation, and case study, we demonstrate that ISEE significantly reduces cognitive load, improves description quality, and enhances downstream task performance.

</details>


### [127] [Self-Organising Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02606)
*Marcello Barylli,Gabriel Béna,Alexander Mordvintsev,Eleni Nisioti,Sebastian Risi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出自组织数字电路（Self-Organising Digital Circuits）框架，将功能逻辑生成与维护建模为图上的元学习问题，采用拓扑掩码Transformer配置电路中布尔门的查找表（LUT），受神经细胞自动机（NCA）模式生成范式启发，能在退化布尔搜索空间中导航以满足计算任务。该架构可从零开始自组装功能电路，并能围绕永久性未见过硬件故障快速重新布线。对软错误，策略在远超训练条件的损伤规模下实现近完美恢复（>99.99%准确率）。还观察到跨电路规模的泛化：在比训练时宽得多的图上准确率有所提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经典计算的容错依赖于静态策略（如硬件冗余和纠错码），而生物系统展现出适应性可塑性，能通过围绕损伤的动态重组来维持功能。作者受这一生物自组织原则启发，旨在将这种动态自修复能力引入数字硬件领域。

Method: 采用拓扑掩码Transformer架构，将功能逻辑生成形式化为图上的元学习问题。核心思路是扩展神经细胞自动机（NCA）的模式生成范式：不再仅再生固定的目标状态，而是在退化的布尔搜索空间中导航以满足具体计算任务。Transformer负责配置电路布尔门的查找表（LUT）。

Result: 实验表明：该架构能够从零开始自组装功能电路；面对永久性、未见过的硬件故障能快速重新布线逻辑；对软错误，在损伤规模远超过训练条件的情况下实现>99.99%的近完美恢复准确率；同时表现出跨电路尺度的泛化能力，在比训练时更宽的电路图上准确率有所提升。

Conclusion: 该工作将生物自组织原则与数字硬件实践领域相连接，展示了一种新型的自修复数字电路范式，其适应性故障恢复能力超越传统静态容错策略，并具备良好的泛化扩展潜力。

Abstract: Fault tolerance in classical computing has traditionally relied on static strategies like hardware redundancy and error-correcting codes. Biological systems, in contrast, exhibit adaptive plasticity, maintaining function through dynamic re-organisation around damage. Inspired by this principle, we introduce Self-Organising Digital Circuits, framing functional logic generation and maintenance as a meta-learning problem on graphs. Our architecture employs a topology-masked Transformer that configures the Lookup Tables (LUT) of a circuit's Boolean gates. Extending the pattern-generation paradigm of Neural Cellular Automata (NCA), it navigates the degenerate Boolean search space to satisfy a computational task, rather than regenerating a fixed target state. We demonstrate that it can self-assemble functional circuits from scratch and rapidly re-route logic around permanent, previously unseen hardware faults. For soft errors, the policy achieves near-perfect recovery (>99.99\% accuracy) from damage sizes far exceeding training conditions. We further observe generalisation across circuit scales: accuracy improves on graphs substantially wider than those seen during training. This work bridges the principles of biological self-organisation with the practical domain of digital hardware.

</details>


### [128] [Beyond the Hivemind: Escaping LLM Homogeneity via Meta-Persona Anchoring and Sequential Temperature Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02618)
*Tairan Fu,Javier Conde,Carlos Arriaga,Gonzalo Martínez,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Coronado-Blázquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Meta-Persona Anchoring + Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS)"的两阶段框架，用于缓解大语言模型中的"Artificial Hivemind"效应（即模型趋向于窄化、同质化共识的现象）。该方法通过让模型自我选择独特人格锚定生成起点，并用Top-p过滤保持语法有效性后再进行极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）来探索更广的概率分布，在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上将平均余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，显著提升回答多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型存在"Artificial Hivemind"效应，即使在高温度采样下，模型也会收敛到窄化、同质化的共识，导致高语义相似度（约0.80-0.90），限制了AI输出的多样性和创造性。

Method: 两阶段生成框架：(1) Meta-Persona Anchoring——提示模型自我选择独特的个性化人格来锚定生成起点；(2) Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS)——双重采样筛，先通过Top-p过滤保证语法有效性，再对幸存候选应用极端温度缩放（T≥4.0）以探索加宽的概率分布。

Result: 在INFINITY-CHAT数据集上与约20B参数的开源模型上评估，平均成对余弦相似度从约0.85降至约0.65，大多数问题低于0.7阈值，显著缩小了人工模式坍缩与人类类型多样性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能有效减少LLM的语义收敛，提升生成多样性，并以开源形式提供实现，为更多样化和更具创造性的AI部署提供支持。

Abstract: Recent studies have identified an ``Artificial Hivemind'' effect in Large Language Models (LLMs) causing models to converge on a narrow, homogenized consensus even for open questions. This semantic collapse limits the diversity of AI, resulting in high inter-response similarity ($\approx 0.80-0.90$) even under high-temperature sampling. In this paper, we propose a novel mitigation framework to increase diversity: Meta-Persona Anchoring combined with Filtered Temperature Scaling (FTS). Our approach utilizes a two-stage generation process: first, the model is prompted to self-select a unique, idiosyncratic persona to anchor its starting point; second, we apply a dual-stage sampling sieve, utilizing Top-$p$ filtering to preserve grammatical validity followed by extreme temperature scaling ($T \ge 4.0$) on the surviving candidates to explore the broadened probability distribution. We evaluate our method using the INFINITY-CHAT dataset on state-of-the-art open weight models under $\sim$20B parameters. Our results demonstrate a significant reduction in semantic convergence, with average pairwise cosine similarity dropping from ($\approx 0.85$) to ($\approx 0.65$). Our scheme achieves a majority of questions below the 0.7 threshold, effectively reducing the gap between artificial mode collapse and human-level typological diversity. We provide our implementation as an open-source framework to enable more diverse and creative AI deployments.

</details>


### [129] [PULSE: An Executable Contract Language for Spatiotemporal Knowledge Graph Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02630)
*Dongxu Yang,Ziyi Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PULSE，一种受Object-Process-Methodology启发的语言，在单一类型化运行时中本地化知识图谱工程中的四种操作角色及其写入效应，并给出了效果约束引理、六项安全性质以及大量测试验证证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱工程中，可接受状态、观测、约束、过程和假设场景常分布在多个工件中，其组合执行契约是外部的，缺乏统一的运行时约束保证。

Method: 设计PULSE语言，将四种操作角色（模式）及写入效应本地化于一个类型化运行时。核心操作包括证据非覆盖、分支隔离、多主体计时器、受保护状态变更以及按声明排序的事件排序。采用核心演算证明效果约束引理和六项安全属性，并用Lean 4验证内核类比（位置、证据、时钟、监视器、原子性、分支源保留）。评估包括88个测试、3534个边界检查和32个Lean/Python运行时-内核案例。

Result: 标准组合和Sismic状态图的独立复现均成功再现测试的冷链轨迹；在37,440条生成的时序轨迹上匹配独立工作流并区分十个单字段变异体；在NOAA IBTrACS自1980年子集上与GEOS和事件扫描在1,476,290个过渡-区域对上一致，含4,800个采样和12,831个限时事件；项目特定GeoSPARQL探针测量了接口覆盖率。

Conclusion: 结果表明PULSE支持契约本地化、安全性论证以及所测片段的轨迹一致性；但语言优越性和可用性不在评估范围内。

Abstract: Knowledge graph engineering often distributes accepted state, observations, constraints, processes, and hypothetical scenarios across artifacts whose combined execution contract remains external. We present PULSE, an Object-Process-Methodology-inspired language that localizes four operational roles and their write effects in one typed runtime. Here, modes denote operational roles rather than modal or deontic logic. The implemented contract fixes evidence non-overwrite, branch isolation, grounded multi-subject timers, guarded state change, and declaration-ranked event ordering over time and space; an external runner still decides whether evidence becomes an authoritative move. GeoSPARQL, SOSA, and SHACL remain generated views. A core calculus gives an effect-confinement lemma and six safety properties. Lean 4 checks kernel analogues for positions, evidence, clocks, monitors, atomicity, and branch source retention; 88 tests, 3,534 bounded checks, and 32 Lean/Python runtime-kernel cases bound the implementation claim to the checked cases. First-author implementations of a standards composition and a separate Sismic statechart reproduce the tested cold-chain trace. Across 37,440 generated temporal traces, PULSE matches a separate workflow and distinguishes ten single-field mutants. On the complete NOAA IBTrACS since1980 subset it agrees with GEOS and an event sweep on 1,476,290 transition-zone pairs, including 4,800 sampled and 12,831 duration-qualified events. Project-specific GeoSPARQL probes measure interface coverage. Overall, the results support contract localization, safety arguments, and trace parity for the tested fragment; language superiority and usability remain outside the evaluation.

</details>


### [130] [HyperAgent: Planning and Acting over Tool-Schema Hypergraphs for Tool-Use LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02650)
*Zian Zhai,Xingyu Tan,Gaowang Zou,Xiaoyang Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperAgent框架，通过构建工具-模式超图（Tool-Schema Hypergraph）来建模工具间的输入输出关系，在任务规划与执行阶段分别生成模式感知的任务DAG和状态条件的工具支持图，从而提升LLM智能体的工具使用规划能力。实验表明在AppWorld基准上优于现有智能体基线，减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部工具完成复杂任务，但现有的工具使用智能体通常仅依赖LLM从文本描述中推断工具组合，存在隐式推理局限性和对真实世界动态执行环境的适应性不足，导致在复杂任务中探索效率低、执行不可靠。

Method: 1) 在模式（schema）级别建模工具关系，构建有向工具-模式超图，工具作为从输入模式节点指向输出模式节点的超边；2) 提出HyperAgent框架，首先提取与任务相关的工具上下文图，用于指导构建模式感知的任务DAG；3) 执行阶段通过赤字导向扩展（deficit-oriented expansion）动态构建状态条件的工具支持图，识别未解决的依赖需求并检索支持的生产者工具。

Result: 在AppWorld基准上的实验表明，HyperAgent提升了任务完成性能，同时减少了冗余API调用、LLM交互次数和token消耗。

Conclusion: HyperAgent通过在模式层面显式建模工具关系，有效克服了隐式推理的局限，实现了更高效、更可靠的工具使用规划与动态执行，为LLM智能体的工具推理提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external tools to complete complex real-world tasks. However, reliable tool-use planning remains challenging due to the limitations of implicit reasoning and the evolving nature of real-world execution environments. Existing tool-use agents typically rely on LLMs to infer tool compositions from textual descriptions, which can lead to inefficient exploration and unreliable execution in complex tasks. To address these challenges, we model tool relations at the schema level and construct a directed Tool--Schema Hypergraph, in which tools are represented as hyperedges from their required input-schema nodes to their output-schema nodes. Furthermore, we propose HyperAgent, a Tool--Schema Hypergraph-guided framework for dynamic planning and execution. Given a task, HyperAgent first extracts a task-relevant tool context graph and uses it to guide the construction of a schema-aware Task DAG. During execution, HyperAgent dynamically realizes each subtask by constructing a state-conditioned tool support graph through deficit-oriented expansion, which identifies unresolved requirements and retrieves supporting producer tools according to the current agent state. Experiments on AppWorld demonstrate that HyperAgent improves task completion performance while reducing redundant API calls, LLM interactions, and token consumption compared with existing agent baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Explainable AI for the EU Right to Explanation: A Systematic Review of the Law-XAI Translation Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02699)
*Benjamin Fresz,Elena Dubovitskaya,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了可解释人工智能（XAI）在欧盟"解释权"（Right to Explanation）语境下的研究现状，聚焦GDPR第15(1)(h)条、AI法案第86条等相关法规，从2643篇文献中筛选出57篇全文、其中仅19篇真正实现了法律与技术视角的跨学科整合，揭示了三类普遍问题（误用GDPR法律依据、忽视CJEU Dun & Bradstreet判例、混淆解释形式与内容），并提出"Addressee/Purpose Framework"框架、四阶段操作化蓝图和六个开放研究问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法的自动决策（贷款、招聘、医疗等）直接影响个人权益，欧盟法律赋予个人"解释权"，但可解释AI（XAI）在实践中能否满足该法律权利尚不明确。作者希望通过系统文献综述澄清XAI与欧盟法律解释权之间的关系，为合规实践提供理论指导和可操作路径。

Method: 采用系统文献综述（SLR）方法，涵盖2024年7月AI法案最终版公布后的文献（因第86条为后期新增条款）。通过宽泛检索识别2643条初始记录，筛选出57篇全文精读，其中仅19篇在实质性层面实现了法律与技术视角的跨学科整合，并以法律条款（GDPR Art. 15(1)(h)、AIA Art. 86）为核心分析框架进行归类。

Result: 发现三个普遍性模式问题：(1)多数文献误认了GDPR的法律依据；(2)极少有文献涉及欧洲法院（CJEU）Dun & Bradstreet判例（可能受发表时间限制）；(3)解释形式（受解释接受者管辖）与解释内容（受法律目的管辖）的区分常被混淆。由此提出Addressee/Purpose框架、四阶段操作化蓝图及六个开放研究问题。

Conclusion: 如果不对上述系统性缺口加以解决，"解释权"将面临沦为形式化法律义务而缺乏技术上可实现合规路径的风险。作者呼吁加强法律与技术学科的深度交叉研究，以真正实现欧盟法规赋予个体的解释权利。

Abstract: When algorithms make or influence consequential decisions---about loan eligibility, hiring, or healthcare---EU law grants affected individuals a Right to Explanation. Yet whether (and how) Explainable AI (XAI) can satisfy this right in practice remains poorly understood, with direct implications for individuals' ability to contest automated decisions that affect their lives. This paper presents a systematic literature review of XAI in the context of the EU Right to Explanation, with particular focus on Art. 15(1)(h) GDPR, Art. 86 AI Act (AIA), and related instruments. We consider papers published from 2024 onwards, as the final version of the AIA was published in July 2024---with Art. 86 being added late. From 2643 initial records identified by a deliberately broad search, we review 57 full texts, of which only 19 papers demonstrate substantive integration of both legal and technical perspectives, showing gaps in the interdisciplinary synthesis of the current regulatory framework. We document three problematic patterns across the corpus: Most misidentify the GDPR legal basis; few engage with the CJEU's Dun & Bradstreet judgment (likely due to publication timing); and the distinction between explanation form (governed by addressee) and content (governed by legal purpose) is often conflated. We conceptualize this as the Addressee/Purpose Framework, propose a four-phase blueprint for operationalization, and identify six concrete open research questions. Without further progress, the Right to Explanation risks remaining a formal obligation without a technically realizable path to compliance.

</details>


### [132] [Predictive Set Theory: A Generative Framework for Cognitive Architecture with Operationalized Core Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02704)
*Yiyang Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出预测集合论（PST），一个从第一性原理重建认知架构的形式化生成框架，以最小操作集（传感器、集合状态刷新、三种参考链）为基础推导核心认知功能，并解决罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性认知地位等经典问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测加工理论缺乏对"预测"结构、预测误差标准化响应及跨更新一致性的操作性定义；贝叶斯认知科学预设封闭假设空间，无法解释概率分布对象如何成为离散可识别指称。两种主流范式均存在根本性缺陷，需要一个从第一性原理出发的形式化生成框架。

Method: PST将认知锚定在最小操作集上：（1）传感器形式化为恒等函数；（2）集合论状态刷新；（3）三种基本参考链形式——参考、反参考和半参考。由此严格推导出状态序列、需求、比较、效率及有限时域概率规划等核心认知功能。该框架是设计规范而非神经机制模型，适用于任何在不完整信息和不可逆风险下需要保持内部一致性的系统。

Result: PST为经典问题提供了新颖解答，包括罗素悖论、哥德尔不完备性的认知地位、负反馈的奠基以及电影剪辑的理解。论文的核心目的通过公开学术记录确立PST框架的原创性与完备性。

Conclusion: PST作为一个形式化生成框架，以更具操作性的方式统一了预测加工与贝叶斯认知的不足，为认知架构提供了从底层操作到高层功能的设计规范，并通过解决多个经典难题验证了其理论效力。

Abstract: Predictive processing theories portray the brain as a hierarchical prediction engine that minimizes prediction error, yet they lack operational definitions for the structure of a "prediction," the standardized response to a prediction error, and the mechanism that maintains consistency across successive updates. Bayesian cognitive science attempts to subsume all uncertainty under probabilistic belief updating, but it presupposes a closed hypothesis space and provides no generative account of how the objects over which probabilities are distributed become discrete, identifiable referents in the first place. This paper introduces Predictive Set Theory (PST), a formal generative framework that reconstructs cognitive architecture from first principles. PST anchors cognition in a minimal set of operations---a sensor formalized as an identity function, set-theoretic state refresh, and three fundamental forms of reference chains (reference, counter-reference, and semi-reference)---and rigorously derives core cognitive functions including state sequences, demand, comparison, efficiency, and finite-horizon probabilistic planning. Rather than modeling neural mechanisms, PST constitutes a design specification for any system that must maintain internal consistency while acting under incomplete information and irreversible risk. The framework offers novel resolutions to classical problems such as Russell's paradox, the cognitive status of Gödelian incompleteness, the grounding of negative feedback, and the comprehension of film editing. The primary purpose of this paper is to establish, through the public academic record, the originality and completeness of the Predictive Set Theory framework.

</details>


### [133] [Towards a new paradigm of scientific discovery with socialized artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02775)
*Xinjie Yao,Xingxin Xu,Xiyuan Gao,Zhoupeng Guo,Kunlong Yang,Dengyu Zhao,Siqi Zhao,Zhihe Fan,Yichen Dong,Xin Li,Jiekang Feng,Jiahe Wu,Sen Wang,Beiming Yu,Kejia Zhao,Ruipu Zhao,Jiaqi Zhou,Heyang Li,Jianjun Chen,Anbo Dai,Xin Liu,Zhengtao Yu,Qinghua Hu,Pengfei Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BLAZE（Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation）范式，将AI从孤立研究任务的助手转变为科学发现的组织化基础设施，通过连接知识、推理、实验验证和人类判断，构建社会化的科学智能，使科学发现更加可追溯、可复现和累积化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现经历了观察实验、理论、计算和数据密集型方法等多次组织变革，当前面临的核心挑战不再是生产更多信息，而是如何将不断扩展的知识、推理和证据组织成一个连贯的发现过程。现有AI工具仅服务于孤立的研究任务，缺乏整合科学发现全流程的组织化能力。

Method: BLAZE将AI定位为科学发现的组织基础设施，而非单任务助手。它在持续的研究生命周期中连接持久知识、集体推理、实证验证和人类判断，将碎片化活动转化为累积性的探究、批判和修订过程。核心前提是科学智能源于知识、假设、实验与集体验证之间的持续互动，而非单纯的计算。

Result: BLAZE将人类与机器组织在共享的科学进程中，使科学发现更可追溯、可复现和累积化，同时保留人类的创造力、判断力和责任感。

Conclusion: 社会化的科学智能可能为下一个科学时代奠定基础，其目的不是取代人类发现，而是扩展集体科学探究的规模、深度和连续性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery has advanced through successive transformations in the organization of knowledge. Observation and experimentation established the empirical foundations of science. Theory made it possible to derive general principles from particular phenomena. Computation extended inquiry into systems beyond direct observation, while data-intensive methods opened new spaces of pattern and prediction. Science now confronts a different frontier. The central challenge is no longer simply to produce more information, but to organize expanding knowledge, reasoning, and evidence into a coherent process of discovery. Here, we introduce Bridging Literature, Agents, and Zero-gap Experimentation (BLAZE), a paradigm of socialized scientific intelligence. BLAZE conceives AI not as an assistant for isolated research tasks, but as an organizational infrastructure for scientific discovery. It connects persistent knowledge, collective reasoning, empirical validation, and human judgment within a continuous research lifecycle, transforming fragmented activities into a cumulative process of inquiry, criticism, and revision. The central premise of BLAZE is that scientific intelligence does not arise from computation alone. It emerges from the sustained interaction among knowledge, hypotheses, experiments, and collective verification. By organizing humans and machines within a shared scientific process, BLAZE makes discovery more traceable, reproducible, and cumulative while preserving human creativity, judgment, and responsibility. Socialized scientific intelligence may provide a foundation for the next era of science. Its purpose is not to replace human discovery, but to extend the scale, depth, and continuity of collective scientific inquiry.

</details>


### [134] [BAP-SQL: Budget-Aware Observation Planning for Agentic Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02876)
*Chong Peng,Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Varun Sah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BAP-SQL通过将观测形成视为预算控制阶段，包含风险估计、SQL重写和独立运行时防护机制，在agentic text-to-SQL任务中提升了严格预算下的成功率，同时减少token使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体并非被动消费观测，其行为决定了后续输入。在agentic text-to-SQL中，宽泛查询会消耗上下文和数据库资源后才出现有用证据，而事后压缩无法恢复被省略的行或已消耗的工作。

Method: BAP-SQL将观测形成作为预算控制阶段：估计查询风险、在有用时重写SQL，并将硬限制委托给独立的运行时防护层。在通用4B、专用FINER-SQL 4B及7B骨干网络上进行测试。

Result: 在BIRD衍生设置中，BAP-SQL相比匹配SFT提升3.4/3.6个百分点成功率，同时减少4.5/5.0% token使用。匹配重训练和任务级迁移将增益归因于策略可见的规划和预算敏感的救援。收益随模型能力和预算增加而减弱，在最宽松设置下出现反转，且未减少数据库工作量。

Conclusion: BAP-SQL在严格预算条件下有效提升agentic text-to-SQL性能，但收益具有条件性：随模型能力和预算增加而衰减，且在宽松设置下可能反转；不减少数据库工作或节省计算资源。

Abstract: Tool-using agents do not merely consume observations: their actions determine what arrives next. In agentic text-to-SQL, a broad query can spend context and database work before useful evidence appears, while post-hoc compression cannot recover omitted rows or expended work. We present BAP-SQL, which treats observation formation as a budget-control stage: it estimates query risk, rewrites SQL when useful, and delegates hard limits to an independent runtime shield. Across general 4B, specialized FINER-SQL 4B, and 7B backbones, BAP-SQL improves tight-budget success. On the primary BIRD-derived setting, it gains 3.4/3.6 percentage points over matched SFT while using 4.5/5.0% fewer tokens. Matched retraining and task-level transfer associate the gain with policy-visible planning and budget-sensitive rescue. The benefit attenuates as model capability and budget increase, reverses at the loosest setting, and does not reduce database work.

</details>


### [135] [VeriTrace: Human-Like Temporal Exploration Completes Agentic Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02878)
*Yu-Tung Liu,Cunxi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VeriTrace是一个多智能体系统，通过为Inspector智能体提供完整的调试动作空间（包括信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度的独立控制），实现了对Verilog RTL生成的100% Pass@1准确率，首次在VerilogEval-V2基准上达到完美功能正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的多智能体系统在标准的Verilog RTL生成基准上精度卡在约95%无法突破，其根本原因是调试动作空间不完整——系统限制了智能体可检查的信号、可查询的时间窗口或两者兼有，导致调试退化为对狭窄预定视图的模式匹配，而非假设驱动的根因分析。

Method: 提出VeriTrace多智能体系统，其Inspector智能体运行于完整的调试动作空间，对信号选择、时间窗口边界和迭代深度进行独立控制。该系统引入Agentic Temporal Exploration（智能体时间探索）能力，使智能体能够形成故障原因假设、查询波形获取证据、迭代精炼理解，模拟人类验证工程师的探索式调试过程。

Result: VeriTrace在VerilogEval-V2基准上达到100%的Pass@1，是该基准上首个实现完美功能正确性的系统。在共享Claude Sonnet 4.0骨干模型下，VeriTrace优于最强复现基线5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 调试智能体的行动自由度（即调试agency）是弥合最终精度差距的关键因素，验证了将完整、可探索的调试动作空间赋予智能体可以突破现有RTL生成系统的性能上限。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promise for automated Verilog RTL generation, yet state-of-the-art multi-agent systems plateau at ~95% accuracy on standard benchmarks. We trace this ceiling to an incomplete debugging action space: existing systems restrict which signals the agent can inspect, which time windows it can query, or both, reducing debugging to pattern matching on a narrow, predetermined view of circuit behavior rather than hypothesis-driven root-cause analysis. We present VeriTrace, a multi-agent system whose Inspector agent operates over a complete debugging action space, with independent control over signal selection, time-window bounds, and iteration depth. This capability, which we term Agentic Temporal Exploration, enables the agent to form hypotheses about failure causes, query the waveform for evidence, and refine its understanding iteratively, mirroring the exploratory process of human verification engineers. VeriTrace achieves 100\% Pass@1 on VerilogEval-V2, the first system to attain perfect functional correctness on this benchmark. On a shared Claude Sonnet 4.0 backbone, VeriTrace outperforms the strongest reproduced baseline by +5.1%, demonstrating that debugging agency closes the final accuracy gap.

</details>


### [136] [Interpreting Black-Box Large Language Models with Sentence-Level Energy Landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02879)
*Maryam Rezaee,Pooriya Safaei,Maryam Asgarinezhad,Fatemeh Seyyedsalehi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关的、事后、句子级别的归因解释器，通过训练能量模型作为替代模型来捕捉LLM的输入输出概念一致性，并训练轻量级解释器网络，在无需额外API查询的情况下量化提示句对目标输出的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专有LLM仅通过封闭API访问，缺乏可解释性，导致负责任部署面临关键挑战。

Method: 1) 训练Energy-Based Model (EBM)作为替代模型，捕捉LLM内部提示与响应之间的概念一致性；2) 利用该能量景观指导轻量级解释器网络的训练；3) 解释器训练完成后作为独立工具运行，无需再向LLM发起API查询即可对提示句子的影响力进行归因；4) 通过全局训练局部解释器，覆盖广泛的生成模式并缓解实例特定偏差。

Result: 实验证明EBM能够准确模拟目标LLM，使得解释器能够有效识别对生成特定目标输出最具影响力的提示句子。

Conclusion: 该方法为黑盒LLM提供了一种无需持续API访问的本地归因解释方案，兼顾了模型无关性、效率与可解释性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of proprietary Large Language Models (LLMs) accessed strictly through closed APIs has created a critical challenge for responsible deployment: a fundamental lack of interpretability. To address this, we propose a model-agnostic, post-hoc attribution interpreter operating at the sentence level. Our approach trains an Energy-Based Model (EBM) as a surrogate to capture the LLM's internal conceptual consistency between prompts and responses. This energy landscape guides the training of a lightweight interpreter network. Uniquely, our interpreter operates as a standalone tool; once trained, it quantifies the influence of prompt sentences on a user-specified target output without requiring further API queries to the LLM. By globally training a local interpreter across diverse inputs, our framework captures broader generation patterns and mitigates instance-specific biases. Experiments demonstrate that our EBM accurately simulates the target LLM, allowing the interpreter to effectively identify the prompt sentences most influential in generating specific target outputs.

</details>


### [137] [Hypercubes, Hyperplanes, and Constraint-Induced Complexity Collapse in Atomic Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02930)
*Irene Tsapara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过超立方体与超平面的几何视角重新审视高元原子概念学习，发现地面实例的r维超立方体并非结构均匀：除全对角线外的超平面可被有限个初等等价类折叠，且其类别数量与项深度无关，而全对角线是例外情况。文章基于作者此前的高维框架，通过典型简单概念、最小排序和代表归约来解释这一不对称性，产出了高维超平面行为的分类学，并表明复杂度是局部化的而非均匀分布于实例空间。论文包含完整的二元情形、三元超立方体的显式处理以及归约机制的详细展开说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高元原子概念学习中逻辑复杂度在实例空间中的分布规律，揭示超立方体几何结构的不均匀性及其与概念归约理论之间的内在联系。

Method: 采用几何与逻辑交叉的视角，从超立方体和超平面结构出发，并结合作者先前建立的高维框架，通过典型简单概念、最小排序和代表归约等工具对超平面行为进行分类和分析。

Result: 确立了高维超平面的行为分类学：除全对角线外，每个超平面可坍缩为有限个初等等价类，且上界不依赖于项深度；复杂度被证明是局部化的。三维情形已展示正交族、部分对角线和全对角线例外等核心现象。

Conclusion: 这种几何—逻辑视角阐明了原子概念学习中复杂度集中的位置，并提出了一种以受约束假设空间和结构化分类为核心的现代解释。

Abstract: We revisit higher-arity atomic concept learning through the geometry of hypercubes and hyperplanes of ground instances. Our starting point is the observation that the ambient r-dimensional hypercube of ground atoms is not structurally uniform. Its logical complexity is organized by hyperplanes: every hyperplane other than the full diagonal collapses into finitely many elementary-equivalence classes, with a bound independent of the term depth, while the full diagonal is exceptional and its class count grows without bound. This asymmetry is not merely geometric. It reflects the reduction-theoretic structure of the concepts themselves. Building on a higher-dimensional framework developed in the author's earlier work, we reinterpret these results through canonical simple concepts, minimal orderings, and representative reductions. This yields a taxonomy of hyperplane behavior in higher dimensions and shows that complexity is localized rather than spread uniformly through the instance space. The paper includes a fully worked binary case, an explicit treatment of the ternary hypercube, and an unpacked account of the reduction machinery that drives the collapse. The three-dimensional case already exhibits the essential phenomenon of orthogonal families, partial diagonals, and the exceptional full diagonal. This geometric-logical perspective clarifies where complexity is concentrated in atomic concept learning and suggests a modern interpretation in terms of constrained hypothesis spaces and structured classification.

</details>


### [138] [When Compression Scores Cannot Decide: Information Boundaries for Group-Robust LLM Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02940)
*Andrew Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了可复现的压缩统计量可能选出错误候选，提出了"信息界面"理论框架来建模统计量与候选端点之间的差距，并给出了包括锥形定律、精确池化代价公式、三种双世界构造及观测纤维半径在内的完整分析工具。实验表明组解析对角方法可恢复粗略损伤排序（Spearman 0.9239），粗粒度深度分配在三个稠密LLM上降低最差组困惑度膨胀12.6-20.9%，模型特化的完整掩码端点选择相对提升2.7-8.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩统计量即使在统计学上具有可复现性（如0.906的裂半信度），也可能选出比对照组差6.0%和7.7%的错误端点。作者旨在解释这一"统计量—端点"差距产生的机制，并建立严格的数学框架来界定每种统计量所能支持的区别，从而指导可靠的选择。

Method: 提出了信息界面（information interfaces）理论，形式化刻画每种统计量可支持的区分范围。具体包括：(1) 对等权组推导出锥形定律，给出正线性固定候选损伤的精确池化代价，涵盖对角及满PSD二阶矩；(2) 构建三种双世界构造和精确观测纤维半径，刻画池化矩、组局部矩与参考路径曲率未解决的模糊性；(3) 提出组解析对角方法（恢复Spearman 0.9239的粗略损伤顺序）；(4) 提出粗粒度深度分配策略，与均衡均匀分配对比；(5) 在OLMoE中用路由器轨迹预测单例方向；(6) 使用有限菜单决策进行逐层端到端验证。

Result: 粗粒度深度分配在三个稠密LLM上使最差组困惑度膨胀降低12.6-20.9%；模型特化的完整掩码端点选择比对照基准提升2.7-8.0%；在OLMoE中路由器轨迹预测单例方向达114/192，优于最强重标号的81/192；单层有限菜单决策在保留的最差组KL上分别降低13.7%和7.2%。组解析对角可恢复粗略损伤秩序（Spearman 0.9239）但细粒度秩序仍弱。

Conclusion: 局部测量仅用于构建候选集，而选择必须由完整候选端点的验证或经校准的均匀保证来授权，且不确定性须针对每次比较进行校准。这一框架为压缩统计量的可靠选择提供了严格的理论边界与实践协议。

Abstract: A reproducible compression statistic can still select the wrong candidate. A dense pruning score with 0.906 split-half reliability predicted a 16.1% gain. Its selected endpoint was 6.0% and 7.7% worse than two controls. We model the gap through information interfaces that delimit which distinctions each statistic supports. For equal-weight groups, a conic law gives the exact pooling price for positive linear fixed-candidate damage, including diagonal and full PSD second moments. Three two-world constructions and an exact observation-fiber radius characterize what pooled moments, group-local moments, and reference-path curvature leave unresolved. A group-resolved diagonal recovers broad damage order (Spearman 0.9239) while fine order remains weak. Relative to balanced uniform allocation, a coarse depth allocation cuts worst-group perplexity inflation by 12.6--20.9% across three dense LLMs. Model-specific complete-mask endpoint selection improves over those references by 2.7--8.0%. In OLMoE, router traces predict singleton direction (114/192 versus 81/192 under the strongest relabeling). Finite-menu decisions on one layer yield held-out worst-group KL reductions of 13.7% and 7.2%. Local measurements construct candidates. Selection is licensed by complete candidate endpoints or a validated uniform guarantee, with uncertainty calibrated to every comparison.

</details>


### [139] [On the missing data layer and a potential solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02949)
*Francis F Daniel,Mauro Ibañez,Francis Perelman,Marian Basti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DataHub，一种面向拉丁美洲AI生态的任务优先数据基础设施，通过/<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>本体组织，解决数据集发现和供给两大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉丁美洲缺乏AI基础设施的两个基础层：数据集层和基准层。数据集层面临发现和供给两个叠加问题——现有数据集分散在各个平台缺乏统一索引，且即使索引完善，总量也远低于前沿AI开发所需。本文聚焦数据集层，旨在解决这一基础设施缺口。

Method: 提出DataHub框架：一种任务优先（task-first）的数据基础设施，通过本体结构 /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>/ 进行组织，并整合数据集发现、元数据管理、贡献机制、许可管理和复用等关键功能。

Result: 论文提出了一种针对拉丁美洲AI数据集层的系统性基础设施方案，但目前仅停留在框架设计层面，尚未报告具体的实施结果或实证评估。

Conclusion: DataHub旨在通过任务优先的本体组织和系统化的数据管理机制，填补拉丁美洲AI数据集层的基础设施空白，为后续基准层建设奠定基础，从而推动区域AI生态发展。

Abstract: Latin America is missing two foundational layers of AI infrastructure: the dataset layer and the benchmark layer. This paper targets the dataset layer. The dataset layer faces two compounding problems: discovery and supply. Latin American AI datasets exist but are scattered across platforms with no shared index. Even with perfect indexing, the total volume would remain far below what frontier AI development requires. We propose DataHub: a task-first data infrastructure organized through the ontology /<task?>/<domain?>/<language?>, with mechanisms for dataset discovery, metadata, contribution, licensing, and reuse.

</details>


### [140] [Neurosymbolic Reasoning with Incremental Knowledge for Sample Efficient Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02993)
*Subrat Prasad Panda,Blaise Genest,Arvind Easwaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号分层强化学习框架InK，通过可更新的增量知识符号规划（使用D*）结合低层目标条件神经模块，显著提升了稀疏奖励长时程任务中的样本效率；同时开发了Belief World Tree Search以利用先验知识进行最优符号规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在稀疏奖励且需要长时程推理的环境中，扁平RL智能体面临严峻的样本效率挑战。虽然可以将知识融入学习，但标准HRL以固定、不可更新的形式编码知识，无法利用探索过程中获得的增量知识进行推理，导致在获取足够环境知识之前样本效率低下。

Method: 提出神经符号HRL框架InK：符号高层组件在可更新的当前增量知识表示上进行符号规划（如D*算法），低层目标条件神经模块通过经验与奖励塑形学习运动基元。此外，开发Belief World Tree Search以在拥有先验世界知识时执行最优符号规划。

Result: 在导航任务上的实验表明，引入增量知识（InK）显著提升了样本效率。

Conclusion: 增量知识更新与符号规划的神经符号分层框架能够有效提升稀疏奖励长时程导航任务的样本效率，代码已开源。
