Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) integrates text, audio, and vision to infer human affect, yet real-world multimodal observations are often incomplete. Existing methods for incomplete-observation MSA mainly follow two paradigms. Reconstruction-based methods recover missing information from observed modalities, while joint-representation methods learn directly from incomplete inputs. Although effective, these methods usually treat modality reliability only implicitly within representation learning or fusion design rather than modeling it explicitly. We argue that modality reliability is a central variable in incomplete-observation settings. Failure to model it explicitly gives rise to two related issues. The first is reliability mismatch, in which the affective evidence retained by each modality varies across samples and missing rates. The second is reliability propagation bias, in which messages from degraded modalities may adversely affect cross-modal interaction and predictive performance. To address these issues, we propose MRCF, a Modality Reliability-Calibrated Framework for MSA with incomplete observations. MRCF contains a Reliability-Aware Branch that estimates sample-specific modality reliability from intramodal quality cues and cross-modal semantic consistency, a Reliability-Guided Interaction Branch that uses the estimated scores to modulate cross-modal information flow, and a Reliability-Calibrated Fusion Module that integrates reliability and semantic cues for final prediction. Experiments on CMU-MOSI, CMU-MOSEI, and CH-SIMS show that MRCF achieves strong performance under standard incomplete-observation protocols. Further analyses provide evidence that explicit reliability modeling helps mitigate reliability mismatch and reliability propagation bias during interaction and fusion.

</details>


### [206] [Unequal Verdicts: Investigating Gender Bias in LLM-Based Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03627)
*Razieh Chalehchaleh,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统性地调查了大型语言模型在基于真实数据的假新闻检测中的性别偏见，通过增强LIAR基准数据集，在六个先进LLM上发现9.79%-35.13%的陈述在不同性别变体下获得不一致标签，且五个模型表现出统计显著的定向性别偏向，多为"男性怀疑"模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于自动化事实核查，但其在该场景中的性别偏见敏感性尚未被充分探索。本研究旨在填补这一空白，首次使用真实世界数据系统性调查LLM假新闻检测中的性别偏见。

Method: 对LIAR基准数据集的每条陈述增加三种说话者职位标题的性别变体（中性、男性、女性），评估六个最先进的LLM，使用多种偏见与公平性度量指标，量化不一致标签率与翻转率，并识别偏见的两种表现形态：不稳定性和方向性。

Result: 所有模型均表现出性别敏感性：9.79%-35.13%的陈述在三种变体间获得不一致标签，男性-女性比较显示6.5%-23.6%的翻转率。五个模型表现出统计显著的定向效应，最强效应呈现"男性怀疑"（male-skeptic）模式。

Conclusion: 性别偏见同时损害了LLM假新闻检测的可靠性和公平性，强调了需要偏见感知的评估和缓解策略。增强数据集已公开发布以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for automated fact-checking, yet their susceptibility to gender bias in this context remains underexplored. This study presents the first systematic investigation of gender bias in LLM-based fake news detection using real-world data. We augment the LIAR benchmark with three gender variants of speaker job titles (Neutral, Male, Female) for each statement to test whether veracity judgments vary solely based on gender presentation. Six state-of-the-art LLMs are evaluated across multiple bias and fairness metrics. All models exhibit gender sensitivity: 9.79%-35.13% of statements receive inconsistent labels across the three variants, with Male-Female comparisons showing 6.5%-23.6% flip rates. Two primary bias manifestations are identified: instability (inconsistent judgments) and directionality (systematic favoritism). Five models show statistically significant directional effects, with the strongest effects displaying male-skeptic patterns. These findings demonstrate that gender bias undermines both reliability and fairness in LLM-based fake news detection, highlighting the need for bias-aware evaluation and mitigation strategies. The augmented dataset is publicly released to support future research.

</details>


### [207] [Cross-Layer Interaction under Weight-Space Ablation: A Closed-Form Attention Jacobian Bound and a Test on a Real Pretrained Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03629)
*Abdallah Khemais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了同伴论文中关于激活修补与权重空间消融一致性的结果，突破了单一残差块和小型合成任务的限制。主要贡献包括：推导出跨层消融相互作用的同块分解公式；精确隔离两层的跨层余项为混合二阶导数的二重积分，并指出需要注意力子块的雅可比界限来界定它；以闭式推导并在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct真实权重上零违例验证该界限；给出了同伴论文未展示的曲率常数；并在该模型中发现了间接宾语识别的涌现电路，测试结果显示部分而非全部案例满足塌缩、解离和相互作用特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同伴论文在理想化模型（条件计算通过残差流加性进行）中研究了激活修补与权重空间消融的一致性，但结果局限于单一残差块且仅在小合成任务上验证。本文旨在将这一理论结果扩展到更深层次的实际模型中，并探索该模型的真实能力。

Method: 1) 将跨多层的消融载体的相互作用精确分解为每层同块项加上跨层余项；2) 对两层情况，将余项精确隔离为混合二阶导数的二重积分，识别出界定它所需的注意力子块雅可比界限，并以闭式推导该界限；3) 在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct的真实权重的每一层上验证该界限；4) 给出同伴论文遗漏的曲率常数的闭式解；5) 使用原始激活修补方法在未设计的模型中搜索间接宾语识别电路，并测试其塌缩、解离和相互作用性质。

Result: 1) 跨层相互作用分解为同块项加跨层余项的结果成立；2) 雅可比界限以闭式推导出，且在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct真实权重的各层上零违例验证，但尚未跨层串联；3) 曲率常数以闭式给出；4) 在该模型中发现间接宾语识别的涌现电路；测试结果混合：五个实例中出现共享载体，塌缩和解离在大多数但非全部实例中成立，五个中三个在非同块层对上测到非零相互作用。

Conclusion: 本文成功将激活修补与权重消融一致性的理论框架从单块扩展到多层，给出了关键的雅可比界限闭式解并验证。但跨层余项的界限尚未完全闭合，且涌现电路的性质测试结果混合，说明理论结果在实际模型上的适用性仍有局限，需要进一步的跨层串联和更深入的经验验证。

Abstract: A companion paper studies when activation patching and weight-space ablation agree, inside an idealized model where a conditional computation is carried additively through a residual stream. For the one composition in that model where two carriers are architecturally dependent, an attention head and its own layer's normalization-MLP composition, it derives an exact first-order interaction formula, zero when only the MLP is ablated and second-order bounded when the head is also ablated. That result is confined to a single residual block and checked only on small transformers on a synthetic task.
  This paper extends the result past both limits. First, the interaction from ablating carriers spanning several layers decomposes exactly into same-block terms, one per touched layer, plus a cross-layer remainder on which the decomposition makes no claim of smallness. Second, we isolate that remainder exactly, for two layers, as a double integral of a mixed second derivative, and name the missing ingredient needed to bound it: a Jacobian bound for the attention sub-block. We derive this bound in closed form and verify it, without a single violation, against Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct's real weights, though we do not yet chain it across layers. We also give, in closed form, the curvature constant the companion paper's bound leaves unexhibited.
  Third, on that same model, we search for and find an emergent circuit for indirect object identification, never designed into it, using the original activation-patching method for this task, and test collapse, dissociation, and interaction on it. The result is mixed: a shared carrier emerges across all five tested instances, collapse and dissociation hold on most but not all, and a nonzero interaction is measurable on three of five, at layer pairs outside the same-block case the companion theorem covers.

</details>


### [208] [When Teachers Mislead: Spurious-Signal-Aware On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03632)
*Yinuo Jiang,Yongjie Ye,Zhou Tao,Xiang Zhuang,Qiang Zhang,Huajun Chen,Tiankai Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SA-OPD框架，通过在on-policy蒸馏中识别并过滤"伪信号"（即受语言先验、格式惯例或刻板推理模板驱动而非任务特定证据的token级监督），从而提升蒸馏质量和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的选择性on-policy蒸馏方法（如基于置信度、信息量或可学习性的信号优先策略）忽略了语言模型的一个根本缺陷：token级别的判断可能被输入无关的语言先验、格式惯例或刻板推理模板所驱动，而非任务特定证据。这类"伪信号"会产生大梯度，但对任务改进方向的贡献甚微，从而阻碍蒸馏优化。

Method: SA-OPD框架包含两个关键组件：(1) 一个轻量级的输入接地性（input-groundedness）代理，用于估计token级蒸馏信号是否真正依赖于输入；(2) 一个过滤机制，仅过滤同时满足"低输入接地性"和"极端蒸馏发散度"两个条件的token，从而去除高影响的伪更新，实现细粒度的on-policy蒸馏优化。

Result: 在LLM和VLM两种设置下的大量实验表明，SA-OPD在性能上一致优于Vanilla OPD和多种具有竞争力的选择性蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: SA-OPD验证了输入接地性是on-policy蒸馏监督选择的关键维度，并提供了一种简单有效的缓解伪更新策略，为蒸馏中的监督信号筛选提供了新视角。

Abstract: On-Policy distillation (OPD) transfers teacher capabilities by supervising student-sampled trajectories with dense token-level teacher signals. Recent selective OPD methods improve this process by prioritizing signals that are confident, informative, or learnable. However, the assumptions overlook a fundamental failure mode of language models: their token-level judgments can be driven by input-agnostic language priors, formatting conventions, or stereotyped reasoning templates rather than task-specific evidence. We refer to such optimization-relevant but weakly input-grounded supervision as spurious signals in OPD, which may produce large gradients while contributing little task-improving direction. To mitigate this issue, we propose SA-OPD, a Spurious-Signal-Aware On-Policy Distillation framework that identifies and filters misleading token-level supervision based on input-groundedness and optimization impact. SA-OPD introduces a lightweight input-groundedness proxy estimating whether a token-level distillation signal truly depends on the input. It then filters only tokens that simultaneously exhibit low input-groundedness and extreme distillation divergence, thereby removing high-impact spurious updates and achieving fine-grained OPD optimization. Extensive experiments on both large language model (LLM) and vision-language model (VLM) settings demonstrate that SA-OPD consistently outperforms Vanilla OPD and competitive selective methods. These results establish input-groundedness as a key dimension for OPD supervision selection and offer a simple, effective strategy for mitigating spurious updates.

</details>


### [209] [Is Inter-Seed Cross-Play Enough? Evaluating the Robustness of Zero-Shot Coordination Algorithms to Implementation Details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03644)
*Maksymilian Wolski,Nicholas Hoernle,Johannes Forkel,Jakob Foerster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地评估了零样本协调（ZSC）算法对实现细节差异的鲁棒性，提出了一种新的评估方案——跨实现交叉游戏（cross-implementation cross-play），并以此评估了流行的ZSC算法Other-Play，发现标准ZSC评估方法在实际中可以合理代理这种更彻底的跨实现评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZSC算法旨在使独立构建的AI智能体在未见过的场景中与人类或其他智能体协调。然而，现有ZSC算法的评估几乎只用单一实现在不同随机种子下训练，未考虑不同团队独立解读和实现同一算法规范时产生的实现差异，导致对算法鲁棒性存在疑问。

Method: 作者提出cross-implementation cross-play评估方案，系统地改变先前研究已证明会影响多智能体强化学习（MARL）算法性能的实现细节，并使用该方案评估流行的ZSC算法Other-Play。

Result: 结果表明，对于Other-Play算法，标准的ZSC评估方法实际上是更彻底的跨实现评估的合理代理，即该算法对实现细节差异具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这项工作是首次系统评估ZSC算法对规范歧义和实现细节的鲁棒性，其令人鼓舞的发现表明标准评估方法在特定情况下是足够的，为ZSC算法的评估实践提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: AI agents deployed in real-world settings must be capable of coordinating with humans and other AI agents they have not encountered before. Zero-shot coordination (ZSC) algorithms aim to achieve this by specifying high-level learning rules such that independently engineered agents can coordinate with each other at test time. Rigorous evaluation of ZSC algorithms remains difficult: ideally, multiple independent implementations of each proposed algorithm must be used, reflecting the variation that arises when independent parties interpret and implement the same specification. In practice, however, ZSC algorithms have almost exclusively been evaluated using a single implementation trained across different random seeds, with only a handful of works additionally varying the neural network architecture. This leaves open questions about robustness to specification ambiguities and implementation details. In this work, we provide the first systematic evaluation of this robustness. We introduce a new evaluation scheme, cross-implementation cross-play, varying implementation details that prior work has shown to affect the performance of multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithms, and we evaluate Other-Play, a popular ZSC algorithm, with this scheme. Our findings are encouraging and suggest that, for Other-Play, the standard ZSC evaluation is, in fact, a reasonable proxy for this more thorough cross-implementation evaluation.

</details>


### [210] [AutoSND: From Execution Evidence to Structural Policies for Automated Network Dismantling Heuristic Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03653)
*Zhijing Hu,Changjun Fan,Yufan Deng,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AutoSND，一个三阶段树搜索框架，用于自动设计完整的网络瓦解算法程序，无需人工手动设计启发式规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络瓦解是分析复杂系统鲁棒性与脆弱性的基础任务，但现有实用启发式算法需要在效果与计算效率之间权衡，且通常依赖研究者手工设计。虽然基于大语言模型的自动启发式设计方法能够生成和筛选候选方案，但它们难以在执行过程中将候选质量或失败状态转化为结构层面的指导信息用于后续生成。

Method: AutoSND采用三阶段树搜索框架：阶段一从简单启发式出发进行广泛搜索并归档执行证据；阶段二将候选记录编译为关于局部信号、邻域访问和状态更新范围的结构化策略；阶段三基于这些策略继续树搜索，最终获得质量优先的AutoSND-Q和速度优先的AutoSND-S两个候选程序。

Result: 在12个真实网络和3个大型真实网络上的实验表明，AutoSND取得了更好的搜索性能和稳定性，发现了更具竞争力且结构可解释的网络瓦解程序。最终候选程序以残差度为主干，用有界局部信号调整节点顺序，并限制状态更新范围。

Conclusion: AutoSND不仅提升了自动搜索网络瓦解算法的效果与稳定性，还生成了结构可解释、可复用的完整瓦解程序，代码已开源。

Abstract: Network dismantling is fundamental to analyzing the robustness and vulnerability of complex systems, yet practical heuristics must balance effectiveness and computational efficiency, and are usually designed manually by researchers. Existing large language model based automatic heuristic design methods can generate and screen candidates, yet they have difficulty further transforming candidate quality or failure states during execution into structural-level guid- ance for subsequent generation. We propose AutoSND, a three stage tree search framework for complete network dismantling pro- grams. Stage I broadly explores from simple heuristics and archives execution evidence. Stage II compiles candidate records into struc- tural policies concerning local signals, neighborhood access, and state update ranges. Stage III continues tree search conditioned on these policies and obtains the final quality prioritized and speed prioritized candidates, AutoSND-Q/S. Experiments on 12 real world networks and 3 large real world networks show that AutoSND achieves better search performance and stability and discovers more competitive and structurally interpretable network disman- tling programs. The final candidates form an interpretable structure that uses residual degree as the backbone, adjusts node order with bounded local signals, and restricts the state update range. Code is available at https://github.com/MirrorNew/AutoSND.

</details>


### [211] [Taming the Implicit: Dual-Channel Risk-Aware Reinforcement Fine-Tuning for Continual Multimodal Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03660)
*Yibei Liu,Jiajun Chen,Qianle Zhang,Tangyue Jin,Mengying Zhu,Meng Xi,Yangyang Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出风险感知策略优化（RAPO），一种用于多模态大语言模型持续后训练中强化微调（RFT）的显式风险治理框架，通过策略通道和数据通道双通道协同，显著降低灾难性遗忘。在MLLM-CL基准上，RAPO相对RLOO骨干将最终遗忘率降低79.8%，且保持新任务竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（RFT）被广泛认为在持续后训练中具有天然的抗灾难性遗忘能力。然而在显著的任务分布偏移下，代表性RFT算法的遗忘急剧上升，其根源在于RFT固有的隐式奖励方差正则化无法抑制不受控的优化风险。因此需要显式的风险治理机制。

Method: 提出双通道框架RAPO：在策略通道中，风险感知策略缩放（Risk-Aware Policy Scaling）通过rollout可靠性和Fisher启发的局部预测敏感性自适应校准每个样本的更新幅度；在数据通道中，风险感知动态桶采样（Risk-Aware Dynamic Bucket Sampling）通过动态风险分层重组训练批次，引导优化朝向信息丰富且稳定的样本。该方法是即插即用的，无需跨任务记忆，可与任何RFT算法无缝集成。

Result: 在公开的MLLM-CL基准上，RAPO相对其RLOO骨干将最终遗忘率降低79.8%，同时保持新任务竞争力。

Conclusion: RAPO通过显式风险治理有效解决了RFT在任务分布偏移下的灾难性遗忘问题，且作为通用即插即用策略无需修改即可适配任意RFT算法，为多模态持续后训练的鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) is widely believed to inherently resist catastrophic forgetting in continual post-training of multimodal large language models. Under pronounced task distributional shifts, however, forgetting across representative RFT algorithms escalates sharply. This stems from the implicit reward-variance regularization inherent to RFT, which proves incapable of suppressing uncontrolled optimization risk. We propose Risk-Aware Policy Optimization (RAPO), the first dual-channel framework for explicit risk governance in continual RFT. On the policy channel, Risk-Aware Policy Scaling adaptively calibrates per-sample update magnitude via rollout reliability and Fisher-inspired local predictive sensitivity; on the data channel, Risk-Aware Dynamic Bucket Sampling reorganizes training batches through dynamic risk stratification, steering optimization toward informative yet stable samples. As a plug-and-play strategy requiring no cross-task memory, RAPO generalizes to any RFT algorithm without modification. On the public MLLM-CL benchmark, RAPO reduces final forgetting by 79.8% relative to its RLOO backbone while retaining new-task competitiveness.

</details>


### [212] [Shielding for Higher-Order Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03662)
*Filip Cano,Thomas A. Henzinger,Konstantin Kueffner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高阶平滑约束的安全防护盾合成方法，通过有限差分定义微分安全性质，将安全防护盾合成问题归约为历史状态空间上的普通安全博弈，并设计了逐级迭代求解的最大允许防护盾合成算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典安全防护盾仅针对状态谓词（当前物理状态安全或不安全）进行合成，但在许多信息物理系统中，安全性不仅涉及状态本身，还涉及状态的导数（如速度、加速度、加加速度）。例如车辆不仅要避免碰撞，还要遵守速度限制、加速度力限制和加加速度限制。这种基于导数的约束需要更高阶的平滑性考量，而现有方法无法处理这类高维物理约束。

Method: 本文首先在离散化状态空间上使用有限差分定义微分安全属性，并刻画其表达能力。然后在这些约束下将防护盾合成问题归约为历史状态空间上的普通安全博弈，提出了合成算法，该算法生成的防护盾对k阶属性恰好存储k个过去状态，并证明了该记忆量是必需的。此外，还提出了一种迭代合成程序，用于构造最大允许的防护盾，该程序按照导数约束的层次结构逐级求解：每次迭代的求解结果用于剪枝下一约束的状态空间，从而避免探索已知不安全的大范围区域，提高合成效率。

Result: 提出了针对高阶平滑约束的安全博弈构造方法；给出了合成算法（每个盾需存储k个过去状态）并证明了该记忆量的必要性；提出了在导数约束层次上迭代求解的最大允许防护盾合成算法，该算法通过逐级剪枝显著提高了实际合成效率。

Conclusion: 本文系统地解决了高阶导数约束下的安全防护盾合成问题，通过有限差分离散化、历史状态空间归约和迭代剪枝策略，既保证了合成防护盾的正确性（必要记忆量k）和最大允许性，又提升了实际合成效率。

Abstract: Safety shields are runtime enforcement mechanisms that restrict the actions of a controller to guarantee safety. Classical shields are usually synthesised for state predicates: the current physical state is either safe or unsafe, and the shield disables precisely those actions that can force the system into an unsafe state in the future. In many cyber-physical applications this view is too coarse. A vehicle approaching an obstacle should not only avoid collision, but also respect speed regulations, force limits induced by acceleration, and jerk limits to prevent injuries. From a physical perspective, these requirements are predicated over the derivatives of the state. This paper develops a finite-state safety-game construction for such high-order smoothness constraints. We define differential safety properties using finite differences over a discretised state space, characterise their expressiveness, and reduce shield synthesis to an ordinary safety game over a history state space. We give a synthesis algorithm whose shields store exactly $k$ past states for properties of order $k$ and prove that this memory is necessary. We describe an iterative synthesis procedure for a maximally permissive shield that operates over hierarchies of derivative constraints. The algorithm solves constraints iteratively in increasing order and uses the solution at each iteration to prune the state space for the next constraint. This makes shield synthesis more efficient in practice, as the algorithm refrains from exploring large regions of the state space that are known to be unsafe.

</details>


### [213] [LiveEvalBench: Toward Open-World Evaluation for Web Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03689)
*Yiyao Wang,Zhen Wen,Yinghao Tang,Yixiao Fu,Lin Yuan,Xiaolau Zhang,Jun Zhou,Wei Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LiveEvalBench是一个将前端项目生成评估从静态评估范式重构为智能体化、自适应且可扩展的框架。它通过构建工程师、代码工程师和UI测试员三个协作角色，在部署、代码检查和浏览器交互全生命周期中收集证据，并结合共享准则与实现特定标准来处理实现的多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准将web生成视为静态评估问题，但前端项目本质上是交互式的、允许多样化且等价的有效实现，且迭代速度快于刚性流水线。这些特性使得现有静态评估方法无法准确捕捉前端生成质量。

Method: LiveEvalBench将评估实例化为一个协作审查工作流：构建工程师、代码工程师和UI测试员三个角色分别负责部署、代码检查和基于浏览器的交互，共同收集证据。一个自适应协议将共享评分准则（用于跨模型可比性）与实现特定标准（针对每个产物定制）相结合。框架支持增量集成新的评估角色和评估维度，无需重新设计流水线。

Result: 在多样化的真实世界web生成场景实验表明，LiveEvalBench与人类专家判断高度一致，并能对前沿模型的web生成能力提供细粒度的洞察。

Conclusion: LiveEvalBench证明了一种智能体化、自适应和可扩展的评估范式更适合前端项目生成任务，为web生成评估提供了超越静态基准的新方向。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly capable of synthesizing executable frontend projects, yet existing benchmarks still treat web generation as a static evaluation problem. We argue that frontend artifacts demand a different paradigm: they are interactive rather than static, admit diverse yet equally valid implementations, and evolve faster than rigid pipelines can accommodate. To address these gaps, we present LiveEvalBench, an automated framework that reformulates web-generation evaluation as an agentic, adaptive, and extensible process. LiveEvalBench instantiates evaluation as a collaborative review workflow, in which a Build Engineer, a Code Engineer, and a UI Tester collectively gather evidence across the full lifecycle of a frontend project, from deployment and code inspection to browser-based interaction. To handle implementation diversity, an adaptive protocol couples shared rubrics for cross-model comparability with implementation-grounded criteria tailored to each artifact. The framework further supports incremental integration of new evaluator roles and assessment dimensions without pipeline redesign. Experiments across diverse real-world web-generation scenarios show that LiveEvalBench aligns closely with human expert judgment and provides fine-grained insights into frontier models' web generation capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/wyysteelhead/LiveEvalBench

</details>


### [214] [TARL: Transaction-Aware Reliable Ledgers for Executable Memory Management in Long-Term Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03699)
*Han Xiao,Hongjun Xu,Xin Zhang,Yidong Chen,Xiaodong Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TARL，一个将记忆更新从二元 Write/Hold 决策细化为五种可执行动作（添加、忽略、修订、拒绝、延迟）的持久记忆状态更新框架，并引入 TARL-Mem 基准。通过对比不同更新操作产生的记忆状态进行训练，TARL 在多种评估场景下显著提升动作预测和状态恢复能力，减少记忆污染和累积错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持久记忆系统将记忆更新简化为二元 Write/Hold 决策，无法区分新信息应当被添加、忽略、用于修订过时信念、拒绝为不可靠，还是延迟验证。这些不同的操作可能共享相同的二元标签，却产生根本不同的记忆状态，导致单次更新错误会反复扭曲未来的检索和推理。

Method: TARL 通过四个步骤实现记忆状态更新：识别受影响的记忆、解析其时间范围、比较来源可靠性、更新 accepted/pending/rejected 三个登记簿。训练阶段通过比较不同替代更新操作产生的记忆状态，鼓励模型选择能导向正确结果的操作。同时构建了 TARL-Mem 基准，包含细粒度动作标签和下一状态目标。

Result: 在域内、跨来源、时间、反事实和顺序评估五类场景中，TARL 在动作预测和状态恢复方面均有提升，有效减少了记忆污染，保留了冲突证据，并限制了累积性记忆损坏。

Conclusion: 将记忆更新从二元决策细化为多动作分类并基于目标状态进行训练，是提升长期代理记忆系统可靠性的有效途径。模型实现已开源提供。

Abstract: Persistent memory helps long-term agents retain knowledge, yet a single update error can repeatedly distort future retrieval and reasoning. Most existing systems reduce memory updating to a binary Write/Hold decision, which cannot distinguish whether new information should be added, ignored, used to revise an outdated belief, rejected as unreliable, or deferred for verification. These choices may share the same binary label while producing fundamentally different memory states. We introduce TARL, a memory state update framework that maps each statement to one of five executable actions. TARL identifies the affected memory, resolves its temporal scope, compares source reliability, and updates accepted, pending, and rejected ledgers. It is further trained by comparing the memory states produced by alternative update operations, encouraging the model to select the operation that leads to the correct result. We also introduce TARL-Mem, a benchmark with fine-grained action labels and next-state targets. Across in-domain, cross-source, temporal, counterfactual, and sequential evaluations, TARL improves action prediction and state recovery, reduces memory pollution, preserves conflicting evidence, and limits cumulative corruption. The complete model implementation is provided in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [215] [Less Traffic, Better Outcomes: Competition-Aware Request Dispatch in Real-Time Ad Exchanges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03705)
*Jonaid Shianifar,Blaz Mramor,Fangda Zou,Matthieu C. Martin,Xingsheng Guo,Zhihua Zhu,Rong Zhou,Bichen Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种竞争感知的请求分发框架，用于实时竞价广告交易系统。该框架通过分布式竞价预测和概率转发来决定是否将每个请求发送给各个DSP，并通过轻量级策略优化动态调整每DSP阈值以适应非平稳市场。在生产平台（日请求量超200亿）上通过四次在线实验验证，多DSP部署在降低34.2%的DSP请求量的同时使净收入提升4.6%（p<0.001）。进一步分析揭示了流量段间的强异质性，表明聚合指标可能具有误导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RTB广告交易平台通常将几乎所有请求转发给DSP，但实际只有一小部分会产生出价，导致过度分发。DSP在计算和预算约束下会抑制参与，浪费了有限的竞价容量，削弱了拍卖效果。需要一种方法来优化请求分发决策。

Method: 提出竞争感知的请求分发框架，核心技术包括：(1) 分布式竞价预测，用于估计每个请求对每个DSP的价值分布；(2) 概率转发机制，决定是否将请求发送给各DSP；(3) 轻量级策略优化，自适应调整每个DSP的阈值，以追踪非平稳的市场条件。

Result: 在日处理200亿+请求的生产平台上进行了四轮序列在线实验。完整多DSP部署后，策略下DSP请求量减少34.2%，同时净收入增加4.6%（p<0.001），在14天窗口内观察到效果。进一步分析显示流量段间存在强烈异质性，聚合指标可能掩盖真实情况；段级和DSP级分析表明策略揭示了DSP间的比较优势，在不增加总请求量的情况下改善了变现效果。

Conclusion: 竞争感知请求分发框架能够有效减少不必要的请求分发，提升RTB系统的总体净收入。该框架在真实生产环境中经过验证，且段级分析表明其能够挖掘DSP的差异化竞争优势。同时，论文强调了在评估此类系统时需关注异质性和避免依赖聚合指标的重要性。

Abstract: Real-time bidding (RTB) ad exchanges typically forward nearly all incoming requests to demand-side platforms (DSPs), even though only a small fraction receive bids. This over-distribution weakens auction outcomes: DSPs throttle participation under compute and budget constraints, reducing the effective use of limited bidding capacity. We present a competition-aware request dispatch framework that uses distributional bid prediction and probabilistic forwarding to decide whether each request should be sent to each DSP. The system adapts per-DSP thresholds over time through lightweight policy optimization to track non-stationary market conditions. We evaluate the framework through four sequential online experiments on a production platform serving over 20 billion daily requests. A full multi-DSP deployment reduces DSP request volume under the policy by 34.2% while increasing net revenue by 4.6% (p<0.001) in a recent 14-day window after an initial DSP adaptation period. Further analysis highlights strong heterogeneity across traffic segments and reveals that aggregate metrics can be misleading. Segment-level and per-DSP analyses suggest that the policy surfaces comparative advantages among DSPs, improving monetized outcomes without increasing overall request volume.

</details>


### [216] [When Outputs Disperse, Does Epistemic Revision Follow? A Black-Box Coupling Diagnostic for Machine Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03722)
*Molood Arman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"离散-修订耦合"诊断框架，用于检测LLM集体中看似多样化的输出是否真正改变认知立场，而非仅以不同表述保留相同结论。通过双通道测量（输出通道的Coherence Index与认知通道的逐轮立场标注），并引入Meta-Predictive Clarity System（含Re-Differentiation Protocol）作为估计该耦合机制的复用方法。在两种模型配置上的实验表明，条件性异见在GPT上显著提升虚假前提恢复率（+17.7分），但对Gemini无效（即使输出离散度确已变化），揭示后者通过框架内异见保留虚假前提（94%重表述vs 24%让步）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体智慧研究传统上将分歧视为认知多样性的证据，但在LLM集体中这一代理指标可能失效：模型能生成外观多样的论证却维持相同结论。因此需要一种黑盒诊断方法，区分"真正的立场修订"与"保留前提的重表述"。

Method: 提出黑盒诊断框架（仅基于生成的文本，不访问模型内部表征）。两条独立测量通道：输出通道用Coherence Index (CI)验证干预是否改变输出离散度；认知通道用逐轮立场标注测量集体是否真正修订。在此基础上构建Meta-Predictive Clarity System (MPCS)，当输出过度收敛时插入Re-Differentiation Protocol (RDP)。

Result: 在gpt-4o-mini上，条件性异见使虚假前提恢复率提升+17.7分（p<1e-6），而静态人格多样性反而损害恢复（-8.1，p=.007）。在gemini-2.5-flash上，相同干预在可比预算下无增益（26.1% vs 27.1%，p=.84），尽管验证了输出离散度下降，但两模型的处理效应显著不同（z=3.79，p<.001）。机制标记显示Gemini通过框架内异见保留虚假前提：94%的标记后RDP响应为重表述而非让步（GPT仅为24%）。

Conclusion: LLM集体中的输出多样性可能仅是外观层面的，不能直接等同于认知多样性。建议在报告准确性的同时，补充逐干预立场转变率与前提保留率指标，以更可靠地评估集体修订能力。

Abstract: Collective intelligence research treats disagreement as evidence of epistemic diversity: if agents express different views, the group should retain capacity to revise. In LLM collectives this proxy can break: agents can produce diverse-looking arguments while preserving the same conclusion. We operationalize dispersion-revision coupling: the degree to which an intervention that verifiably increases the dispersion of a collective's outputs in embedding space is accompanied by genuine revision of its epistemic stance rather than premise-preserving reformulation. The diagnostic is black-box: it operates on generated text alone and makes no claims about the internal representations of the generating models. Two channels are measured independently: an output channel, the Coherence Index (CI), verifies that the intervention changed output dispersion; an epistemic channel, per-turn stance annotation, measures whether the collective revised. We propose CI with the Meta-Predictive Clarity System (MPCS), which inserts a Re-Differentiation Protocol (RDP) when outputs over-converge, as a reusable method for estimating this coupling regime. We evaluate five-agent collectives from two configurations (gpt-4o-mini and gemini-2.5-flash; 310 paired episodes per condition). On gpt-4o-mini, conditional dissent improves false-premise recovery by +17.7 points (p<1e-6) while static persona diversity harms recovery (-8.1, p=.007). On gemini-2.5-flash, the same intervention at a comparable budget yields no gain (26.1% vs 27.1%, p=.84) despite a verified dispersion drop; the two treatment effects differ from each other (z=3.79, p<.001). Mechanism tagging shows Gemini preserves the false premise via intra-framework dissent: 94% of tagged post-RDP responses reformulate rather than concede (vs 24% on GPT). We recommend reporting per-intervention stance shift and premise-preservation rate alongside accuracy.

</details>


### [217] [SAT-Edge-Agent: Hardware-in-the-Loop Edge-Agent Orchestration for Onboard Satellite Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03728)
*Longji He,Jeto Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍SAT-Edge-Agent，一个部署在商用ARM异构边缘SoC上的星上边缘智能体系统，通过浏览器工作区、FastAPI代理和本地OpenAI兼容语言服务，配合YOLO风格定向目标检测端点，在硬件在环环境中完成卫星任务调度。两个FAIR1M工作负载各重复20次均成功，平均完整Agent延迟为29.353秒和60.937秒，但大部分延迟出现在检测器执行之外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星载卫星智能需要任务层将任务意图转化为本地工具调用，暴露执行状态，并在通信和功耗约束下返回机器可消费的产物。当前缺乏在真实硬件环境下可复现的星载边缘Agent编排系统验证。

Method: 基于商用ARM异构边缘SoC部署硬件在环（HIL）边缘Agent系统。架构包括浏览器工作区、FastAPI代理、本地OpenAI兼容语言服务，以及返回FAIR1M元数据支持的结构化结果的YOLO风格定向目标检测端点。设计了两个固定FAIR1M工作负载（单图像和串行双图像请求），各重复20次进行性能剖析。

Result: 所有40次请求全部成功（20/20每工作负载）。平均Full-Agent延迟为29.353秒（单图）和60.937秒（双图），经验P95为31.166秒和66.882秒。平均检测器时间为861.386毫秒和1510.920毫秒，仅占完整Agent均值的2.93%和2.48%。平均CPU利用率约20.5-20.8%。200毫秒NPU负载字段平均100%，但仅代表共享加速器软件字段而非校准占用率。

Conclusion: 该工作为可观测的星载边缘Agent编排建立了可复现的硬件在环基准，但明确不声称检测器精度、新的地理定位方法、校准能效或飞行就绪性。

Abstract: Onboard satellite intelligence requires a task layer that translates mission intent into local tool calls, exposes execution state, and returns machine-consumable artifacts under communication and power constraints. We present SAT-Edge-Agent, a hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) edge-agent system deployed on a commercial off-the-shelf ARM-based heterogeneous edge system-on-chip. A browser workspace and FastAPI agent coordinate a local OpenAI-compatible language service with a project-internal YOLO-style oriented-object-detection endpoint that returns FAIR1M metadata-backed structured results. Two fixed FAIR1M workloads, one single-image and one serial two-image request, were repeated 20 times each and completed 20/20 attempts. Mean Full-Agent latency was 29.353 s and 60.937 s, with empirical P95 values of 31.166 s and 66.882 s. Mean detector time was 861.386 ms and 1510.920 ms, only 2.93% and 2.48% of the corresponding Full-Agent means. Profiling indicates that most visible latency occurs outside detector execution. Mean CPU utilization was 20.761% and 20.482%. A 200-ms NPU-load field averaged 100% for both workloads, but it represents a shared-accelerator software field rather than detector-only occupancy or calibrated utilization. The public evidence package provides sanitized request-level records, redacted JSON, normalized SSE examples, and scripts reproducing the reported statistics. These results establish a reproducible HIL boundary for observable satellite edge-agent orchestration, but do not establish detector accuracy, a new geolocation method, calibrated energy efficiency, or flight readiness.

</details>


### [218] [CARE-Bench: Benchmarking Patient-Facing LLM Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03731)
*Yining Hua,Hongbin Na,Cyrus Ayubcha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE-Bench，一个基于来源证据的基准测试，用于评估面向患者的医疗大语言模型在顺序分诊任务中的表现。该基准包含500个案例和1059个评估前缀，采用四标签当前-动作分类任务。在269轮测试中评估了11个模型，结果显示即使经过提示优化，模型的宏F1分数仍然较低（46.9-63.4），且存在严重的时序性错误——在需要进一步询问信息时，只有33.5%的模型输出保留了该步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向患者的医疗LLM在临床接触前就被用来回答症状问题，关键的安全问题在于模型建议用户采取什么行动。现有的基准缺乏对顺序性分诊决策和动作时序的显式评估，因此需要一个新的基准来系统性地衡量这些模型在分诊中的安全性。

Method: CARE-Bench将患者分诊建模为四标签（per-turn current-action）的顺序任务。基准数据来自医疗对话、咨询和随访问题来源重建的500个案例和1059个患者披露前缀。作者在未提示和最小提示两种开放式协议下评估11个模型共269轮，使用固定的GPT-5.5映射器将模型输出编码到四标签动作空间。

Result: 未提示条件下宏F1分数在31.2-50.4之间，处于低位。提示优化改善了11个模型中的10个，使宏F1提升至46.9-63.4，但仍存在大量阈值错误。当正确答案是要求更多信息时，提示后仅有33.5%的输出保留了该步骤，模型倾向于过早推荐护理措施。

Conclusion: 提示优化后的持续错误表明，面向患者的医疗分诊并非简单的提示工程问题，需要在部署前对动作时序进行显式评估。研究支持在临床部署前引入针对动作时机的专门评估机制。

Abstract: Patient-facing medical LLMs and agents increasingly answer symptom questions before clinician contact, where the key safety question is what action the user should take next. We introduce CARE-Bench, a source-grounded benchmark that evaluates sequential patient-facing triage as a four-label per-turn current-action task. CARE-Bench contains 500 cases and 1,059 evaluated patient-disclosure prefixes reconstructed from medical dialogue, consultation, and follow-up-question sources. We evaluate 11 models on 269 held-out rounds under unprompted and minimally prompted open-ended protocols, using a fixed GPT-5.5 mapper to code each response into the four-label action space. Unprompted macro-F1 remains low, ranging from 31.2 to 50.4. Prompting improves 10 of 11 models, with prompted macro-F1 ranging from 46.9 to 63.4, but substantial threshold errors remain. Prompted models often recommend care before needed clarification is obtained; when the correct action was to ask for more information, only 33.5% of prompted outputs preserved the step. The persistence of these errors after prompting suggests that patient-facing triage is not a simple prompting problem and supports explicit evaluation of action timing before deployment.

</details>


### [219] [Failure-Informed Image Self-Augmentation for Multimodal Large Language Model Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03733)
*Chunyang Jiang,Pingping Zhang,Yuzhi Zhao,Wenao Ma,Zhijian Hou,Mengyang Wu,Yiyang Cai,Senkang Hu,Sitong Cheng,Chi-Min Chan,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FISA框架从模型自身的失败案例中构建图像增强数据，通过生成视觉上具有挑战性但答案保持不变的图像，配合自检与双保真度过滤策略，在不依赖外部监督的情况下提升多模态大语言模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型依赖大规模高质量标注数据，成本高昂。现有的自增强方法以文本为中心，而图像增强要么使用通用变换要么使用手工变换，与模型的实际能力短板缺乏对齐。

Method: FISA框架包括三个核心步骤：(1) 从模型自身的失败案例中生成视觉上具有挑战性但答案保持不变的图像；(2) 通过自我检查（self-examination）验证生成图像的有效性；(3) 应用双保真度过滤（dual fidelity filtering）以避免语义失真。

Result: 在视觉问答基准上，FISA在分布内（in-distribution）和分布外（out-of-distribution）场景下均一致地提升了模型性能。同时验证了其与现有文本自增强方法的兼容性、优于通用图像增强基线的数据效率，以及所提过滤策略的实际有效性。

Conclusion: FISA证明了从失败中学习（failure-informed）的图像自增强范式是有效的，为多模态模型的自主改进提供了一条无需外部标注的新路径，且能与既有文本增强方法协同工作。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across vision-language tasks, but their progress depends heavily on large-scale, high-quality multimodal data that are costly to annotate. Self-augmentation offers a promising alternative by enabling models to expand their own training data without external supervision. However, existing MLLM self-augmentation methods are largely text-centric, while image augmentation remains underexplored and typically relies on generic or handcrafted transformations that are weakly aligned with the model's actual incapability. We propose Failure-informed Image Self-Augmentation (\textbf{FISA}), a framework for MLLM self-improvement that constructs augmented images from the model's own failure cases. Our method generates visually challenging yet answer-preserving image complications, verifies their utility through self-examination, and applies dual fidelity filtering to avoid semantic distortion. Experiments on visual question answering benchmarks show that the proposed method consistently improves performance across both in-distribution and out-of-distribution settings. Further experiments validate the compatibility of FISA with existing textual self-augmentation approaches, the superior data efficiency of the synthesized samples over generic image augmentation baselines, and the practical effectiveness of the proposed filtering strategy.

</details>


### [220] [AgenticECO: An Agentic Framework for ECO on 3D Integrated Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03738)
*Shuo Ren,Yaohui Han,Libo Shen,Zhiqiang Jia,Rongliang Fu,Bei Yu,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgenticECO，一个证据门控的工具使用智能体工作流，用于3D-IC的工程变更指令（ECO），配合EcoRoute最小扰动ECO布线层。在九个匹配的自然缺陷案例中，AgenticECO清除七个，而全重布线和现有修复方案仅清除两个，平均扰动0.66%，零时钟网络触碰；在跨骨干重跑下全部九个均清除。受控实验、预注册视觉研究和盲诊断实验均验证了其有效性，所有验收结果通过布线、时序、DRC和结构等价门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摩尔定律放缓使工业界转向三维集成，但3D-IC布线后的设计中会暴露无2D对应的键合级缺陷；现有的后布线ECO仍依赖手工和专家经验。更糟糕的是，标准的"先编辑再完全重布线"流程将修复与布线器扰动纠缠在一起，导致签核数字无法归因于引起它的编辑操作。因此需要一个可归因、最小扰动的自动化ECO方法。

Method: 提出AgenticECO：一个证据门控的工具使用智能体工作流，运行在开源TaiWei流程上；配套EcoRoute，一个最小扰动ECO布线层，驱动未修改的固定布线器，使得修复操作可归因于编辑。通过证据门控机制控制代理决策，通过占空感知选择合法落脚点，通过跨骨干重跑验证契约一致性。

Result: 在九例匹配的自然缺陷案例中，相同预算下AgenticECO清除七例（全重布线和现有修复方案均仅清除两例），平均扰动0.66%，零时钟网络触碰，跨骨干重跑清除全部九例。受控研究表明修复动作在保持下是必要的、占空感知选择带来合法着陆而非修复成功、收紧时钟时最小扰动使"接受vs拒绝"翻转。三个预注册视觉研究定位像素工具的边界，一个预注册盲诊断精确恢复所有被注入缺陷，是唯一零错误编辑的分支。所有验收结果通过布线、提取、最大/最小时序、DRC和结构等价门控。

Conclusion: AgenticECO结合EcoRoute有效解决了3D-IC后布线ECO中修复与布线扰动纠缠的问题，实现了可归因、最小扰动的自动修复，在修复成功率上大幅超越现有方案，且所有结果均通过完整签核验证。代码、环境和逐episode审计工件已作为补充材料发布。

Abstract: As Moore's law slows, the industry is turning to three-dimensional integration; yet in merged 3D-IC flows, routed designs expose bond-level defects with no 2D analogue, and post-route engineering change orders (ECO) remain manual, expertise-bound work. Worse, the standard edit-then-fully-reroute practice entangles a repair with router churn, so a signoff number cannot be attributed to the edit that motivated it. We present AgenticECO, an evidence-gated tool-using agent workflow for 3D-IC ECO on the open-source TaiWei flow, paired with EcoRoute, a minimal-disturbance ECO-routing layer that drives the unmodified pinned router so a repair is attributable to its edit. Across nine matched natural defect cases under identical budgets, AgenticECO clears seven versus two for both full reroute and stock repair, at 0.66\% mean disturbance over cleared cases and zero clock nets touched, and a cross-backbone rerun under the same sealed contract clears all nine. Controlled studies show that the repair moves are necessary under preservation, that occupancy-aware choice buys legal landings rather than repair success, and that under tightened clocks minimal disturbance flips accept versus reject. Three preregistered visual studies localize the pixel instrument's edge to contested landing sites, and a preregistered blind diagnostic exactly restores every held-out injected defect, the only arm with zero wrong edits. Every accepted result passes routing, fresh extraction, max/min timing, DRC, and structural-equivalence gates. Code, environment, and per-episode audit artifacts are released as supplementary material.

</details>


### [221] [MissClick: Exploiting Digit-Serialized Coordinates to Attack GUI Grounding Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03740)
*Yu Ran,Wentao Zhao,Xin Zhang,Yi Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MissClick，一种针对GUI视觉定位模型的白盒对抗攻击方法。通过利用坐标数字标记转换为数值后数量级差异的特性，分别提出MissClick-U（软坐标位移最大化）用于非目标攻击和MissClick-T（位置加权目标数字损失最小化）用于目标攻击，在OS-Atlas和UGround上超过现有攻击方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI视觉定位模型将屏幕坐标生成为数字标记序列，解析后转换为可执行点击。然而，坐标数字的类别预测与数值解析之间存在安全盲区：例如百位数字变化1个单位会导致坐标漂移100像素，造成大幅度的点击位移。现有攻击方法将坐标当作普通文本处理，忽略了坐标输出的数值结构和位置（place-value）特性，且未针对目标攻击与非目标攻击的不同成功条件设计差异化目标。

Method: 提出MissClick白盒对抗攻击框架，包含两个目标特定的目标函数：MissClick-U最大化软坐标位移（soft-coordinate displacement），用于非目标攻击以将点击移出正确区域；MissClick-T最小化位置加权目标数字损失（place-weighted target-digit loss），用于目标攻击以将点击引导至攻击者指定区域。

Result: 在OS-Atlas和UGround模型上，跨桌面、网页和移动平台：MissClick-U的非目标攻击成功率达75.07%和72.93%（较基线提高16.62和30.72个百分点）；MissClick-T的目标攻击成功率达44.86%和62.67%（较基线提高31.73和47.06个百分点）。目标函数对比实验进一步验证了软坐标位移对非目标攻击最优、位置加权目标数字优化对目标攻击最优。

Conclusion: 攻击目标的成功条件决定了最优攻击目标函数的选择：非目标攻击倾向于最大化软坐标位移，而目标攻击倾向于位置加权目标数字优化。该工作揭示了GUI坐标生成过程中的安全漏洞，呼吁对该类模型的坐标生成机制进行安全加固。

Abstract: Recent GUI visual grounding models generate screen coordinates as sequences of digit tokens that are parsed into numerical values and mapped to executable clicks. The security implications of this coordinate generation process have been largely overlooked. We observe that each coordinate digit is predicted as a categorical token, yet after parsing, changing a hundreds-place digit by one changes the corresponding numerical coordinate component by 100 units, which can induce a large displacement of the executed click. This observation motivates attack objectives that account for the numerical and place-value structure of coordinate outputs rather than treating them as ordinary text. Moreover, untargeted and targeted attacks impose different success conditions--displacing the click outside the correct region versus into an attacker-specified region--and therefore benefit from different objectives. We propose MissClick, a simple and effective white-box adversarial attack with two goal-specific objectives: MissClick-U maximizes soft-coordinate displacement for untargeted disruption, while MissClick-T minimizes a place-weighted target-digit loss for targeted hijacking. Compared with existing attacks against GUI grounding models on OS-Atlas and UGround across desktop, web, and mobile platforms, MissClick-U achieves untargeted success rates of 75.07\% and 72.93\% (+16.62 and +30.72 pp), and MissClick-T achieves targeted success rates of 44.86\% and 62.67\% (+31.73 and +47.06 pp). Attack objective comparison further shows that soft-coordinate displacement yields the highest untargeted attack success rate, whereas place-weighted target-digit optimization yields the highest targeted attack success rate, revealing distinct objective preferences for the two attack goals.

</details>


### [222] [Agents Catching Agents: Shortcut Cascades and Benchmark Gaming in Clinical Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03744)
*Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez,Agastya Munnangi,Aldo Marzullo,Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Quang Bui,Mohammad Shahin,Armaan Grewal,Emmanuel Paul Kwesiga,Anqi Peter Li,Josephine Nanyonjo,Aaditya Panchal,Arshnoor Bhutani,Nikhil Jaiswal,Milit S. Patel,Maximin Lange,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言模型智能体（LLM代理委员会）在临床决策支持中如何被"捷径"（shortcuts）博弈。作者在6个公共数据集、7个队列（文本、影像、表格ICU记录）上测试发现：委员会对孤立线索具有抵抗力（翻转率5-16%），但社会性合理捷径（两个同行坚持同一错误答案、虚假"预筛"系统标志）会显著传播（38%采用率）。三种监督代理中，门控器无法区分采纳与诚实同意，同源裁判在文本上有效但影像上失效，而独立重新查询的裁判在影像上转移良好。提高线索视觉显著性不影响传染，但增加第二个同行声音使传染率提升50%。游戏隐藏评分标准几乎无声（10个文本漂移者中仅1个、134个影像漂移者中仅1个提及）。结论：博弈委员会的关键是社会合理性，只有独立于自我报告的裁判才能捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策支持正转向共享工作空间上多个语言模型代理的委员会式审议。作者想知道这种委员会是否能被捷径（benchmark奖励但临床医生会忽略的线索）所博弈，以评估其临床安全性与可靠性。

Method: 在6个公共数据集（MedQA-USMLE、MedMCQA、MIMIC-CXR报告、NIH ChestX-ray14、MIMIC-CXR-JPG、CheXpert、SUPPORT2）的7个队列上测试Gemini委员会。实验设计包括：孤立线索测试、两个同行共同主张的社交传染测试、虚假"预筛"标志测试、三种监督代理（门控器、同源裁判、独立重新查询裁判）的检测能力比较、视觉显著性操控和同行数量操控。

Result: 委员会对孤立线索抵抗（翻转率5-16%），但社交捷径（两个同行相同错误答案或虚假预筛标志）在两种能力级别上均导致38%采用率。门控器假阳性率100%；同源裁判在文本上精确率100%、召回率93%但在影像上失效；独立重新查询裁判在影像上77-88%精确率、13-21%假阳性率。视觉显著性三倍不影响传染，第二个同行声音使传染率提高50%。隐藏评分标准博弈几乎不可检测（文本1/10、影像1/134漂移者提及）。

Conclusion: 博弈委员会的核心机制是社会合理性（social plausibility）而非线索显著性。只有独立于自我报告的裁判（独立重新查询）能够有效检测采用行为，特别是在影像领域。这为临床LLM代理委员会的安全监督提供了重要设计原则。

Abstract: Clinical decision support is moving toward committees of language-model agents deliberating on a shared workspace. We ask whether such committees can be gamed by shortcuts, cues a benchmark rewards but a clinician would ignore. Across seven cohorts on six public datasets spanning text (MedQA-USMLE, MedMCQA, MIMIC-CXR reports), imaging (NIH ChestX-ray14, MIMIC-CXR-JPG, CheXpert) and tabular ICU records (SUPPORT2), Gemini committees resist these cues in isolation (flip 5-16%), yet a socially plausible shortcut spreads: when two peers assert the same wrong answer, the holdout under test adopts it in 38% of cases, as does a false "pre-screen" system flag, on both capability tiers. Of three oversight agents, a gate cannot separate adoption from honest agreement (false-positive rate 100%); a same-lineage judge reading only the transcript flags adoption on text (precision 100%, recall 93%) but collapses onto the gate in imaging; a referee that privately re-queries the holdout transfers to imaging (77-88% precision, 13-21% false-positive rate). Tripling a cue's visual salience does not move contagion, whereas a second peer voice raises it by half again. Gaming a hidden rubric is near-silent: only 1/10 text and 1/134 imaging drifters name the rubric they moved toward. What games a committee is social plausibility, and only a referee independent of self-report catches it. Code: https://github.com/criticaldata/benchmaxxing

</details>


### [223] [Risky Business: Measuring The Faithfulness-Safety Tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03745)
*Dominik Meier,Luca Joshua Francis,Marco Bernhard Kaiser,Terry Ruas,Jan Philip Wahle,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现大推理模型（LRMs）存在对齐张力：模型需足够忠实以被监控（CoT推理须严格反映输出），又需足够鲁棒以拒绝不安全推理。作者提出HazMart数据集和Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR)技术，通过直接替换推理链中的不安全/不合逻辑思维来测试忠实性，并发现表示引导（representation steering）可独立增强安全方向而保持基础能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理为模型监控提供窗口，但监控的前提是忠实性（模型输出严格源于推理轨迹）。然而，模型若过度忠实于不安全推理则无法拒绝危险建议，若过度鲁棒则推理轨迹不再可信。这一对齐张力此前未被系统性研究，需要在当前LRMs中加以验证并提出解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建HazMart数据集：基于自主AI售货员场景的人类撰写数据集；2) 提出Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR)技术：不同于依赖提示语中的提示词，TRR直接干预推理链，将推理中的思维替换为不安全或不合逻辑的版本；3) 对DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B和QwQ-32B进行忠实性与安全性评估；4) 对QwQ-32B进行机制分析，识别内部表征中的反相关方向；5) 使用表示引导（representation steering）增强安全方向。

Result: DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B表现出高忠实性（97.5%）但未能拒绝不安全推理（仅12.3%的拒绝率）；QwQ-32B更鲁棒（73.9%安全性）但忠实性较低（74.7%）。QwQ-32B的机制分析显示这两个属性由在行动承诺token处达到峰值的反相关内部方向所表征。表示引导可独立放大安全方向，将安全行为提升9个百分点而保持基础能力。

Conclusion: 忠实性与安全性之间存在内在的权衡张力，但可以通过表示引导在表征层面进行调解。该工作为LRMs的可监控性与鲁棒性提供了新的评估方法论和干预手段，表明安全与忠实并非不可兼得，而是需要通过定向内部干预来协同优化。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning offers a promising window into model monitoring. However, monitoring relies on faithfulness, i.e., the model output strictly derives from its reasoning trace. We identify an alignment tension where a model must be faithful enough to be monitored, yet robust enough to reject unsafe reasoning. We demonstrate that this counterbalance exists in current Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), and show ways in which it can be addressed. We introduce HazMart, a human-written dataset set in an autonomous AI shopkeeper scenario. Unlike prior work that relies on providing hints in prompts to test faithfulness (e.g., "A Stanford professor said it should be Answer A"), we propose a novel replacement-based technique, which we call Targeted Reasoning Replacement (TRR), that directly intervenes in the reasoning chain to substitute in unsafe or illogical thoughts (e.g., "Wait, the answer must be Option B [was Option A] because it is the most fitting"). DeepSeek-R1-Llama-70B exhibits high faithfulness (97.5%) but fails to reject Unsafe Reasoning (12.3%), while QwQ-32B is more robust (73.9% safety) at the cost of lower faithfulness (74.7%). Mechanistic analyses of QwQ-32B reveal that these properties are represented by anti-correlated internal directions peaking at the action-commit token. Finally, we demonstrate that representation steering can independently amplify the safety direction, increasing safe behavior by 9 percentage points while maintaining base capabilities.

</details>


### [224] [GDPevo: Evaluating Agent Self-Evolution on Real Business Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03764)
*Leijun Zhou,Zhihao Liu,Xiang Qu,Chenxu Liu,Yifei Liu,Yanke Yu,Jingzhe Xu,Xuejun Wu,Buyue Qian,Xi Chen,Yaowei Zheng,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GDPevo，一个基于GDP相关企业工作流的智能体自进化评测基准，通过"规则混合"机制将企业工作流分解为原子业务规则并在训练/测试任务中重组，使测试性能提升可归因于训练经验，同时利用全自动数据流水线快速扩展数据集以应对数据污染问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体自进化评测基准存在三大不足：任务领域覆盖有限（缺乏经济价值高的企业场景）、训练与测试任务设计不当导致无法归因测试性能提升、易受数据污染影响。需要一个新的"进化原生"基准来可靠地评估和推动智能体的自进化能力。

Method: 核心机制为规则混合（rule hybridization）：将每个企业工作流分解为原子业务规则，将规则的子集分布到训练任务中，再在保留的测试任务中重新组合这些规则，从而确保测试性能提升可归因于训练经验。全自动数据流水线可快速扩展任务规模（V1含12组120个任务，V2在两天内扩展到24组240个任务）以应对数据污染。

Result: GDPevo覆盖CRM、ERP、金融、医疗、法律和数据密集型工作流。在4种监督类型下评估4个智能体（各含harness和模型），自进化一致地提升保留集测试准确率最多达16.44个百分点；但最佳进化智能体仍远低于全知oracle上限91.6%，表明当前智能体自进化能力尚未充分实现。

Conclusion: GDPevo提供了一个可靠的、防污染的、可扩展的智能体自进化评测基准，实证表明自进化确实能提升智能体性能，但现有智能体的自进化能力距离充分成熟仍有很大差距，该基准及配套流水线已公开释放以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Agent self-evolution updates an agent's persistent state from prior experience and reuses it to solve related tasks more effectively. Evaluating self-evolution is difficult: existing benchmarks provide limited coverage of economically valuable task domains, do not always design training and test tasks such that test-time gains can be attributed to training experience, and remain vulnerable to data contamination. We present GDPevo, an evolution-native benchmark grounded in GDP-related enterprise workflows, together with the fully automated data pipeline that generates it. Its core mechanism, rule hybridization, decomposes each enterprise workflow into atomic business rules, distributes subsets of these rules across training tasks, and recombines them in held-out test tasks so that test-time gains are attributable. GDPevo spans CRM, ERP, finance, healthcare, legal, and data-centric workflows. Its V1 release contains 120 tasks in 12 groups, with five training and five held-out test tasks per group. Full automation enables the pipeline to expand the suite to 240 tasks in 24 groups (V2) within two days, providing a practical response to contamination. Using GDPevo, we evaluate four agents, each comprising a harness and a model, under four supervision types. Self-evolution consistently improves held-out accuracy by up to 16.44 percentage points. But the best evolved agents remain far below the fully informed oracle ceiling of 91.6%, indicating that the self-evolution ability of current agents remains far from fully realized. We publicly release the pipeline, benchmark, and full evaluation results at https://github.com/Prism-Shadow/GDPevo.

</details>


### [225] [Computing Actual Causes for Neural Network Predictions under Structured Causal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03772)
*Jannick Strobel,Muqsit Azeem,Stefan Leue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经网络预测的解释形式化为Halpern-Pearl (HP)实际原因，使用布尔结构化因果模型（SCMs）建模输入依赖关系，并通过边界传播和分支定界技术计算HP原因，在可扩展性和完备性上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的解释方法（如特征归因或最小充分集）通常假设输入特征相互独立，当输入存在结构化依赖时会产生误导性解释。因此需要一种能够显式建模输入依赖关系的解释框架。

Method: 将解释形式化为Halpern-Pearl (HP)实际原因，使用布尔结构化因果模型（SCMs）建模输入特征之间的依赖关系；通过边界传播（bound propagation）和分支定界（branch-and-bound）技术计算HP原因，并提供完备性和最小性的形式化保证。

Result: 在可扩展性方面大幅优于暴力搜索和ILP基线，在图规模增长时优于启发式搜索；在搜索空间高达2.3×10^13候选（原因、反事实）对的SCM上（最多28个节点），能在180秒/实例的预算内计算出所有最小实际原因。案例研究表明，忽略输入依赖会膨胀报告的原因数量，其中14.9%的原因在我们的SCM下是虚假的。

Conclusion: 通过形式化HP因果解释并显式建模输入依赖，能够在保证完备性和最小性的前提下显著提升计算可扩展性，并且忽略输入依赖会导致大量虚假解释，验证了建模输入依赖的重要性。

Abstract: Explaining the predictions of neural networks is a central challenge in trustworthy AI. Existing explanation methods, such as those based on feature attribution or minimal sufficient sets, typically treat input features as independent, which can yield misleading explanations when inputs exhibit structured dependencies. We address this by formalizing explanations as Halpern-Pearl (HP) actual causes, modeling input dependencies using Boolean Structural Causal Models (SCMs). We compute HP causes by applying bound propagation and branch-and-bound techniques, while providing formal guarantees of completeness and minimality. Our experiments show that we substantially outperform brute-force and ILP baselines in scalability, and outperform heuristic search as graph size grows, computing all minimal actual causes on instances with search spaces of up to $2.3\times10^{13}$ candidate (cause, contingency) pairs, on SCMs with up to 28 nodes, within a 180s per-instance budget. In a case study, we further show that ignoring input dependencies inflates the number of reported causes, 14.9% of which are spurious under our SCM.

</details>


### [226] [KnowHal: A Knowledge-Driven Benchmark for Comprehensive Multimodal Hallucination Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03782)
*Ruihan Li,Jiyang Tan,Kailin Jiang,Huining Li,Hengyang Lu,Yu Huang,Qian Li,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KnowHal，一个将知识幻觉纳入多模态幻觉评估的统一基准，涵盖实体、属性、关系和知识四个维度，通过配对的正负问题实现可控比较，包含1,800个样本、10个领域和50个类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）幻觉基准主要关注实体、属性和关系幻觉，而知识相关失败通常被单独研究，缺乏一个统一的多维度幻觉评估框架。作者旨在弥合这一评估缺口。

Method: KnowHal基准采用半自动化构建流程（结合LLM辅助、基于CLIP的过滤和人工验证），构建了共享图像和实体的配对正负问题，支持对感知错误、知识相关错误和虚假前提接受进行可控对比分析，覆盖四个幻觉维度（实体、属性、关系、知识）。

Result: 在14个代表性MLLM上的评估显示：知识维度对几乎所有模型构成最大挑战；大多数模型在负问题上表现出显著的性能下降，反映了对虚假前提的鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: KnowHal通过统一四个幻觉维度并采用配对问题设计，填补了现有评估框架的重要空白，使MLLM的幻觉评估更加全面和系统化。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a critical challenge for developing trustworthy Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). While existing benchmarks mainly focus on entity, attribute, and relation hallucinations, knowledge-related failures are often investigated separately, lacking a unified evaluation framework across different hallucination dimensions. To overcome this, we propose \textbf{KnowHal}, a benchmark that explicitly incorporates knowledge hallucination into multimodal hallucination evaluation spanning four dimensions: entity, attribute, relation, and knowledge. KnowHal constructs paired positive and negative questions over shared images and entities, enabling controlled comparisons among perceptual errors, knowledge-related errors, and false-premise acceptance. The benchmark contains 1,800 samples across 10 domains and 50 categories, constructed through a semi-automated pipeline combining LLM assistance, CLIP-based filtering, and human verification. We evaluate 14 representative MLLMs on KnowHal and conduct extensive analyses. Results show that the knowledge dimension consistently presents the greatest challenge for nearly all evaluated models, while most models exhibit substantial performance degradation on negative questions, revealing limited robustness to false premises. By unifying four hallucination dimensions with paired question design, KnowHal addresses an important gap in existing evaluation frameworks and enables a more comprehensive assessment of hallucinations in MLLMs.

</details>


### [227] [Does Forgetting Transfer Across Modalities? A Real-World Benchmark for Cross-Modal Knowledge Unlearning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03791)
*Chunlin Liu,Junnian Chen,Haitong Jiang,Jianyu Zhao,Yingsen Pang,Jingchen Li,Jiabiao He,Youming Lu,Jinhe Bi,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了UNLINK-VL，一个面向视觉语言模型跨模态知识遗忘的基准测试，包含四个互补子集，用于评估直接遗忘、关系知识传播、非目标知识保留和对语义等价查询的鲁棒性。实验发现多模态遗忘在文本评测下有效，而纯文本遗忘难以迁移到视觉和跨模态场景，表明仅依赖单模态（尤其是纯文本）评测会高估VLM知识遗忘的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识遗忘研究主要聚焦于单模态场景，虽然已有工作开始探索跨模态一致性，但VLM中真实世界知识的跨模态迁移遗忘尚未被充分研究。由于VLM可能从预训练语料中记忆敏感、版权或有害知识，构建可信AI系统需要有效的知识遗忘机制，因此有必要对跨模态知识遗忘进行系统评估。

Method: 提出UNLINK-VL基准，在事后遗忘设置下（原始遗忘/保留语料不可用），选取视觉可识别的真实世界实体作为遗忘目标，从Wikidata关联对应图像及一跳和多跳事实。基准包含四个互补子集：直接遗忘评估、关系知识传播评估、非目标知识保留评估、语义等价查询鲁棒性评估。在文本-only和多模态两种遗忘设置下训练模型，并从文本、视觉和跨模态场景评测遗忘效果与保留能力。

Result: 实验揭示了跨模态迁移的显著不对称性：多模态遗忘在文本评测下仍然有效，而纯文本遗忘难以迁移到视觉和跨模态场景。同时，被评测的方法大多能保持模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: 仅依赖单模态（特别是纯文本）评测会大幅高估VLM中知识遗忘的有效性，凸显了跨模态遗忘和评测的必要性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs), like Large Language Models (LLMs), may memorize sensitive, copyrighted, or harmful knowledge from their pretraining corpora. Removing such knowledge is essential for building trustworthy AI systems. However, existing studies primarily focus on forgetting within individual modalities. Although recent work has begun to explore cross-modal consistency in unlearning, the cross-modal transfer of real-world knowledge unlearning remains insufficiently studied. To address this gap, we introduce UNLINK-VL, a real-world benchmark for cross-modal knowledge unlearning in VLMs. Under a post-hoc unlearning setting in which the original forget and retain corpora are unavailable, UNLINK-VL selects visually identifiable real-world entities as unlearning targets and associates them with corresponding images and one-hop and multi-hop facts derived from Wikidata. The benchmark comprises four complementary subsets that evaluate direct forgetting of target knowledge, the propagation of forgetting through relational knowledge, the preservation of related non-target knowledge, and robustness to semantically equivalent queries. We train models under text-only and multimodal unlearning settings and evaluate forgetting effectiveness and retained utility across textual, visual, and cross-modal scenarios. Extensive experiments reveal a pronounced asymmetry in cross-modal transfer: multimodal unlearning remains effective under textual evaluation, whereas text-only unlearning transfers poorly to visual and cross-modal scenarios. Meanwhile, the evaluated methods largely preserve the models' general capabilities. These findings demonstrate that relying solely on intra-modal evaluation, particularly text-only evaluation, may substantially overestimate the effectiveness of knowledge unlearning in VLMs, underscoring the need for cross-modal unlearning and evaluation.

</details>


### [228] [LatentGuard: Efficient and Inspectable Latent Reasoning for LLM Safeguards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03838)
*Zhinan Liu,Jie Li,Mingyu Kang,Jiayi Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentGuard 是一种高效的、可检查的LLM安全护栏框架，将连续潜在推理引入护栏模型，通过分阶段课程蒸馏将文本推理压缩为潜在状态，并配备隔离的辅助解码器用于按需生成审计工件。在F1上超过GuardReasoner-8B，同时将关键路径推理成本从268.56个token降至1.60个。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于推理的护栏模型虽能提升LLM安全保障，但每个交互都要解码显式推理，部署成本高昂。潜推理方法虽能减少token生成，但尚未在安全审核领域得到充分探索，且缺乏部署所需的检查接口。

Method: LatentGuard采用分阶段课程学习，逐步将任务对齐的文本推理压缩为紧凑的潜在状态，使安全判定可直接从连续表示中预测；同时使用隔离的辅助解码器按需生成紧凑审计工件，将推理生成移出标准推理路径以保持可检查性。

Result: LatentGuard-8B在平均加权F1上从83.95提升至84.91（对比GuardReasoner-8B），关键路径推理成本从268.56个生成的推理token降至1.60个潜在推理token；审计解码器达到85.75的审计效用分数。

Conclusion: LatentGuard展示了高效且可检查的路径，为LLM安全护栏的实际部署提供了可行方案，兼顾了推理效率与可审计性。

Abstract: Reasoning-based guard models improve LLM safeguards, but decoding explicit rationales for every interaction makes them costly to deploy. Although latent-reasoning methods reduce token generation by moving reasoning into continuous states, they remain underexplored for safety moderation and lack an inspection interface for deployment. In this paper, we propose LatentGuard, an efficient and inspectable safeguard framework that brings continuous latent reasoning to guard models. LatentGuard uses a staged curriculum to progressively compress task-aligned textual rationales into compact latent states, enabling safety verdicts to be predicted directly from continuous representations. To preserve inspectability, an isolated auxiliary decoder generates compact audit artifacts on demand, keeping rationale generation off the standard inference path. Experiments show that LatentGuard-8B improves mean weighted F1 from 83.95 to 84.91 over GuardReasoner-8B, while reducing critical-path reasoning cost from 268.56 generated rationale tokens to 1.60 latent reasoning tokens. Its audit decoder achieves an audit utility score of 85.75, demonstrating an efficient and inspectable path toward deployable LLM safeguards.

</details>


### [229] [Oilbird: Training-Free Speculative Decoding with Keys the Verifier Already Computes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03839)
*Tao Jin,Phuong Minh Nguyen,Zhenzhu Yan,Teeradaj Racharak,Naoya Inoue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Oilbird，一种无训练的投机解码方法。它诊断出传统基于精确后缀匹配的草稿生成器在工具调用场景下存在"寻址问题"而非"覆盖问题"：池中已有正确草稿但精确匹配无法触达。为此作者提出第二路语义草稿源——用验证器已计算的隐状态对同一池重新索引，并与现有词法草稿生成器的树结构合并。在三个已发表草稿器中，同等池与预算下可提升接受长度24-29%；在API-Bank上达到4.4倍自回归解码加速（最强无训练基线3.9倍，EAGLE-3为2.0倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练投机解码通过精确后缀匹配在上下文池中查找草稿，但在工具调用场景下，请求几乎重复全部内容仅少数新值不同，一旦一个token被拒绝，其后的正确延续也会被丢弃。作者逐位置诊断发现，最强精确匹配草稿器遗漏的内容中约一半已在池中却无法通过精确匹配触达——这是寻址问题而非覆盖问题，因此需要新的寻址方式。

Method: 提出第二种"语义草稿源"：将同一token池改用验证器在每个已提交token处已计算出的隐状态作为键重新建立索引，从而能检索语义相似而非仅字面相同的草稿。同时提出一种合并机制，使该语义草稿能嵌入到现有词法草稿生成器的树结构中共同参与投机解码。

Result: 在三个已发表的草稿生成器中，在匹配的池规模和预算下，该方法将接受长度提升24-29%。所提出的Oilbird系统在API-Bank基准上达到4.4倍自回归解码加速，超过最强无训练基线（3.9倍）和EAGLE-3（2.0倍）。

Conclusion: 通过用隐状态重键池并通过合并机制将语义草稿融入现有词法树结构，可有效解决无训练投机解码在工具调用场景下的寻址失效问题，显著提升接受长度与端到端解码速度。

Abstract: Training-free speculative decoding drafts by matching an exact suffix of the context against a pool of earlier context. That lookup misses correct drafts already in the pool, most visibly on tool-calling traffic, where a request repeats almost everything but the few values minted for it, and where one rejected token discards the correct continuation behind it. We diagnose the failure position by position across ten benchmarks and find it to be a problem of addressing rather than of coverage: on our densest tool-calling benchmark, about half of what the strongest exact-match drafter misses is present in the pool yet unreachable by exact matching. We therefore propose a second, semantic draft source: the same pool, re-keyed by the hidden state the verifier has already computed at each committed token, together with a merge that lets it ride inside an existing lexical drafter's tree. In three published drafters, at matched pool and budget, it lifts accepted length by 24-29%. Oilbird reaches 4.4x autoregressive decoding speed on API-Bank, against 3.9x for the strongest training-free baseline in our harness and 2.0x for EAGLE-3.

</details>


### [230] [MAFIA: Query-Only Memory Attacks via Probing and Factual Injection against Audited LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03844)
*Jiaming Chen,Yisen Gao,Yanping Li,Zifan Liu,Yumeng Zhang,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAFIA框架，一种面向记忆增强型LLM智能体的查询式内存投毒攻击方法，通过记忆探测与事实注入绕过审计，在大型良性记忆池和输入审计场景下实现高达90.7%的攻击成功率，同时将审计检测率从83.3%压制到7.4%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强型LLM智能体依赖丰富上下文进行长时程推理与行动，但其记忆模块对恶意记录暴露了持续的受攻击面。现有查询式攻击在两种现实且普遍的场景（大规模良性记忆池和主动输入审计）下往往失效，无法同时应对高检索竞争力和严格语义检查的双重挑战，亟需更强大的攻击方法。

Method: MAFIA包含两大核心设计：(1) 放置策略——通过记忆探测、预算分配与调度，确保注入的记忆在检索中具有竞争力；(2) 载荷设计——利用紧凑的事实伪装（factual cloaks）绕过语义审计，在保持高语义相似度的同时保留恶意效果。整个攻击仅依赖查询接口（query-only）。

Result: 广泛评估表明，MAFIA实现了高达90.7%的攻击成功率，同时将审计检测率从峰值83.3%抑制到至多7.4%，揭示了智能体记忆系统中的关键安全漏洞。

Conclusion: MAFIA暴露了记忆增强型LLM智能体在记忆安全方面的严重脆弱性，代码已公开，为该领域的安全防御研究提供了重要参考基础和攻击模型基线。

Abstract: Memory-augmented LLM agents rely on rich context for long-horizon reasoning and acting, yet their memory modules expose a persistent attack surface for malicious records, making the study of memory poisoning threats imperative. However, existing query-only attacks often fail to remain effective in two realistic and prevalent settings: large-scale benign memory pools and active input auditing. Consequently, current approaches fall short when facing the dual challenges of high retrieval competitiveness and rigorous semantic checks. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAFIA, a query-only Memory Attack framework via probing and Factual Injection against Audit, tailored to this extended threat model. Specifically, MAFIA introduces: (1) a placement strategy that ensures retrieval-competitive injection via memory probing, budget allocation, and scheduling; and (2) a payload design that bypasses audits using compact factual cloaks, preserving malicious effects while maintaining high semantic similarity. Extensive evaluations reveal that MAFIA achieves up to a 90.7% attack success rate while suppressing audit detection from a peak of 83.3% to at most 7.4%, exposing critical vulnerabilities across agentic memory systems. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingChen1234/MAFIA.

</details>


### [231] [AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00155)
*Dong Yan,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Ran He,Nicholas Jing Yuan,Qi Zhang,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentStream框架，用于在真实流式任务场景下评估自进化智能体。通过构建Isolated、Sequential和Interleaved三种流式场景，对5种自进化方法和3个前沿基座模型进行组合评估，发现自进化的可靠性随流式场景变化、其收益受模型能力门控且与模型强度呈非单调关系，没有任何单一方法在所有场景中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体的研究主要采用独立评估（isolated evaluation），导致其在真实流式环境下（智能体持续适应多样化复杂任务流）的行为和表现尚不清晰。

Method: 提出AgentStream统一框架：将智能体基准组织为可配置的任务流，并在测试阶段实例化Isolated、Sequential和Interleaved三种流式场景（逐步变化任务流的范围和域组成）。在此之上，组合评估5种代表性自进化方法和3个前沿基座模型，解耦模型能力、方法架构和流式场景三者对自进化的联合影响。

Result: 自进化可靠性随流式场景不同而变化；自进化的收益受模型能力门控，且与模型强度呈非单调关系；没有任何单一方法在所有模型和场景中占据主导地位。

Conclusion: 自进化智能体应在真实任务流而非孤立的单任务设置下进行评估，本研究为跨模型和场景选择自进化方法提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can self-evolve by continually improving from their own accumulated experience. However, existing studies predominantly adopt independent evaluation. Consequently, the behavior of self-evolving agents in realistic streaming settings, where agents adapt to diverse and complex task streams, remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we introduce AgentStream, a unified framework that evaluates self-evolving agents spanning diverse evolution components by organizing agentic benchmarks into a configurable task stream and instantiating the \texttt{Isolated}, \texttt{Sequential}, and \texttt{Interleaved} streaming scenarios at test time, which progressively vary the scope and domain composition of the stream. Over these scenarios, we combinatorially evaluate five representative self-evolving methods across three frontier foundation models, disentangling how model capability, method architecture, and streaming scenario jointly shape self-evolution. Our results show that self-evolution reliability varies across streaming scenarios, the benefit of self-evolution is gated by model capability and non-monotonic in model strength, and no single method dominates across models and scenarios. These findings offer concrete guidance for selecting self-evolving methods across models and streaming scenarios. Overall, we advocate that self-evolving agents should be evaluated under realistic task streams rather than isolated single-task settings.

</details>


### [232] [ADMITBench: A Safety-Governed Reference Framework for Evaluating the Admissibility of Industrial LLM Advisories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03866)
*Yash Misra,Javal Vyas,Siddharth Gutta,Mehmet Mercangöz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADMITBench是一个用于评估工业LLM建议的参考框架，它在拟议行动层面通过版本化、安全治理的评估合约来检查建议是否有证据支持、是否在权限和程序范围内、以及是否符合工厂特定的后果检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中LLM生成的建议需要系统的评估框架来验证其安全性和合规性，现有评估方法与工业级安全治理要求不匹配。

Method: 实现了一个版本化的、安全治理的评估合约，包含三个核心检查：证据支持性检查（是否由可用证据支持）、权限与程序合规性检查（是否在既定权威和程序允许范围内）、工厂特定后果检查（依据所选评估配置中的后果检查编码），其中安全治理通过明确的、不可补偿的检查来确定资格。

Result: 发布了Release 0.1.0公共参考实现，用于技术和研究评估目的，而非物理执行授权。

Conclusion: ADMITBench作为参考框架为工业LLM建议提供了结构化评估机制，但明确声明其定位是研究和评估工具，不构成对物理执行的安全授权。

Abstract: This white paper presents ADMITBench, a reference framework for evaluating industrial LLM advisories at the level of the proposed action. The framework implements a versioned, safety-governed evaluation contract that checks whether a recommendation is supported by the available evidence, permitted under the stated authority and procedure, and acceptable under the plant-specific consequence checks encoded in the selected evaluation profile. In this report, \emph{safety-governed} means that eligibility is determined through explicit, non-compensatory checks derived from a versioned plant profile; it does not mean that the evaluator, model, or plant has been safety-certified. Release 0.1.0 is a public reference implementation for technical and research evaluation, not an authorisation for physical execution.

</details>


### [233] [ContinualSkillBench: Can LLM Agents Truly Evolve Their Capabilities?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03874)
*Tianyi Guan,Yiding Wang,Haotong Yang,Siyuan Cao,Shirui Liu,Yi Hu,Jiaqi Li,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ContinualSkillBench，一个面向上下文持续技能学习的动态评估框架，涵盖五个领域各100个相互关联的子任务。实验发现顺序执行整体上提升性能但增益因模型和领域而异；上下文学习与显式技能维护平均效果相当，表明提升主要来自对先前上下文和反馈的适应；显式技能仅在需要可复用流程或精确输出的任务中表现出选择性优势；能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更大、更碎片化的任务特定技能集合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体框架为大型语言模型配备外部技能库以解决复杂任务，但现有系统能否有效演化技能、以及演化出的技能是否真正提升任务解决能力仍然不明确。作者旨在通过系统性的动态评估框架来弥合这一认知空白。

Method: 提出ContinualSkillBench动态评估框架，覆盖五个代表性领域，每个领域包含100个按难度递增且存在跨任务技能复用机会的相互关联子任务，用于评估大语言模型在上下文持续技能学习中的表现。

Result: 顺序执行总体提升性能，但增益在不同模型和领域间差异显著；上下文学习与显式技能维护平均表现相当；显式技能在需要可复用流程或精确输出的任务中提供选择性优势；能力较弱的模型倾向于积累更大更碎片化的技能集。

Conclusion: 当前的上下文技能演化机制虽能支持持续适应，但在将经验一致地整合为稳健且可迁移的技能方面仍然存在困难。

Abstract: Modern agent frameworks equip large language models with external skill libraries to solve complex tasks. However, it remains unclear whether these systems can effectively evolve their skills and whether the resulting skills improve task-solving capabilities. To bridge this gap, we introduce ContinualSkillBench, a dynamic evaluation framework for in-context continual skill learning. It covers five representative domains, each containing 100 interconnected subtasks ordered by increasing difficulty and opportunities for cross-task skill reuse. Our experiments show that sequential execution generally improves performance, but the gains vary substantially across models and domains. Moreover, in-context learning performs comparably to explicit skill maintenance on average, suggesting that much of the improvement arises from adaptation to prior context and feedback rather than reusable skill abstraction alone. Explicit skills nevertheless provide selective benefits for tasks requiring reusable procedures or precise outputs. We further find that less capable models tend to accumulate larger, more fragmented collections of task-specific skills. These findings show that current in-context skill evolution mechanisms can support continual adaptation, but still struggle to consistently consolidate experience into robust and transferable skills.

</details>


### [234] [Intertemporal Preference Steering in Qwen3 via Contrastive Activation Addition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03892)
*Michal Mráz,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究在Qwen3-32B大语言模型中识别线性表示的时间视野方向，并通过对比激活添加（contrastive activation-addition）调控模型的跨期偏好与规划能力，实验结果证实时间偏好是可测量且可操控的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在进行涉及延迟成本与收益的建议时，其时间相关偏好（如短期与长期选择）对决策质量有重要影响，也与长视野规划的安全性相关。然而，模型的跨期偏好是否可测量、可操控尚不清楚，作者旨在探究这一问题。

Method: 在教师强制（teacher-forced）的时间选择答案上训练对比线性探针（contrastive linear probes），在残差流（residual stream）中提取"短期vs长期"方向，并采用对比激活添加（contrastive activation-addition）进行引导（steering）。在留出的二值时间选择任务、分布外货币跨期选择任务（变化奖励大小与延迟）以及TravelPlanner能力基准上进行评估。

Result: 核心结果是：简单对比线性探针即可识别时间视野方向，且引导能够引起双向、大幅度的偏好改变。在分布外货币选择任务中，引导在两个方向上都强烈地移动了模型在"更小更早"与"更大更晚"奖励之间的无差异阈值。此外，在适度时间引导下，规划相关能力指标有所提升。

Conclusion: 模型的跨期偏好是可测量且可引导的，这对提供延迟成本/收益相关建议的AI系统以及长视野规划的安全性问题具有重要意义。

Abstract: We study linear representations of temporal horizon in the large language model Qwen3-32B and use them to change the model's time-related preferences, recommendations, and capabilities. We train contrastive linear probes on teacher-forced temporal-choice answers to find a short-term versus long-term direction in the model's residual stream, and evaluate contrastive activation-addition steering on a held-out binary temporal-choice task, an out-of-distribution monetary intertemporal-choice task, and a TravelPlanner capability benchmark. The central result is that temporal-horizon directions can be identified with simple contrastive linear probes and then used for steering to induce large, bidirectional preference changes. On an out-of-distribution monetary choice task that varies reward size and delay, steering strongly shifts the model's indifference threshold between smaller-sooner and larger-later rewards in both directions. We further show improvements on a planning-related capability metric under moderate temporal steering. These results suggest that model intertemporal preferences are measurable and steerable, which is relevant for AI systems that give advice involving delayed costs and benefits, and for safety questions about long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [235] [When Efficiency Becomes Fragility: Exploiting Dynamic Routing Vulnerabilities in Adaptive UAV Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03902)
*Shaofeng Liang,Runwei Guan,Wenshuo Chen,Jiemin Wu,Bowen Tian,Haozhe Jia,Kaishen Yuan,Songning Lai,Daizong Liu,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示自适应Transformer跟踪器中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异性，识别出一个可被直接利用的新型攻击面（拓扑路径空间），并提出对抗性路径反转（API）框架，通过精确操纵门控决策迫使推理转向被篡改的计算路径，从而破坏模型表征能力。实验证明API在扰动隐蔽性、攻击有效性和推理速度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机平台的资源约束推动自适应Transformer跟踪器采用输入依赖的动态路由架构来平衡精度与效率，但该架构在离散层跳过决策边界处存在Lipschitz奇异性的结构性缺陷，导致微小输入扰动可在门控模块被放大并引发推理拓扑剧变。现有研究未意识到这一可被直接利用的新攻击面。

Method: 形式化刻画自适应跟踪架构中计算路径决策的Lipschitz奇异特性，将其识别为拓扑路径空间攻击面；据此提出Adversarial Path-Inversion（API）框架，生成不可察觉的扰动精确操纵门控决策，强制推理沿被篡改的计算路径进行，利用原始路径与被反转路径间的严重不一致性瓦解模型表征能力。

Result: 在多种最先进的自适应跟踪器上进行的大量实验表明，API实现了更优的扰动隐蔽性、更强的攻击效果以及更快的推理速度。

Conclusion: 本文为动态跟踪网络的安全性分析开辟了新维度，并为未来构建鲁棒的自适应跟踪架构提供了理论层面的警示。

Abstract: Resource constraints on UAV platforms have driven a paradigm shift in aerial tracking, from pursuing performance toward balancing accuracy with efficiency. Adaptive Transformer Trackers, which leverage an input-dependent dynamic routing architecture, have emerged as a representative solution to this challenge. However, we reveal that behind this computation-on-demand flexibility hides a critical structural flaw: the Lipschitz singularity of computational path decisions, which has an unbounded local Lipschitz constant at discrete layer-skipping decision boundaries. This mathematical discontinuity renders adaptive tracking networks inherently unstable: tiny input perturbations can be amplified at the gating modules, causing dramatic changes in the inference topology. We formally characterize this singularity in the context of adaptive tracking architectures and, for the first time, identify it as a directly exploitable new attack surface. This insight reveals a previously overlooked and highly vulnerable topological path space attack surface. Based on this, we propose the Adversarial Path-Inversion (API) framework. API generates imperceptible perturbations to precisely manipulate the gating decisions, forcing the inference onto altered computational paths. The severe inconsistency between the original and the inverted paths dismantles the representation capability of the model. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art adaptive trackers demonstrate that API achieves superior perturbation stealthiness, more effective attack, and faster inference speeds. This work opens a new dimension for the security analysis of dynamic tracking networks and provides a theoretical warning for constructing robust adaptive tracking architectures in the future.

</details>


### [236] [Socially Grounded Agentic AI: Coordinating Plural Perspectives through Social Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03910)
*Matt Ratto,Abhishek Moturu,Daniel Silver*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张通过社会学理论重新构建"多元对齐"（pluralistic alignment）的概念，将其从追求输出多样化转变为基于社会情境的协调问题，并提出基于角色表征、结构化协调和情境敏感评估的AI系统设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统被部署到日益多样的社会情境中，对齐不应再被视为优化单一统一价值集合的问题，而须识别、表征并响应多种合法视角。然而现有方法缺乏对"价值如何在社会中被组织、争议和协调"的清晰解释，社会理论可为此提供必要的概念与设计资源。

Method: 借鉴社会学既有传统，将视角理解为由角色结构化、通过互动塑造、并分布于权力与专业知识的场域之中；将这些洞见转化为具体的AI系统设计启示，包括基于角色的表征、多视角间的结构化协调，以及情境敏感评估；对于智能体系统，对齐不仅涉及最终输出，还涉及角色激活、审议轨迹、聚合规则和反馈循环等产出的过程环节。

Result: 将多元对齐重新定位为"社会性嵌入的协调问题"而非"输出多样化问题"，勾勒出一个支持多视角以结构化且可问责方式互动的设计空间，并识别了未来在真实环境中实施和实证评估这些方法的若干方向。

Conclusion: 多元对齐的本质在于如何让系统在社会情境中结构化地协调多种合法视角，而非单纯生成多样化输出；社会学理论为这一重新定位提供了关键的框架基础，未来需在实际场景中对所提出的设计路径进行实证检验。

Abstract: As AI systems are deployed across increasingly diverse social contexts, alignment can no longer be framed as the optimization of a single, unified set of values. Instead, systems must be able to recognize, represent, and respond to multiple legitimate perspectives. This has led to growing interest in pluralistic alignment, which seeks to move beyond one-size-fits-all models of appropriate behaviour. However, current approaches often lack a clear account of how values are socially organized, contested, and coordinated in practice. In this paper, we argue that social theory provides essential conceptual and design resources for addressing these challenges. Drawing on established traditions in sociology, we show how perspectives can be understood as structured by roles, shaped through interaction, and distributed across fields of power and expertise. We translate these insights into concrete implications for AI system design, including role-based representations, structured coordination among perspectives, and context-sensitive evaluation. For agentic systems, this requires aligning not only final outputs, but also the role activations, deliberative traces, aggregation rules, and feedback loops through which those outputs are produced. Our contribution is to reposition pluralistic alignment as a problem of socially grounded coordination rather than output diversification. We outline a design space for systems that engage multiple perspectives in structured and accountable ways, and we identify directions for future work to implement and empirically evaluate these approaches in real-world settings.

</details>


### [237] [Implementing Causal Perception: Competing SCMs and Situated Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03917)
*Jose M. Álvarez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次实现了Álvarez和Ruggieri（2025）提出的因果感知（causal perception）框架，将结构性（因果图不一致）和参数性（权重不一致）因果感知操作化，设计算法计算干预分布和反事实分布，并提出距离度量量化分歧。基于德国信用数据集，本文展示了因果感知如何影响多专家决策场景中的准确性和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果感知是一个有前景的基于概率的框架，但此前仅停留在纯理论层面，未曾有实际实现。为了验证该框架在真实场景中的有效性，需要将其操作化并落地实现。

Method: 将因果感知分为两类：结构性因果感知（代理者对因果图结构存在分歧）和参数性因果感知（代理者认同因果图但对其权重存在分歧）。设计了计算干预分布与反事实分布的算法，并提出了适合的距离度量来量化代理者之间的分歧程度。使用德国信用数据集进行实证分析。

Result: 因果感知的"感知判定"（perception verdict）对距离度量的选择和阈值设定高度敏感。因果感知会影响公平性评估和基于阈值的决策。偏差被证明是相对于代理者SCM而言具有情境性的（situated），表明公平问题中的竞争性世界观不可忽视。

Conclusion: 公平性问题的竞争性世界观不能被忽略——因果感知框架表明，不同代理者基于各自的因果模型会对同一系统得出不同的公平性评估，而偏差是情境化的，取决于代理者所持有的SCM。

Abstract: Causal perception occurs when agents with competing Structural Causal Models (SCMs) of the same system infer different probability distributions, including the hypothetical distributions implied by each agent's SCM under the same set of interventions. It shapes how agents reason about the system and how they perceive its fairness. Causal perception is a promising probabilistic framework, but it has remained purely theoretical. This work provides the first implementation of the causal perception framework of Álvarez and Ruggieri (2025). We operationalize structural (agents disagree on the causal graph) and parametrical (agents agree on the causal graph but disagree on its weights) causal perception. We design algorithms for computing interventional and counterfactual distributions and propose suitable distance measures to quantify the disagreement. Using the German Credit dataset, we illustrate how causal perception affects accuracy and fairness in a multi-expert decision setting. We show that the perception verdict is sensitive to the choice of distance metric and threshold. We also show that causal perception changes fairness assessments and threshold-based decisions. Bias proves situated with respect to the agent's SCM, demonstrating that competing worldviews in fairness problems cannot be ignored.

</details>


### [238] [The Transformer Revolution, Part 1: Dynamic Processing through Output- Weight Interconnections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03921)
*Marco Giunti,Fabrizia Giulia Garavaglia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对Transformer推理过程的全新解释，即SIDPP（序列级交互动态并行处理）框架：Transformer在推理期间动态生成参数化变换，用概念转换概念，而非仅仅复现训练中的统计规律。该框架将Transformer解释为一个"用概念转换概念"的系统，动态处理贡献随提示长度增长，并推测人类大脑皮层可能以相似方式实现语言处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳"随机鹦鹉"观点——该观点认为大语言模型只是在复现训练中学到的统计规律。作者认为这种观点低估了Transformer在推理阶段实际进行计算的性质，提出了一个更高层次的计算解释框架。

Method: 提出SIDPP（Sequence-level Interactive Dynamic Parallel Processing，序列级交互动态并行处理）框架。核心思想包括：(1) 将token向量视为"被转换的概念"，将矩阵和向量定义的参数化变换视为"转换的概念"；(2) 这些变换可以是静态的（训练中固定）或动态的（从输入序列中生成）；(3) 在机制上对应多组简单神经网络的组合；(4) Transformer的架构新颖性在于输出-权重互连（某些网络的输出决定其他网络的权重）与普通的输出-输入互连相结合。
