Despite this additional requirement, GENESIS consistently outperforms purely statistical CD methods on the majority of benchmark datasets across all sample regimes in terms of Structural Hamming Distance (SHD), while achieving performance comparable to state-of-the-art LLM-assisted approaches.

</details>


### [336] [Enhancing VLM Reward Models Through Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03875)
*Pyrros Koussios,Chenhao Li,Xin Chen,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAFT是一种自监督方法，通过LoRA适配器利用任务内在的结构先验来正则化VLM的潜空间，从而在线去噪不完美的奖励信号，无需真实监督即可加速策略收敛并提升对齐质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RL中手动设计奖励函数是主要瓶颈，虽然VLM可作为奖励模型绕过人工设计，但其奖励信号嘈杂不可靠，限制了直接部署的效用。

Method: SAFT通过LoRA适配器利用任务固有的结构先验来正则化VLM的潜空间，在线精炼不完美的奖励信号，无需真实标签监督。

Result: SAFT在不同基础模型能力上均一致地降低了奖励噪声，使策略收敛更快，且显著提升了与基础模型的EPIC对齐距离，表明许多失败源于结构脆弱性而非语义理解不足。

Conclusion: SAFT用结构归纳偏置替代大量人工偏好标注，为稳定文本条件RL提供了可扩展路径，凸显了将任务结构作为通用归纳偏置的更广泛价值。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions remains a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL). Recent work uses large foundation Vision-Language Models (VLMs) as reward models, computing text-observation similarity to bypass manual reward engineering. Although promising, these rewards are often noisy and unreliable, limiting their direct utility during deployment. We present Structure-Aware Fine-Tuning (SAFT), a simple, self-supervised method that refines these imperfect reward signals online without access to ground-truth supervision. SAFT leverages intrinsic structural priors to regularize the VLM's latent space via LoRA adapters. We rigorously evaluate SAFT across a spectrum of base model capabilities to demonstrate its versatility. Our results show that SAFT consistently denoises the reward landscape, yielding faster policy convergence and substantially improved alignment (EPIC distance) relative to the underlying base model, suggesting that failures can often be attributed to structural brittleness rather than semantic misunderstanding. By replacing extensive human preference annotation with structural inductive biases inherent to the task, SAFT offers a scalable path for stabilizing text-conditioned RL and underscores the broader value of incorporating task structure as a general inductive bias.

</details>


### [337] [Operationally Feasible Synthetic Power-Grid Scenarios via Learning the AC-Operable Joint Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03878)
*Chenhan Xiao,Xinyu He,Haoran Li,Hanghang Tong,Yang Weng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可行性感知的分布学习框架，通过分层扩散模型学习交流可运行的电网场景联合分布（网络拓扑、支路参数、负荷曲线），在生成过程中融入AC潮流收敛性和运行约束，取代生成后验证/优化，显著提升场景的运营可行性、故障鲁棒性并保持统计保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成电网场景生成方法虽然在结构真实性和运营可行性上有所改进（通过生成后验证、优化或物理感知生成），但生成的场景仍可能具有较低的AC可行性和鲁棒性，限制了其在电力系统下游研究中的实际应用价值。

Method: 提出可行性感知的分层扩散分布学习框架：1) 将AC潮流收敛性和运行约束直接嵌入分层扩散模型的学习过程中，使生成器本身产生运营可行的场景，无需生成后优化；2) 分层架构将高维生成任务分解为三个工程驱动的阶段——拓扑与母线属性生成、基于结构的支路参数生成、基于结构和电气特性的负荷曲线生成。

Result: 在基准系统上的实验表明，该框架显著提升了运营可行性和故障鲁棒性，同时保持了强大的统计保真度，并消除了基于优化的后处理步骤。

Conclusion: 通过在扩散模型中嵌入AC可行性和运营约束，可以在生成过程中直接获得运营可行的电网场景，避免了传统的事后验证/优化的不足，为合成电网数据的实际工程应用提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Synthetic power-grid scenarios are essential for planning, resilience assessment, contingency analysis, and data-driven power-system applications. Recent synthetic grid generation methods have improved structural realism and operational feasibility by incorporating engineering knowledge through post-generation validation, optimization, or physics-aware generation. However, generated scenarios may still exhibit low AC feasibility and robustness, limiting their practical value for downstream power-system studies. This paper proposes a feasibility-aware distribution-learning framework that learns the AC-operable joint distribution of network topology, branch electrical parameters, and time-varying load profiles. Instead of enforcing feasibility after generation, the proposed framework incorporates AC power-flow convergence and operational constraints into hierarchical diffusion-based distribution learning. This enables the generator itself to produce operationally feasible grid scenarios through efficient diffusion sampling. The hierarchical architecture decomposes the high-dimensional generation task into three engineering-motivated stages: topology and bus-attribute generation, branch-parameter generation conditioned on the generated structure, and load-profile generation conditioned on both network structure and electrical characteristics. Experiments on benchmark systems demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly improves operational feasibility and contingency robustness while maintaining strong statistical fidelity and eliminating optimization-based post-processing.

</details>


### [338] [Omega-S: A Functional Resilience Index for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03887)
*Alberto Acedo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omega-S是一种无需旧数据、Fisher矩阵或旧权重副本的drop-in正则化惩罚项，仅从权重矩阵计算，用于缓解大语言模型微调时的灾难性遗忘。在Llama-3-8B LoRA实验中将HumanEval保留率从62.9%提升至84.1%，且在9/10（vs无正则）、10/10（vs weight decay）、8/10（vs EWC）的种子对比中胜出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在新数据上微调会破坏其先前学到的能力（灾难性遗忘），现有解决方案要么依赖旧任务数据、要么需要Fisher信息矩阵或存储旧权重副本，资源开销大且实现复杂。作者希望设计一种仅依赖权重矩阵自身的、极简且计算开销极小的正则化惩罚项。

Method: Omega-S从权重矩阵单独构造惩罚项，其数学目标基于Tr(A^3)（拓扑量）。实际实现中通过对四个因子进行弹性（elasticity）测量发现有三个因子几乎不动（弹性≤1e-4），真正起作用的是节点度方差项（弹性为9e-3），因此复合惩罚实际退化为对节点度方差的惩罚（方模块惩罚行幅值，非方模块惩罚方向对齐）。该方法只需三行代码即可嵌入现有训练循环，额外开销不足4%。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B LoRA上从代码微调到散文，HumanEval十种子实验：无正则基线pass@1从0.173提升至0.238（保留率62.9%→84.1%），9/10种子胜出（sign test p=0.011, Wilcoxon p=0.006）；对比tuned weight decay和tuned EWC分别在10/10（p=0.002）和8/10（p=0.014）种子上胜出。另发现相同配置重复实验在保留率上的标准差为0.104，这一噪声水平关涉所有种子配对比较的有效性。

Conclusion: Omega-S以极简实现和不足4%的计算开销有效缓解了LLM微调中的能力退化，且优于多种主流正则化基线。论文强调了两点：一是方法的实际作用机制需通过测量而非名称断言来确认（实际起作用的是度方差项而非全部拓扑因子）；二是相同种子的重复实验噪声（标准差0.104）不可忽视，应作为该领域所有种子配对比较的置信界。代码与负结果记录全部公开。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a large language model on new data degrades what it previously learned. We present Omega-S, a drop-in penalty computed from the weight matrix alone: it needs no previous-task data, no Fisher matrix and no stored copy of the old weights. It is three lines in an existing training loop and adds under 4% to the cost of a step.
  Retention. On Llama-3-8B with LoRA, fine-tuned from code to prose and measured by HumanEval over ten seeds, Omega-S retains more of the original capability than no regularisation on 9 of 10 seeds (0.173 -> 0.238 absolute pass@1; sign test one-sided p=0.011, Wilcoxon p=0.006), as a retention ratio, 62.9% -> 84.1%. It also beats tuned weight decay on 10 of 10 seeds (p=0.002) and tuned EWC on 8 of 10 (p=0.014), every arm re-measured in the same session.
  Mechanism, measured rather than asserted. Omega-S is topological by construction, its objective built from Tr(A^3), but we measured which of its four factors actually moves and three do not: their elasticity with respect to the weights is at or below 1e-4, against 9e-3 for the degree-variance term. As implemented, the composite reduces to a penalty on the variance of node degrees, which means row magnitude in square modules and directional alignment in non-square ones. We report this because a method whose name promises one thing and whose gradient does another should say so. We also enumerate the open design choices, including a contrast-preserving construction that does what it was designed to do and makes retention worse on all ten seeds.
  Repeating an identical configuration, same seed and same hardware, gives a standard deviation of 0.104 in retention ratio. We have not found this quantified for low-rank fine-tuning of language models, and it bounds every seed-paired comparison in this literature, ours included.
  Code, per-seed results and the full record of negative results are available.

</details>


### [339] [Cross-Model KV Cache Transfer in LLM Families: A Closed-Form Linear Mapping for Prefill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03893)
*Taekyung Heo,Rasoul Shafipour,Ritchie Zhao,Maximilian Golub,Mohammad Mahdi Kamani,Ritika Borkar,Makesh Tarun Chandran,Pantea Zardoshti,Bita Darvish Rouhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型KV缓存迁移技术，使接收方模型复用源模型的KV缓存从而跳过预填充阶段。作者发现跨模型KV在匹配KV对上存在显著的线性结构，并设计了一个基于岭回归的逐头线性映射器（三步流程：选择最相关源层、剥离RoPE、小规模校准集拟合），在六个模型对中四个保留73-98%的独立预填充准确率，运行速度比重新预填充快2.7-25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中，同系列不同规模模型之间的切换（成本-质量级联、对话中途切换、路由等）非常常见，但每次切换都要求接收方从零开始进行预填充，造成大量计算开销。如果能让接收方复用源模型的KV缓存，就能跳过预填充，显著降低延迟和计算成本。

Method: 1) 发现跨模型KV在匹配KV对（共享KV头数和每头维度）上存在显著线性结构；2) 设计逐头的闭式岭回归映射器，包含三个步骤：为每个目标层选取top-k个最具预测性的源层并拼接其KV作为输入；将Key中的RoPE剥离后再映射，使拟合与位置无关且可跨上下文长度复用；在500条FineWeb-Edu序列（每序列1024 token）的小型校准集上拟合岭回归。对失败的两个模型对，额外使用非线性MLP恢复性能。

Result: 在三个模型家族的六个模型对上，线性映射器在四个模型对上保留了73-98%的接收方独立预填充准确率，两个对性能严重下降；非线性MLP在失败对上恢复了高达+37个百分点（pp）的HellaSwag保留率。映射器运行速度比重新预填充快2.7-25倍，且在多轮对话切换中保持稳定。

Conclusion: 跨模型KV缓存迁移是实用的，线性映射器足以处理大多数模型对，非线性MLP可作为失败的补救手段。该方法显著加速了模型切换过程，使跨模型KV缓存迁移在实际部署中变得可行。

Abstract: Production deployments often swap between different-sized models in a family for cost-quality cascading, mid-conversation switching, and routing, and each swap forces the receiver to repay the prefill from scratch. We propose cross-model KV cache transfer, where the receiver reuses the source's KV cache, skipping prefill. We find that cross-model KV has substantial linear structure across matched-KV pairs, where source and target share KV head count and per-head dimension. On Qwen3 14B->32B, one source layer explains 56% of variance in the target's keys and 32% in values, rising to 79% and 65% with multiple source layers. Building on this, we design a closed-form ridge mapper that operates per head and proceeds in three steps. First, for each target layer we select the top-k most predictive source layers and concatenate their KV as input. Second, we strip RoPE from the keys before mapping, so the fit is position-free and reusable across context lengths. Third, we fit ridge regression on a small calibration set of 500 FineWeb-Edu sequences of 1,024 tokens each. Surprisingly, across six pairs in three families, this linear mapper retains 73-98% of the receiver's standalone-prefill accuracy on four pairs, while two degrade sharply. A nonlinear MLP recovers up to +37 pp HellaSwag retention on the failures. The mapper runs 2.7-25x faster than re-prefill and remains stable across multi-turn handoff, making cross-model KV cache transfer practical.

</details>


### [340] [Sparse Weight Decomposition for Efficient Circuit Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03913)
*Chuanhao Yan,Xuhan Huang,Yawen Duan,Zhenfei Yin,Hang Zhao,Bryan Dai,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出稀疏权重分解（SWD）方法，将预训练模型的线性投影权重矩阵分解为两个稀疏因子，其共享中间坐标作为可寻址的电路单元，无需训练替换网络即可支持电路提取工作流，在数据效率、保真度和电路充分性/必要性目标上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集预训练Transformer无法自然暴露可解释单元用于电路提取，现有方法需要学习辅助稀疏表示或训练稀疏模型，带来大量额外计算且引入表示与原模型之间的保真度差距。

Method: 将每个预训练线性投影的权重矩阵重新参数化为两个稀疏因子的乘积，共享中间坐标作为独立可寻址的电路单元；支持与学习稀疏特征方法相同的评分、选择和消融电路提取工作流；包括单矩阵替换、全模型替换（微调非零因子值）以及零数据变体。

Result: 单矩阵替换时，SWD以不到基线1%的训练数据达到与Transcoder等强基线相当的保真度；在匹配替换保真度下，SWD在GPT-2、Qwen2.5、Qwen3.5-27B上以更少的活跃读写边和选中单元达到相同的电路充分性和必要性目标；全模型替换微调后依然有效；零数据变体支持更广泛的机械可解释性分析。

Conclusion: SWD是一种高效的稀疏权重分解方法，能以极小数据成本实现可解释的电路单元提取，同时保持原始预训练模型的高保真度，并扩展到全模型替换和零数据场景，为机械可解释性分析提供了更广泛的应用可能。

Abstract: Dense pretrained transformers do not naturally expose interpretable units for circuit extraction. Existing approaches obtain such units by learning auxiliary sparse representations or training sparse models, incurring substantial additional computation while potentially introducing a fidelity gap between the representation being analyzed and the original pretrained model. We propose Sparse Weight Decomposition (SWD), which reparameterizes pretrained linear projections by factorizing each weight matrix into two sparse factors whose shared intermediate coordinates serve as individually addressable circuit units. Without training a separate replacement network, this parametric representation supports the same scoring, selection, and ablation circuit extraction workflow used for methods that learn sparse features. Across single-matrix replacements, SWD matches the held-out fidelity achieved by Transcoder and other strong baselines while using less than 1% of the data that those baselines use to train their replacements. For matched replacement fidelity, SWD reaches the same circuit sufficiency and necessity targets with fewer active read/write edges and selected units across tasks on GPT-2, Qwen2.5, and Qwen3.5-27B. We further show that SWD remains effective for full-model replacement of all attention and MLP weight matrices after fine-tuning the nonzero factor values. Finally, SWD also features a zero-data variant, allowing broader use of mechanistic interpretability analysis (e.g., per-step analysis).

</details>


### [341] [Trajectory inference via Acceleration Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03916)
*Bartolo Dazzini,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Acceleration Matching (AM)的新算法，用于解决轨迹推断问题。该方法将原始插值问题提升到相空间，回归到一个显式的条件加速度场，从而生成随机、平滑且符合给定边缘分布的轨迹。该算法仅需要位置数据，训练时无需轨迹模拟或昂贵的预处理，且在多个基准问题上与现有算法相比具有竞争力或更优的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断是许多科学领域中的基础问题，但现有算法存在计算挑战：要么依赖预处理子程序来强制平滑性，要么依赖基于模拟的训练目标，这两种方式都可能导致高昂的计算成本。作者旨在克服这些局限性，提出一种更高效且无需昂贵预处理或训练模拟的算法。

Method: 提出的Acceleration Matching (AM)算法将原始插值问题提升到相空间（phase space），然后回归到一个显式的条件加速度场。该加速度场能够诱导生成随机的、平滑的轨迹，并且这些轨迹与给定的边缘分布（即离散时间点的快照分布）一致。训练过程仅需要位置数据，避免了训练过程中的轨迹模拟，也不需要昂贵的预处理子程序。

Result: 作者提供了充分的数值实验结果，表明AM算法在多个来自现有文献的基准问题上与现有算法相比具有竞争力或更优越的性能。

Conclusion: Acceleration Matching算法通过升维到相空间并回归条件加速度场的方式，有效解决了现有轨迹推断算法计算代价高昂的问题，在性能上达到或超越了现有方法，为轨迹推断问题提供了一种更加高效且简洁的解决方案。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>


### [342] [PRISM: Powerful Time Series to Image (TS2I) Representations for Multivariate Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03926)
*Mateusz Smendowski,Kamil Faber,Piotr Nawrocki,Nathalie Japkowicz,Roberto Corizzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，一个即插即用的元工作流，用以系统性地构建和评估基于图像的多变量时间序列异常检测（TSAD）表示。通过超过7,000个实验，研究表明精心设计的PRISM配置可与24个时间域基线竞争，在14个数据集中的10个上取得最佳VUS-PR，平均提升41%。研究还发现通道化（channelization）是关键且此前未被充分研究的设计维度，并提出MSM统计方案，相较PCA方案提升11-27%。此外，ImageNet预训练编码器可有效迁移至TSAD，冻结编码器保留92%的微调性能且训练速度快1.8倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列异常检测对表示选择高度敏感，尤其在多变量场景下。虽然将时间序列转换为图像在预测和分类任务上取得了成功，但多变量高维序列应如何映射到多通道图像、以及视觉骨干网络能否在TSAD中匹敌时间域基线，这些问题仍不清楚。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统性地研究图像表示在TSAD中的潜力。

Method: PRISM是一个即插即用的元工作流，用于系统性地构建和评估基于图像的多变量TSAD表示。方法包含两个核心贡献：(1) 构建一个涵盖超过7,000个实验的评估框架，对比多种图像编码配置与24个时间域基线；(2) 识别通道化（即多通道图像通道维度的构建方式）为关键设计维度，并提出MSM（一种基于统计的方案）来替代PCA等现有通道化方案，在表征性能上实现11-27%的提升。

Result: 在超过7,000个实验中，精心设计的PRISM配置在14个数据集中的10个上取得最佳VUS-PR，平均提升41%。MSM通道化方案相较PCA方案在性能上提升11-27%。此外，ImageNet预训练编码器能够有效迁移至TSAD任务，冻结编码器保留了92%的微调性能，同时训练速度快1.8倍。

Conclusion: 基于图像的表示在多变量TSAD中是可行且具有竞争力的方法，PRISM提供了系统化的构建与评估框架。通道化是影响性能的关键设计维度，而MSM方案显著优于现有方案。预训练视觉编码器的有效迁移进一步降低了计算成本，为TSAD的实际部署提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection (TSAD) underpins applications in predictive maintenance, finance, and cloud computing, however performance remains sensitive to representation choices, especially in multivariate settings. While transforming time series into images has shown success in forecasting and classification, it remains unclear how multivariate, high-dimensional series should be mapped to multi-channel images and whether vision backbones can match time-domain baselines in TSAD. We introduce PRISM, a plug-and-play meta-workflow enabling systematic construction and evaluation of image-based representations for multivariate TSAD. Our evaluation spanning over 7,000 experiments shows that well-designed PRISM configurations are competitive with 24 time-domain baselines, achieving the best VUS-PR on 10 of 14 datasets, with an average improvement of 41% over the best competing method on those datasets. Further, we identify channelization - how the channel dimension of multi-channel images is constructed - as a critical and previously understudied design dimension, and introduce MSM, a novel statistics-based scheme achieving 11-27% gains over PCA-based alternatives. Finally, ImageNet-pretrained encoders transfer effectively to TSAD, with frozen encoders retaining 92% of fine-tuned performance while training 1.8 times faster. Our code is available at: https://github.com/Smendowski/PRISM.

</details>


### [343] [A Physics-Flavored Transformer Network for Parametrizing Contraction Dynamics of Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03927)
*Mattias Luber,Timo Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种物理启发神经网络（PFNN），将拉伸指数物理模型嵌入CNN-Transformer架构，通过混合训练范式自动从力-时间曲线中提取肌肉组织的动力学参数，实现高保真的动力学表型分析，弥合了理想生物物理模型与噪声体外实验数据之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化骨骼肌组织（ESMs）的功能表征往往依赖峰值力等简单指标，忽略了关键的动力学信息。虽然机制模型能捕捉这些动力学特征，但其数学复杂度阻碍了规模化应用和领域推广，因此需要一种既能提取物理意义参数又可扩展的自动化方法。

Method: 将拉伸指数（stretched-exponential）物理模型嵌入CNN-Transformer架构，形成PFNN。采用混合训练范式：先在合成数据上训练以建立"物理直觉"，再在未标记的真实测量数据上进行无监督自对齐学习。直接输入力-时间曲线并输出具有物理意义的参数。

Result: 该物理启发方法在多种收缩表型和细胞系上（包括杜氏肌营养不良症模型）实现了高保真的参数化，证明其在复杂生物学场景下具有优秀的泛化能力和准确度。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了可扩展、自优化的高通量生物物理研究工具，连接了理想化生物物理模型与噪声in vitro数据之间的差距，为高通量生物物理筛选提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Engineered Skeletal Muscle Tissues (ESMs) have become a key structure for biomedical disease modeling and pharmacological screening, yet their functional characterization often relies on simplistic metrics like peak force, discarding critical kinetic information. This is partially due to the high level of mathematical complexity which mechanistic models introduce to capture these dynamics. Hence, exactly the complexity prevents scalable application and widespread adaptation in the field. Here we present a Physics-Flavored Neural Network (PFNN) that automates the kinetic phenotyping of ESMs. Our architecture integrates a stretched-exponential physical model into a CNN-Transformer, enabling the extraction of physically meaningful parameters directly from force-time profiles. To address the scarcity of labeled biological data, we employ a hybrid training paradigm: the model develops a "physical intuition" on synthetic data before undergoing unsupervised self-alignment on unlabeled real-world measurements. Our results demonstrate that this physics-flavored approach achieves high-fidelity parameterization across diverse contractile phenotypes and cell lines, including Duchenne Muscular Dystrophy models. Our scalable, self-improving pipeline bridges the gap between idealized biophysics and noisy \emph{in vitro} data, providing a robust tool for high-throughput biophysical research.

</details>


### [344] [Latent Reward Registers for Diffusion Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03929)
*Yuanshen Guan,Zipeng Feng,Zhiwei Xiong,Peiqin Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Reward Registers机制，通过在冻结的Diffusion Transformer输入序列中前置可学习、位置无关的寄存器token，直接从中间噪声潜变量估计终端奖励，从而解决扩散模型对齐人类偏好中的时序信用分配难题。配套的RG-OPD（训练策略）和RGS（推理策略）分别实现了高效的对策略奖励蒸馏和免训练奖励引导采样，在多个指标上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型与人类偏好对齐通常依赖最终生成样本上的稀疏终端奖励，但由于去噪是多步过程，这种稀疏奖励带来了严重的时序信用分配（temporal credit assignment）挑战。作者希望从中间噪声潜变量直接提取奖励证据，实现全过程的密集可微奖励信号。

Method: 方法包含三个核心组件：(1) Latent Reward Registers——在冻结的DiT输入序列前端插入可学习的、位置无关的寄存器token，独立地从中间潜变量读出奖励估计，不改变生成器隐藏状态或速度场；(2) Reward-Gradient On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD)——沿对策略轨迹蒸馏奖励引导的更新，避免标准策略梯度中昂贵的rollout；(3) Reward-Guided Sampling (RGS)——在推理阶段通过幅度匹配的奖励梯度引导轨迹，无需参数更新。

Result: 在高噪声水平（u=0.8）下，寄存器在已评估的潜奖励模型中达到最高成对准确率。RG-OPD优于在线强化学习基线，同时节省高达33倍GPU小时；RGS在免训练方法中达到新SOTA，同时改进对齐和感知指标。

Conclusion: Latent Reward Registers通过全去噪过程中提供密集、可微的奖励信号，有效解决了稀疏终端奖励带来的时序信用分配问题。RG-OPD和RGS两种对齐策略分别在训练效率和推理性能上展现了显著优势，代码和权重已开源。

Abstract: Aligning diffusion models with human preferences usually relies on a sparse terminal reward evaluated on the final generated samples, presenting a severe temporal credit-assignment challenge across the multi-step denoising process. We propose Latent Reward Registers, a mechanism that estimates terminal preference directly from intermediate noisy latents by prepending learnable, position-free register tokens to the input sequence of a frozen Diffusion Transformer (DiT). This independent readout mechanism extracts latent reward evidence without altering the generator's hidden states or velocity field. The resulting dense, differentiable reward signal throughout the full denoising process facilitates two alignment strategies. For training, Reward-Gradient On-Policy Distillation (RG-OPD) distills reward-guided updates along on-policy trajectories, bypassing the computationally expensive rollouts of standard policy gradients. For inference, Reward-Guided Sampling (RGS) steers trajectories via magnitude-matched reward gradients without parameter updates. Empirically, at high noise levels (u = 0.8), the registers reach the highest pairwise accuracy among the evaluated latent reward models. Furthermore, RG-OPD outperforms online reinforcement learning baselines while reducing GPU hours by up to 33x, and RGS establishes a new state-of-the-art among training-free methods, strictly enhancing both alignment and perceptual metrics. Code and weights are available at https://github.com/Guanys-dar/latent-reward-register

</details>


### [345] [Muon Meets Mamba: Spectral Optimization for State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03941)
*Arslan Battalov,Karim Kramin,Alexander Markotenko,Sofia Sinitsina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Mamba-2 130M模型上对比了Muon优化器与AdamW，发现Muon的收益高度局部化：仅在输出投影上使用Muon效果最佳，优于在输入投影或两者同时使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器通过Newton-Schulz迭代对权重矩阵更新进行正交化，在谱范数下执行最速下降，但几乎所有实验证据都来自Transformer模型，其在状态空间模型上的表现尚未被充分研究。

Method: 采用受控实验协议，在Mamba-2 130M模型上仅改变哪些权重组使用Muon训练，与AdamW进行系统对比，并测试两个语料库和两种token预算。

Result: 仅在输出投影上使用Muon优于在输入投影或两者都使用的情况；优势主要体现在token效率上；在超过计算最优点的训练中依然保持；条件数下降并不解释收益来源。

Conclusion: Muon在Mamba-2上的收益是局部化的且与条件数无关，提示其作用机制可能需要重新审视，而非简单的谱条件改善。

Abstract: Muon is a recent optimizer that orthogonalizes the update to each weight matrix with a Newton-Schulz iteration, which performs steepest descent under the spectral norm. Almost all the evidence for it comes from Transformer models, and its behavior on state-space models is largely unreported. We compare Muon with AdamW on Mamba-2 130M under a controlled protocol that varies only which weight groups are trained with Muon. The benefit is localized. Muon on the output projection alone beats Muon on the input projection or on both. The advantage is mainly one of token efficiency. It holds on two corpora and two token budgets, and persists when training continues well past the compute-optimal point. Conditioning does not explain the gain. Muon lowers the condition number of whichever projection it trains, but the better-conditioned input projection is not the one that helps.

</details>


### [346] [Assessment of Conditional Diffusion Model for Synthetic Histopathology Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03990)
*Seyed Kahaki,Shijie Li,Weijie Chen,Nicholas Petrick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了合成组织病理学图像质量评估方法的局限性，提出使用基于病理学预训练基础模型的改进型FID和IS指标，并辅以precision-recall指标进行质量评估。通过在四个基准数据集上训练条件去噪扩散模型，系统性地生成不同质量特征的合成数据集，并验证了改进指标与下游细胞核分割性能的相关性，发现病理特异性指标具有更好的判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成组织病理学图像评估方法（如FID和IS）依赖ImageNet预训练的特征提取器，可能无法充分反映病理图像的特征分布，导致对合成数据质量的评估不够准确。需要开发更适合病理图像领域特性的评估指标。

Method: 1）提出使用数字病理数据集上预训练的基础模型替代ImageNet预训练模型来修改FID和IS指标；2）引入基于precision-recall的额外质量评估指标；3）使用条件去噪扩散模型，采用两步训练方法在四个基准数据集上生成具有系统性差异质量特征的合成数据集；4）通过相关分析（Spearman相关）衡量合成数据质量指标与下游细胞核分割性能（AJI+和Dice系数）之间的关联。

Result: 病理特异性指标提供了更好的判别力。改进后的IS指标与AJI+的相关性r=0.6096（p=0.0122），远高于原始IS的相关性r=0.0708（p=0.7944）。此外，研究发现增加生成训练数据的多样性比提高单个生成图像的视觉保真度对分割模型性能具有更高的正相关。

Conclusion: 病理学特异性评估指标能更准确地反映合成组织病理学图像在下游任务中的可用性；合成数据的多样性对下游模型性能的贡献比单个图像的视觉逼真度更重要。这为合成病理图像的质量评估提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: Synthetic histopathology image generation has emerged as an approach that may address data scarcity in computational pathology, yet current evaluation methodologies may not fully assess synthetic data quality for medical applications. This work investigates and addresses limitations in existing evaluation metrics, investigating an approach for assessing synthetic histopathology image quality through domain-specific metrics and downstream task validation. We show that conventional synthetic data evaluation metrics such as Frechet Inception Distance (FID) and Inception Score (IS) may have limitations when applied to histopathology images due to their reliance on ImageNet-pretrained feature extractors. To address these limitations, we propose for consideration modified FID and IS approaches utilizing foundation models pretrained on digital pathology datasets, supplemented by precision-recall based metrics as part of an additional quality assessment. Using conditional denoising diffusion models trained on four benchmark datasets, with a two-step training approach, we generated synthetic datasets with systematically varied quality characteristics. We also measured the correlation between the synthetic data quality metrics with downstream nuclei segmentation performance using common metrics including the aggregated Jaccard index (AJI+) and the Dice coefficient. The study results suggest that pathology-specific metrics may provide improved discriminative power. Specifically, the modified Inception Score indicates higher correlation with downstream task performance (r=0.6096 with AJI+, p=0.0122), compared to the original IS (r=0.0708, p=0.7944). Our observations indicate that increasing the variety of generated training data has a higher positive correlation with segmentation model performance than improving the visual fidelity of individual generated images.

</details>


### [347] [Test-Time Scaling in Reasoning LLMs: Inference Regimes, Evaluation, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04001)
*Mohsen Hariri,Weicong Chen,Nahal Shahini,Vikash Singh,Kai Ye,Amirhossein Samandar,Debargha Ganguly,Sreehari Sankar,Yanyan Zhang,Shouren Wang,Jerry Peng,Biyao Zhang,Michael Hinczewski,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地建立了测试时扩展(test-time scaling)的统一理论框架，沿着三个轴——统计结构（三种扩展机制）、评估原则（将整个推理系统作为评估对象）、可复现性要求——对不同的测试时推理算法进行规范化描述和比较，并发布了超过20亿条完整推理轨迹的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前"测试时扩展"一词涵盖了多种结构迥异的推理算法（单轨迹延展、采样候选后投票/验证聚合、对未完成部分状态搜索），这些算法在统计结构、计算核算和失败模式上各不相同。将它们视为可互换的单一标量"预算"或将精度与推理协议脱钩报告，使研究结果难以跨研究比较。缺乏统一的系统化框架来描述和比较这些方法。

Method: 本文沿三个轴构建系统化框架：(1) 将测试时扩展形式化为自回归模型隐式前缀树上的预算推理，区分三种结构机制——单轨迹顺序扩展、叶级扩展（终端归约）、前缀级扩展；(2) 将评估对象视为整个推理系统，提出分离端到端系统性能与候选库诊断的评估原则，引入可恢复或界定量复采样常用指标的评估剖析，并要求协议匹配的计算与不确定性报告；(3) 规定推理协议的可复现性要求，区分精确重放与分布可复现性，并识别支撑各自的产物。此外还组织了开放权重推理生态系统，在多个基准上验证原则，发布超20亿条推理轨迹。

Result: 论文提出了一套完整的测试时扩展分析框架：定义了三种结构机制（单轨迹、叶级、前缀级）及其差异；引入了新的评估剖析（evaluation profile），其坐标和简单函数可恢复或界定量复采样常用指标；规定了协议匹配的报告标准；建立了精确重放与分布可复现性的区分；并发布了超过20亿条带有逐步丰富验证器和token级信号的完整推理轨迹数据集。

Conclusion: 测试时扩展不应被视为单一标量预算下的可互换过程，而应作为具有不同统计结构和失败模式的独立推理系统来分析和报告。论文的系统化框架、评估原则和大型数据集为跨研究的可比较性提供了基础，推动该领域向更严谨的方法论标准发展。

Abstract: Large language models can solve substantially harder reasoning problems with more inference-time compute. The term "test-time scaling," however, now covers diverse inference algorithms that extend deliberation along a single trajectory, sample completed candidates and aggregate them through voting or verification, or search over unfinished partial states. These algorithms differ in their statistical structure, compute accounting, and failure modes. Treating these procedures as interchangeable under a single scalar "budget," or reporting accuracy without the inference protocol that produced it, makes results difficult to compare across studies. We develop a systematic account of test-time scaling along three axes. First, we formalize test-time scaling as budgeted inference over the implicit prefix tree of an autoregressive model and distinguish three structural regimes: single-trajectory sequential scaling, leaf-level scaling with terminal reduction, and prefix-level scaling. Second, we treat the evaluated object as the entire inference system and develop evaluation principles that separate end-to-end system performance from candidate-bank diagnostics. We introduce an evaluation profile whose coordinates and simple functionals recover or bound common repeated-sampling metrics, and prescribe protocol-matched reporting of compute and uncertainty. Third, we specify reproducibility requirements for inference protocols, distinguishing exact replay from distributional reproducibility and identifying the artifacts needed to support each. We also organize the open-weight reasoning ecosystem by model-side and interface mechanisms, apply these principles to broad-knowledge, symbolic-reasoning, and competition-mathematics benchmarks, and assemble over 2 billion full reasoning traces for release with progressively richer verifier and token-level signals.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [348] [Light-Loco-Parkour: Versatile Perceptive Whole-Body Locomotion via Multi-Skill Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02653)
*Hongming Chen,Zhuoran Li,Hongxi Wang,Jiangpeng Hu,Ziliang Li,Peize Liu,QingRui Zhao,Xuhao Liu,Liang Pan,Ximin Lyu,Yuntao Ma,Tingxiang Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Light-Loco-Parkour (LLP)，一个端到端的感知全身人形机器人运动系统，仅依赖机载深度传感器和速度指令，即可自主决定何时行走、平衡、攀爬、下台阶或跨跃，无需参考输入、技能标签、手写门控或运动图，可零样本迁移至真实室内外硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人形全身控制系统存在两难：要么追踪表达性全身参考但缺乏地形泛化能力，要么在线响应地形但未充分利用手臂、躯干和膝盖。此外，现有方法依赖运动语料库、技能标签或手工状态机，缺乏自主技能切换能力。需要一种端到端、仅依赖机载感知的单一可部署策略来解决这一差距。

Method: LLP包含三个关键技术贡献：(1) 全身感知控制流水线：将RL训练的速度跟踪运动策略与从物体交互运动中学习的跑酷技能扩展相结合，使同一策略在开阔地形跟踪速度、在障碍物处执行全身穿越、之后恢复运动。(2) 从稀疏种子学习地形条件技能：仅通过将单个运动扩展为动态可行、地形配对的参考，覆盖不同障碍物几何，而非依赖大规模运动语料库。(3) 从奖励中学习自主技能切换：策略仅从深度和指令自主决定何时调用何种全身技能，无需one-hot技能标签、手工状态机或运行时运动生成器。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，LLP在基准地形和未见障碍物变体上均取得高成功率，同一策略可零样本迁移至室内外硬件实验，在户外场景下仅凭机载感知和单一可部署策略实现了自主感知全身人形运动。

Conclusion: LLP证明了人形机器人可在复杂地形中实现自主、端到端的感知全身运动，无需参考轨迹、技能标签或手工编排，展示了在户外场景中仅依赖机载感知即可完成全身跑酷式运动的能力。

Abstract: Existing humanoid whole-body control systems still fall short of the way humans move through cluttered terrain: they either track expressive whole-body references without terrain generalization, or react to terrain online while leaving the arms, torso, and knees largely unused. We present \texttt{Light-Loco-Parkour} (LLP), an end-to-end perceptive whole-body locomotion system that closes this gap with a single deployable policy. Conditioned only on onboard depth and a velocity command, the policy decides when to walk, balance, climb, step down, or vault, with no reference input, skill label, hand-coded gate, or runtime motion graph. Compared with prior humanoid systems, LLP makes three contributions. First, it introduces a whole-body perceptive-control pipeline that extends an RL-trained, velocity-tracking locomotion policy with parkour skills learned from object-interacting motions, so the same policy tracks velocity in open terrain, executes whole-body traversal at obstacles, and resumes locomotion afterward. Second, it acquires terrain-conditioned skills from sparse seeds by expanding a single motion into dynamically feasible, terrain-paired references across obstacle geometry, rather than relying on a large motion corpus. Third, it learns autonomous skill transitions from reward, letting the policy decide when and which whole-body skill to invoke from depth and command alone, with no one-hot skill label, hand-coded state machine, or runtime motion generator. Simulation and real-world experiments show high success across both benchmarked terrains and unseen obstacle variations, and the same policy transfers zero-shot to indoor and outdoor hardware experiments. These results demonstrate autonomous perceptive whole-body locomotion on a humanoid in outdoor settings, using only onboard sensing and a single deployable policy.

</details>


### [349] [Semantic Haptic Feedback Enhances Dexterous Robotic Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02780)
*Bingjian Huang,Sahar Aseeri,Jonas Schmidtler,Joseph Zhang,Sonny Chan,Andrew Doxon,Jom Preechayasomboon,Evan Pezent,Alberto Rigo,Amir Memar,Nicholas Colonnese,Chase Tymms*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义触觉"（semantic haptics）用于机器人遥操作，通过抽象触觉模式传达机器人关键状态信息（分为"确认"和"异常"两类），采用模块化触觉渲染管线并通过气压和振动腕带传递反馈，简化硬件需求并支持一对多的触觉模式-状态映射。在三项评估研究中发现，语义触觉在单臂任务中与其他反馈方法表现相当，但在双臂任务中显著优于感觉触觉和视觉反馈，具有更低的任务负荷、更高的情境感知和整体偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作触觉反馈方法致力于复现真实交互中的高保真感官触觉，但其性能受限于传感和反馈硬件能力，且可能导致更高的操作者工作负荷。因此需要一种更简洁、抽象的触觉反馈方式来传递关键信息。

Method: 提出语义触觉概念，将机器人状态分为"确认"（Confirmations）和"异常"（Exceptions）两类；在机器人仿真中实现模块化触觉渲染管线；通过气压式（pneumatic）和振动式（vibrotactile）腕带向操作者传递语义触觉反馈；设计三项评估研究来确定拾放遥操作任务中最有效的语义触觉设计，并将语义触觉与感觉触觉、视觉反馈进行对比。

Result: 语义触觉在单臂任务中与其他反馈方法（感觉触觉、视觉反馈）表现相似；但在双臂任务中表现出更优性能，降低了任务工作负荷，提高了情境感知能力，并获得操作者整体偏好。

Conclusion: 语义触觉作为简化硬件需求且支持一对多映射的抽象触觉反馈方式，在遥操作尤其是双臂复杂任务中具有明显优势，是一种有前景的替代高保真触觉反馈的方案。

Abstract: In robot teleoperation, haptic feedback can be used to help human operators accomplish dexterous manipulation tasks. However, existing haptic feedback methods try to replicate high-fidelity sensory haptics that are felt in real world interactions, which are constrained by the sensing and feedback hardware capability and may lead to higher workload.
  To addresses these limitations, this work introduces semantic haptics for teleoperation, which uses abstract haptic patterns to convey critical information about robot states. We categorize robot states into "Confirmations" and "Exceptions", implement a modular haptic rendering pipeline in robot simulation, and deliver semantic haptic feedback to operators through pneumatic and vibrotactile wristbands. This simplifies hardware requirements and enables one-to-many mappings between haptic patterns and robot states.
  Through three evaluation studies, we identify the most effective semantic haptic design for a common pick and place teleoperation task and compare semantic haptics to other teleoperation feedback approaches including sensory haptics and visual feedback. Results suggest that while semantic haptics performs similarly as other feedback in unimanual tasks, it achieves superior performance in bimanual tasks, with reduced task workload, increased situational awareness, and overall preference.

</details>


### [350] [Toward Certified Functional Safety for Industrial Humanoid Robots: The Fail-Passive Gap and a Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02809)
*Caiwu Ding,Tao Cui,Lingyun Wang,Chengtao Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出工业人形机器人功能安全认证的核心障碍——"故障-被动失效缺口"（fail-passive gap）：腿部机器人安全态是主动受控态，违背ISO 13849-1/EN 60204-1基于断电的故障-被动假设。文章用认证的外部安全链（光幕、急停、安全PLC、PROFIsafe）精确定位不可认证元素，不主张端到端PL e/SIL 3认证，并在Unitree G1 EDU分拣单元上验证，贡献了人形专用主动安全态分析和计时预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业人形机器人受困于腿部平台功能安全认证不成熟，而非运动或操作能力不足。其根本困难在于腿部机器人的安全态是主动受控状态，违反ISO 13849-1/EN 60204-1的故障-被动假设——对步行双足机器人断电会导致不受控摔倒，经典断电本身即成危害。

Method: 以经过认证的外部安全链（光幕、急停、故障安全输入、故障安全PLC、无线PROFIsafe）作为测量仪器来精确定位缺口：因外部链闭合且可用成熟方法（PFHD、DC、CCF、PL/SILCL）量化，残余不可认证元素被定位到机器人侧反应链。采用西门子故障安全S7-1500急停参考，展示其可认证的Reaction子系统是接触器断电（停止类别0），恰为平衡人形机器人无法拥有的元素。在Unitree G1 EDU分拣单元验证，贡献人形主动安全态分析及带来源标记的计时预算。将工业SDA控制器置于机器人上并与平衡策略同置，使PROFINET/PROFIsafe接收移至标准化IEC 61131-3接口。

Result: 明确了外部"闭合且可量化"的安全链可精确定位剩余不可认证元素为机器人侧反应链；确认SDA控制器与平衡策略之间的接口是缺口集中位置；由于G1机载计算非安全评级硬件，该端点不是认证安全运行时，反而强化而非解决了故障-被动缺口。

Conclusion: 人形机器人无法采用基于断电的停止类别0作为可认证安全反应，故障-被动缺口被精确定位到SDA与平衡策略接口，该方法不主张端到端认证，但提供了将缺口隔离到明确接口的实证框架。

Abstract: Industrial humanoid robots are constrained less by locomotion or manipulation capability than by the immaturity of functional safety certification for legged platforms. The root difficulty is that the safe state of a legged robot is an actively-controlled state, which violates the fail-passive assumption underlying ISO~13849-1 / EN~60204-1: removing power from a walking biped causes an uncontrolled fall, so classical de-energization is itself a hazard. We term this the fail-passive gap and use a certified external safety chain (light curtain, emergency stop, fail-safe input, fail-safe PLC, and wireless PROFIsafe) as an instrument to locate it precisely: because the external chain is closed and quantifiable with established methods (PFHD, DC, CCF, PL/SILCL), the residual uncertifiable element is pinpointed to the robot-side reaction chain. Using a Siemens fail-safe S7-1500 emergency-stop reference, we show its certifiable Reaction subsystem is contactor-based power removal (Stop Category~0)---exactly the element a balancing humanoid cannot have. We deliberately do not claim end-to-end certified PL~e / SIL~3. We validate the approach on a Unitree G1 EDU pick-and-place cell in a 3m x 1.5m semi-enclosed workspace, and contribute a humanoid-specific analysis of the active safe state (fall-as-hazard, single-support stop bounds, balancing-policy residual risk, ISO~13855 separation) and a provenance-labeled timing budget. Hosting an industrial software-defined automation (SDA) controller on the robot, co-located with the balancing policy, moves robot-side PROFINET/PROFIsafe reception onto a standardized IEC~61131-3 interface; because the G1's onboard compute is not safety-rated hardware, this endpoint is not a certified safety runtime, which reinforces rather than resolves the fail-passive gap and localizes it to the SDA-to-balancing-policy interface.

</details>


### [351] [Staying on Spec: Real-Time Monitoring under Uncertainty with a Maritime Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02811)
*Elizabeth Dietrich,Hanna Krasowski,Emir Cem Gezer,Roger Skjetne,Asgeir Johan Sørensen,Murat Arcak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实时的数据驱动监测框架，利用数据驱动的可达集在不确定性下评估复杂任务和安全规格，并在海事导航中进行了实例化，通过仿真和硬件实验验证了其鲁棒性和相比现有指标的改进风险检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统必须在不确定性下运行，同时满足复杂任务和安全规格。现有的规格监测方法通常需要大量数据或显式的不确定性分布，这在实践中难以满足，因此需要一种数据需求更低的实时监测方案。

Method: 提出一个实时监测框架，通过数据驱动的可达集来评估规格满足情况，减少对数据量的依赖。针对海事导航场景，构建了数据高效的可达集构造管线，并推导出适合实时部署的监测公式。规格由交通规则（如避碰规则）衍生而来。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明，该框架在现实扰动下实现了鲁棒的监测，与最先进的度量指标相比，在风险检测方面取得了改进。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了数据驱动可达集可用于实时监测复杂规格，在降低数据需求的同时提升风险检测性能，为不确定性下的机器人安全监测提供了一种实用且可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic systems must operate under uncertainty while satisfying complex task and safety specifications. Monitoring such specifications under uncertainty remains challenging, as existing formulations typically require extensive data or explicit uncertainty distributions. In this paper, we propose a real-time monitoring framework that reduces data requirements by leveraging data-driven reachable sets for specification evaluation. We instantiate the framework for maritime navigation, where complex specifications arise from traffic rules. We develop a data-efficient pipeline for constructing reachable sets and derive a monitoring formulation suitable for real-time deployment. Simulation and hardware experiments demonstrate robust monitoring under realistic disturbances, achieving improved risk detection compared to state-of-the-art metrics.

</details>


### [352] [Biconvex Optimization for Smooth Minimum-Time Trajectories around Convex Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02834)
*Peter Werner,Tobia Marcucci,Daniela Rus*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双凸优化的最小时间运动规划方法，通过变量替换联合凸化最小时间目标与任意阶导数约束，利用时变分离平面处理碰撞规避，将问题转化为双凸规划并交替优化求解。方法保证收敛、支持任意时刻求解，在无人机导航和双臂卸载实验中展现出高鲁棒性和高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分解式运动规划器对初始化敏感且仅能处理受限问题类别，而凸优化方法在处理避障时往往受限于局部最小值。需要一个既能处理任意阶导数约束、保证收敛、对初始化鲁棒的运动规划方法。

Method: 基于双凸规划的求解框架：(1) 通过变量替换将最小时间目标和所有导数约束联合凸化；(2) 利用时变分离平面将避障约束线性化为交替更新；(3) 交替执行两个子问题——计算最大间隔分离平面与优化轨迹；(4) 仅对当前迭代碰撞的障碍物添加平面，使轨迹能够绕开障碍物并逃离局部最小值。

Result: 在无人机导航和双臂箱卸载实验中，该方法可靠地产生高质量轨迹，计算时间与最先进的分解式运动规划器相当，但能处理更广泛的问题类别，且对不良初始化具有显著更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提双凸方法为最小时间运动规划提供了一种保证收敛、支持任意时刻求解、具备任意阶导数约束能力的通用框架，在鲁棒性和适用性上优于现有方法。

Abstract: We present a biconvex approach for minimum-time motion planning around convex obstacles that is guaranteed to converge, is anytime, and supports derivative constraints to arbitrary order. We jointly convexify the minimum-time objective and all derivative constraints through a change of variables, and handle collision avoidance via time-varying separating planes, reducing the problem to a biconvex program. This program is solved by alternating between computing maximum-margin separating planes and optimizing the trajectory. By only adding planes for obstacles that the current iterate collides with, the trajectory can jump around obstacles and escape local minima. The method is guaranteed to converge starting from a simple collision-free polygonal curve. In our experiments on drone navigation and dual-arm bin unloading, we find that the proposed method reliably produces high-quality trajectories with computation times comparable to state-of-the-art decomposition-based motion planners, while handling a larger class of problems and being substantially more robust to bad initialization. Project page:https://wernerpe.github.io/bmtp-website/

</details>


### [353] [Control Barrier Functions via Minkowski Operations for Safe Navigation among Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02886)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Calin Belta,Michael Otte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多面体机器人-多面体障碍物的精确有符号距离函数（SDF）公式，并将其与非光滑控制屏障函数（CBF）集成，用于在保形环境中进行安全导航。该方法通过Minkowski运算和伴随凸规划计算精确SDF，并利用灵敏度分析推导出梯度的统一解析表达式，从而支持非保守的安全操控和安全性恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CBF方法通常用保守的光滑形状（如球体或椭球体）近似多面体，以获得显式可微的距离函数，这种近似导致了对安全区域的保守估计，无法充分利用系统的动力学和几何特性。

Method: 提出精确的多面体SDF公式，通过Minkowski运算在无碰撞（正号）和碰撞（负号）两种情况下借助伴随凸规划计算精确SDF；利用2D Minkowski运算的几何性质和伴随凸规划的最优性条件，通过灵敏度分析推导出精确SDF梯度的统一解析表达式，并与非光滑CBF集成。

Result: 在纯平移案例和三种单轮车模型场景（从不安全初始状态恢复、单障碍物和多障碍物避障）中验证了所提框架的有效性，与基准方法的比较表明该框架能够实现非保守的机动和安全恢复。此外，精确的旋转梯度揭示了由几何与非完整运动学耦合导致的一类此前被掩盖的局部极小值。

Conclusion: 所提出的精确SDF与CBF集成框架避免了保守近似，能够在多面体环境中实现更安全、更灵活的运动规划，同时揭示了几何与运动学耦合带来的新挑战。

Abstract: Safely navigating polytopic environments while respecting the dynamics, control, and exact geometry of the underlying system is a challenge in robotics. Control barrier functions (CBFs) synthesize safe control policies by rendering the safe set forward invariant, but many existing CBF-based methods approximate polytopes using conservative smooth shapes, such as spheres or ellipsoids, to obtain explicit differentiable distance functions. In this article, we propose an exact Signed Distance Function (SDF) formulation for a {\it polytopic} robot and {\it polytopic} obstacles and integrate it with nonsmooth CBFs. Leveraging Minkowski operations, the proposed method computes the exact SDF via companion convex programs in both the collision-free (positive-sign) and in-collision (negative-sign) cases. Furthermore, by exploiting the convenient geometric properties of 2D Minkowski operations and the optimality conditions of the two companion convex programs, we derive a unified analytical expression for the gradient of the exact SDF via sensitivity analysis. The exact rotational gradient further reveals a previously masked class of local minima induced by the coupling between geometry and nonholonomic kinematics. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework through a pure-translation case and three scenarios with unicycle models involving recovery from an unsafe initialization and single- and multiple-obstacle avoidance. Comparisons with baseline methods highlight how the proposed framework enables non-conservative maneuvers and safety recovery.

</details>


### [354] [Contact-Driven Localization in a Freeform Robotic Self-Assembled Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02895)
*Mohammadali Rashidioun,Michael Sosa,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于接触驱动的模块化机器人定位方法，仅利用机器人的二进制接触信息（是否物理连接）进行定位，通过虚拟力框架使机器人迭代调整位姿（吸引向已连接的邻居、排斥未连接的邻居），无需外部基础设施和高成本传感器，实现了可扩展的自由形态自组装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人中精确定位仍是关键挑战，尤其对自重构系统需要识别相对位置以形成多种结构。现有方法多依赖外部跟踪基础设施或高成本传感器，限制了在非结构化环境中的可扩展性和部署能力。

Method: 提出一种接触驱动的定位方法：仅利用本地通信中的二进制接触信息（两机器人是否物理连接）作为定位线索；引入虚拟力框架，机器人迭代优化自身位姿——对已对接的邻居施加吸引力，对未连接的机器人施加排斥力，从而实现分布式、无基础设施的定位。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法在塔式结构和悬臂结构组装过程中能够实现有效的定位，支撑了精确、可扩展、自由形态的自组装。

Conclusion: 该接触驱动的定位方法摆脱了对外部跟踪设施和高成本传感器的依赖，仅需极简的机载感知即可实现精确的分布式定位，为模块化机器人在非结构化环境中的大规模自组装提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate localization remains a key challenge in swarm robotics, particularly for self-reconfigurable systems that must identify relative positions to form diverse structures. Most existing approaches rely on external tracking infrastructure or high-cost sensors, which limit scalability and deployment in unstructured environments. In this paper, we propose a novel contact-driven localization method for modular robots that leverages only local communication through binary contact information (whether two robots are physically connected or not). To exploit these contact cues, we introduce a virtual-force framework in which robots iteratively refine their poses attracting toward dock-connected neighbors and repelling from non-connected ones. The method requires no external infrastructure and relies only on minimal onboard sensing. Simulations show effective localization during the assembly of towers and cantilevers, enabling accurate, scalable, free-form self-assembly.

</details>


### [355] [DeRP: An Algorithm for Self-Assembly of Power-Delivery Networks using Recursive Branching in Information-Limited Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02904)
*Mohammadali Rashidioun,Sangwoo Park,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Dendritic Recursive Pivoting (DeRP)框架，一种基于局部通信和方位感知的分散式多目标网络构建方法，用于机器人群体在非结构化环境中自主搭建电源网络。该方法通过递归分支操作近似Steiner树的Steiner点，实现无需全局规划的可扩展自适应网络形成，其网络长度约为全局最小值的125%，功率损耗降低至欧几里得Steiner树的65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非结构化野外环境中，通过预规划有线网络或电池方案为分布式设备持续供电面临巨大的基础设施和物流挑战，需要一种无需全局规划的自主电力网络构建方法。

Method: DeRP采用分散式框架，机器人作为导设备基于局部通信和朝向sink的方位感知自组装电源网络。网络从共同源点形成分支，在局部选定的枢轴点近似Steiner树的Steiner点，递归执行分支操作以实现可扩展和自适应的网络形成。

Result: DeRP形成的网络长度渐近约为全局最小长度的125%，功率损耗相对于欧几里得Steiner树降低至65%；在最多100个sink的情况下，仿真完成时间呈亚线性扩展。

Conclusion: DeRP能够在传统基础设施部署困难的场景中实现具有弹性的自适应电力输送，为机器人群体供电提供了一种可行的分散式解决方案。

Abstract: Delivering sustained power to distributed equipment in unstructured field environments using pre-planned wired networks or battery-based solutions presents significant infrastructure and logistics challenges. This paper presents Dendritic Recursive Pivoting (DeRP), a decentralized framework for multi-target network formation in robot swarms based solely on local communication and bearing-based sensing toward sinks. We envision a system in which robots, acting as a conduit, self-assemble a power network from a common source, forming branches at locally selected pivot points that approximate the Steiner points of Steiner trees to efficiently route to multiple Sinks. This branching operation is performed recursively to enable scalable and adaptive network formation without global planning. The proposed method is evaluated in terms of the total network length and estimated power loss, and is quantitatively compared against global baselines such as the Minimum Spanning Tree and Steiner tree solutions (GeoSteiner), which require complete knowledge of Sink locations. Specifically, we found that the networks formed by DeRP asymptotically form approximately 125\% of the global minimum length while reducing power losses to 65\% relative to Euclidean Steiner trees. In addition, we empirically characterize scaling behavior by measuring simulation completion time as the number of Sinks and robots increases, and find that this scaling was sub-linear for up to 100 sinks. The proposed approach enables resilient, adaptive power delivery in environments where deployment of traditional infrastructure is challenging.

</details>


### [356] [ValueFormer: A Causal Transformer Value Function with Stage-Aware Labels for Semi-Autonomous Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02958)
*Inkyu Sa,Konstantin Stulov,Rajat Bhageria*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ValueFormer，一种紧凑的策略无关因果Transformer，基于冻结的DINOv3骨干网络，在单次前向传播中同时输出平滑的蒙特卡洛价值（用于优势估计）和锐利的二值信号（用于在线错误检测）。通过在真实机器人双臂三明治组装任务（1,427个回合）上的实验，基于评论家推导的逐帧训练权重将任务完成率从70%提升至85%，且批量化bf16编码器将实时服务成本降低3~5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆训练的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略会静默失败：仅从动作流观察，崩溃的轨迹与正常进展难以区分，因为模仿学习不提供任何进展概念。强化学习虽能提供进展信号，但在真实机器人上成本高昂且变形食品难以仿真。终极成功/失败位在原理上可学习，但过于稀疏，无法指出轨迹何时出错。因此作者认为标签的逐帧设计（而非网络架构）才是难点所在——标签必须同时具备稠密性、连续性和正确形状。

Method: ValueFormer采用策略无关的因果Transformer架构，在冻结的DINOv3骨干之上，单次前向传播输出两种逐帧信号：(1) 平滑的蒙特卡洛价值V_mc用于优势估计；(2) 锐利的二值信号用于在线错误检测。这两个目标在设计中刻意产生相反方向的拉动力。失败回合使用阶段感知的"成功-而后-衰减"回报标注，保留了失败阶段之前的成功曲线；检测任务从错误区间（而非单一失败时刻）监督，使策略能从中恢复的错误也携带信号。配套的batched bf16编码器将实时服务成本降低3~5倍。

Result: 在真实机器人双臂三明治组装任务（1,427个回合）上，由评论家推导的逐帧训练权重将任务完成率从70%提升至85%（n=20时在噪声范围内）；batched bf16编码器使评论家能以2Hz频率与策略并行运行在单GPU上，实时服务成本降低3~5倍。

Conclusion: 作者的核心结论是：对于VLA策略的失败检测与进展估计，关键在于设计密集、连续且形状正确的逐帧标签，而非追求更复杂的网络架构。ValueFormer在真实机器人任务上验证了该思路的有效性，同时保持了策略无关性、计算高效性和实用性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies trained by behavior cloning fail silently: from the action stream alone, a collapsing rollout looks much like one making clean progress, because imitation supplies no notion of progress. Reinforcement learning would supply one, but it is impractical here, where real-robot experience is costly and deformable food resists simulation. The cheap alternative, a terminal success / failure bit, is learnable in principle yet far too sparse to say when a rollout went wrong. We argue that the per-frame label, not the architecture, is the hard part: to be useful it must be dense, continuous, and correctly shaped. We present ValueFormer, a compact policy-agnostic causal transformer over a frozen DINOv3 backbone that emits two per-frame signals in one forward pass: a smooth Monte Carlo value, V_mc, for advantage estimation and a sharp binary value for online mistake detection, targets that pull in opposite directions by design. Failed episodes are labeled with a stage-aware, success-then-decay return that preserves the success curve before the failure stage, and detection is supervised from mistake intervals rather than a single failure time, so mistakes the policy recovers from also carry signal. On a real-robot bimanual sandwich-assembly task 1,427 episodes), a critic-derived per-frame training weight lifts task completion from 70% to 85% (within noise at n=20), and a batched bf16 encoder cuts the live serving cost 3~5 times so the critic runs at 2 Hz alongside the policy on a single GPU.

</details>


### [357] [EmbodiedVAE: Disentangled Video VAE for Efficient and Controllable Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02990)
*Jiayi Luo,Hanxin Zhu,Chen Gao,Jiankun Wang,Cong Wang,Tianyu He,Jianxin Li,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EmbodiedVAE，一种专为机器人操作世界模型设计的视频VAE，通过双编码器-单解码器架构和非对称时空压缩模块解耦机械臂运动与背景环境，获得紧凑且可控的潜在表征，并引入基于最优传输的一致性模块保证运动时序一致性，相比SOTA视频VAE平均提升2dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜扩散模型（LDMs）使用的VAE是针对自然场景优化的，未考虑具身操作场景的独特特性，导致潜在表征既不紧凑也不可控，阻碍了LDM的高效训练和机器人精确控制。

Method: EmbodiedVAE采用双编码器-单解码器架构，配备非对称时空压缩模块，自动将机械臂运动与背景环境解耦，在保证整体紧凑性的同时提供明确的具身潜在表征以支持细粒度动作控制；并引入基于最优传输的一致性模块，显式强化运动保真度和帧间连贯性。

Result: 实验表明EmbodiedVAE在机器人操作场景中实现了优越的重建质量和更高的压缩率，同时在动作控制方面更加精确，相比最先进的视频VAE平均提升2dB PSNR。

Conclusion: EmbodiedVAE有效解决了现有VAE在具身操作世界中潜在表征不紧凑、不可控的问题，通过场景解耦和最优传输一致性模块，同时实现了高效压缩与精确动作控制，为机器人操作世界模型的构建提供了更优的潜在表征基础。

Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) have recently significantly advanced embodied learning in constructing powerful embodied manipulation world models. However, despite the remarkable performance, existing LDMs predominantly rely on Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) optimized for natural scenes while failing to account for the unique characteristics of embodied manipulation scenarios, yielding latent representations that are neither compact nor controllable, thereby hindering efficient training of LDMs and precise robotic control. To solve this problem, we present EmbodiedVAE, a novel video VAE that provides compact yet controllable latent representations tailored for the robotic manipulation world models. Specifically, EmbodiedVAE adopts a dual-encoder, single-decoder architecture with an asymmetric spatio-temporal compression module, which automatically disentangles the robot arm's motion from background environment, resulting in overall compactness while providing explicit embodied latent to support fine-grained action control. To further preserve the temporal consistency of learned robotic motion latent, we introduce an optimal-transport-based consistency module that explicitly enforces motion fidelity and inter-frame coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed EmbodiedVAE achieves superior reconstruction quality with high compression rate, while enabling more precise action control in robotic manipulation scenarios with an average of 2dB PSNR improvement over state-of-the-art video VAEs.

</details>


### [358] [A Wearable Stiffness-Rendering Haptic Device with a Honeycomb Jamming Mechanism for Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03002)
*Thomas M. Kwok,Bohan Zhang,Wai Tuck Chow*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用蜂窝夹紧机制的轻量级可穿戴触觉设备HJ-Haptic（20g），用于双边遥操作中的运动觉反馈，通过真空压力调节刚度（1.15至2.64 N/mm），实现物体刚度的实时渲染与手柄抓取力调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边遥操作中缺乏可靠的运动觉反馈，传统刚性设备重量大、体积大，难以在保持沉浸感的同时提供精确的物体刚度感知，需要一种轻量、紧凑且安全的可穿戴触觉解决方案。

Method: 设计基于蜂窝夹紧机制的穿戴式触觉设备HJ-Haptic，利用30 kPa真空压力驱动刚度变化（1.15-2.64 N/mm）；将该设备集成到遥操作系统框架中，通过三点弯曲测试评估蜂窝夹紧机制性能，并开展遥操作物体抓取任务实验验证功能。

Result: 在遥操作运动、刚度渲染和交互力反馈方面均实现了较小的均方根误差（RMSE）和强相关性；设备能根据实时夹爪反馈调整刚度，模拟直接用手抓取物体的感觉。

Conclusion: 蜂窝夹紧机制在增强遥操作场景及扩展现实环境中的触觉反馈方面具有显著潜力；真空压力的使用保证了气体泄漏或材料失效时的操作安全。

Abstract: This paper addresses the challenge of providing kinesthetic feedback in bilateral teleoperation by designing a wearable, lightweight (20 g), and compact haptic device, the HJ-Haptic, utilizing a honeycomb jamming mechanism for object stiffness rendering. The HJ-Haptic device can vary its stiffness, from 1.15 N/mm to 2.64 N/mm, using a 30 kPa vacuum pressure. We demonstrate its implementation in a teleoperation framework, enabling operators to adjust grip force based on a reliable haptic feedback on object stiffness. A three-point flexural test on the honeycomb jamming mechanism and teleoperated object-grasping tasks were conducted to evaluate the device's functionality. Our experiments demonstrated a small RMSE and strong correlations in teleoperated motion, stiffness rendering, and interaction force feedback. The HJ-Haptic effectively adjusts its stiffness in response to real-time gripper feedback, mimicking the sensation of direct object grasping with hands. The device's use of vacuum pressure ensures operator safety by preventing dangerous outcomes in case of gas leakage or material failure. Incorporating the HJ-Haptic into the teleoperation framework provided the reliable perception of object stiffness and stable teleoperation. This study highlights the potential of the honeycomb jamming mechanism for enhancing haptic feedback in various applications, including teleoperation scenarios, as well as interactions with extended-reality environments.

</details>


### [359] [Forbidden Region Dynamic Active Constraints in Robot-Assisted Minimally Invasive Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03010)
*Zejian Cui,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种基于深度相机的能量耗散型禁止区域主动约束（FRAC）策略，可在机器人辅助手术中适应呼吸运动引起的动态组织变形，采用精细网格表示（122,806个多边形，运行于43.48Hz），并通过体外轨迹跟踪实验验证了其安全性与时效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FRAC策略在临床遥操作手术应用中面临多项挑战：无法适应动态组织变形、难以维持能量无源性、实现速度受限等，需要一种能应对呼吸运动等动态组织变化的FRAC方法。

Method: 提出一种能量耗散型FRAC策略的流水线设计，利用深度感应相机捕捉呼吸引起的动态组织变形，采用包含122,806个多边形的精细网格表示方法，以43.48Hz频率运行，并设计由"虚拟"外科医生执行的体外轨迹跟踪实验进行定量评估。

Result: 在所有试验中成功维持预定义的安全距离，确认了任务安全性的有效性；与基于简单几何主动约束表示的FRAC方法进行对比研究，证明该方法整体上提供更稳健有效的引导，同时时间成本相当或更低。

Conclusion: 所提出的FRAC策略能够有效适应动态组织变形，在维持任务安全性的同时具备良好的鲁棒性和时间效率，具有临床遥操作手术应用的潜力。

Abstract: In robot-assisted surgery, Forbidden Region Active Constraints (FRAC) represent a control strategy that helps maintain task safety by generating anisotropic haptic guidance to surgeons. However, several challenges need to be overcome before FRAC can benefit teleoperative surgery in a clinical setting. These challenges include the ability to allow for dynamic tissue deformation, maintain energetic passivity, and speed of implementation, among others. In this study, we propose the pipeline design for an energy dissipative FRAC strategy, which accommodates the dynamic tissue deformation caused by respiratory movements, by utilizing a depth sensing camera. The proposed FRAC strategy adopts a fine mesh representation, with a total number of 122,806 polygons in the case study presented, while running at 43.48Hz. We designed in vitro trajectory tracking experiments conducted by a "virtual" surgeon to aid quantitative assessment of the method, including its effectiveness in maintaining task safety, which was confirmed by successfully maintaining a pre-defined safety distance across all trials. We also conducted comparative studies to investigate the robustness and time-efficiency of our method against other FRAC methods that rely on simple geometry AC representations. We demonstrate that our method provides a more robust and effective guidance overall, while maintaining comparable, if not lower, time costs.

</details>


### [360] [PACE: Adaptive Budget Allocation for Time-Efficient Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03034)
*Yuchen Huang,Xijiang Ying,Zhenhua Ma,Xiaxiang Yuan,Zhijie Gao,Jiayi Huang,Ruichi Mao,Jiazheng Zhang,Hongsheng Ti,Maotao Tian,Rong Shi,Lu Zhao,Shizhuang Zhang,Zhuo Cui,He Wang,Ling Liu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE框架通过交错思考和执行的架构以及动态预算分配器，将思考时间隐藏在动作执行窗口内，在Robotouille基准上相比ReAct+Think基线实现了67%的成功率提升和6.9倍的思考加速，使推理增强型LLM能够实际部署于对延迟敏感的具身系统中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理增强型大语言模型在规划任务上取得了显著进步，但在具身系统中的部署因推理延迟过长（通常超过每分钟）而不可行。根本瓶颈在于现有范式的串行特性：模型必须在所有推理完成后再执行动作，执行时间窗口完全未被利用。

Method: 提出PACE（Planning with Adaptive Cognitive Effort）框架，包含两个关键创新：1) Interleaved Think-Act架构，将认知处理与动作执行流水线化；2) Dynamic Budget Allocator，动态调整推理token预算以适配可用的执行时间窗口。

Result: 在Robotouille基准上使用Qwen3-8B-AWQ模型，PACE达到10%的成功率（相比ReAct+Think基线提升67%），思考时间加速6.9倍，66.8%的思考时间被隐藏在动作执行窗口内。

Conclusion: 策略性的认知努力分配能够同时提升规划质量和时间效率，时间感知的架构创新使推理模型能够在原本不切实际的延迟敏感型具身领域运行。

Abstract: Reasoning-enhanced large language models have achieved remarkable improvements in planning tasks, yet their deployment in embodied systems remains impractical due to prohibitive inference delays-often exceeding minutes per planning instance. The fundamental bottleneck stems from the serial nature of existing paradigms: models must complete all reasoning before any action execution, leaving execution time windows entirely unexploited. We introduce PACE (Planning with Adaptive Cognitive Effort), a framework that enables interleaved reasoning and execution through two key innovations: an Interleaved Think-Act architecture that pipelines cognitive processing with action execution, and a Dynamic Budget Allocator that adapts reasoning token budgets to available execution time windows. On the Robotouille benchmark using Qwen3-8B-AWQ, PACE achieves a 10% success rate-representing a 67% improvement over the ReAct+Think baseline-while delivering 6.9 times acceleration in thinking time compared to unconstrained reasoning. The framework hides 66.8% of thinking time within execution windows, demonstrating that strategic cognitive effort allocation can simultaneously improve both planning quality and time efficiency. These results provide evidence that time-aware architectural innovations enable reasoning models to operate in latency-sensitive embodied domains where they were previously impractical.

</details>


### [361] [CUDA MPC: A GPU-Native Solver for Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03051)
*Babak Akbari,Melissa Greeff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CUDA MPC，一种GPU原生的模型预测控制框架，通过并行ADMM算法拆分、融合CUDA内核及片上共享内存设计，实现在与CPU求解器相同时间预算内支持一到两个数量级更长的预测时域，解决快速动态和高维机器人系统的实时控制瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC依赖在线优化，在快速动态、高维模型或长预测时域的系统上受到计算瓶颈限制。现有GPU实现仅将GPU当作线性代数加速器，优化循环仍然依赖频繁的内核启动和高延迟的内存传输，未能充分发挥GPU架构的并行潜力。

Method: CUDA MPC采用三方面协同设计：(1) 算法层面采用并行时域方向上的ADMM拆分；(2) 执行模型上使用融合CUDA内核，将整个迭代求解过程完全在设备端运行；(3) 内存架构上优化中间变量驻留在低延迟的片上共享内存，并使用局部原子标志协议仅同步相邻时域块，减少主机干预、内核调度开销和全局内存流量。

Result: 在六个非线性机器人基准测试中，CUDA MPC在CPU求解器相同时间内支持一到两个数量级更长的预测时域：可在0.1秒采样间隔内求解100s前向避碰泊车问题，是唯一能在集中式10智能体集群中实现实时执行和无碰撞协调的求解器；相比张量框架的ADMM实现，融合内核速度提升高达965倍。

Conclusion: 通过算法、执行模型与内存架构的协同设计，CUDA MPC证明了GPU原生设计可以实质性地突破MPC的计算瓶颈，在长时域和高维场景下同时实现实时性与可行性，为GPU在实时优化控制中的深度应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) delivers constraint-aware control, but its reliance on online optimization limits its use on systems with fast dynamics, high-dimensional models, or long horizons. Existing GPU implementations typically treat the device as a linear-algebra accelerator, leaving the optimization loop dependent on repeated kernel launches and high-latency memory transfers. This paper introduces CUDA MPC, a GPU-native MPC framework that co-designs the optimization algorithm, execution model, and memory architecture for CUDA hardware. CUDA MPC pairs a parallel-in-horizon alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) splitting with a fused CUDA kernel that runs the entire iterative solve on the device. Intermediate optimization variables stay in low-latency, on-chip shared memory, and a localized atomic-flag protocol synchronizes only adjacent horizon blocks, minimizing host intervention, kernel-dispatch overhead, and global-memory traffic. Across six nonlinear robotics benchmarks spanning increasing state dimension and constraint density, CUDA MPC sustains real-time rates at horizons one to two orders of magnitude longer than CPU solvers: it solves an optimization-based collision-avoidance parking problem with 100 s of lookahead within a 0.1 s sampling interval, and is the only solver evaluated that achieves both real-time execution and collision-free coordination for a centralized 10-agent swarm, where acados and CasADi return no feasible solution and require 3.5 s and 4.5 s per solve. Against tensor-framework implementations of the same ADMM splitting, the fused kernel is up to $965\times$ faster.

</details>


### [362] [How Should Vision-Language-Action Models Use Proprioceptive State?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03052)
*Yiren Zhao,Ziyang Chen,Ziyang Rao,Pengteng Li,He Zhang,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Rushi Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验系统研究了VLA模型中机器人本体感受状态输入的三个核心问题：当前状态是否提升闭环控制、状态历史长度的影响及作用机制、状态应注入模型的哪个模块（视觉-语言骨干还是动作生成模块）。通过在flow-matching VLA上固定骨干网络、训练数据、动作表示和评估协议，实现了五种状态接口（离散状态提示、VLM前缀、动作前缀、状态专家、特征调制），并在45个原子任务和20个复合任务上进行评估，同时扫描了1到96帧的状态历史长度，最终提炼出可测试的状态感知VLA设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型几乎都使用机器人本体感受状态作为输入，但集成方式各异（序列化为文本提示、投影到视觉-语言前缀、或直接馈入动作专家），且几乎只使用单帧状态。这种设计上的不一致导致了三个悬而未决的问题：（1）当前状态是否以及在哪类任务上确实能改善闭环控制；（2）状态历史有多大帮助，其收益是来自真实的时序变化还是仅仅是增加了条件能力；（3）状态应该在模型何处注入——视觉-语言骨干还是动作生成模块。本文旨在通过受控实验系统回答这三个问题。

Method: 采用flow-matching VLA作为基座模型，严格固定骨干网络、训练数据、动作表示和评估协议；实现五种代表性状态接口——离散状态提示、VLM前缀、动作前缀、状态专家和特征调制，并确保实现细节匹配；在覆盖三个任务家族的45个原子任务和20个复合任务上评估，同时将状态历史长度从1帧扫描到96帧以分析历史状态信息对模型性能的影响。

Result: 实验系统性地回答了两个核心问题：当前状态是否提升闭环控制及在哪些任务上有效、状态历史的作用机制（真实时序变化vs条件容量增加）、以及状态应注入模型的具体位置，并将这些发现凝练为可测试的状态感知VLA设计原则。

Conclusion: 通过大规模受控实验，本文为状态感知VLA提供了系统性的实证答案和可操作的设计原则，有助于统一和指导未来VLA模型中本体感受状态的集成方式，推动更一致的模型设计和更好的闭环控制性能。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models almost universally take robot proprioceptive state as input, yet wire it in incompatible ways -- serialized into text prompts, projected into the vision-language prefix, or fed directly to the action expert -- and almost always as a single current frame. Three questions remain open: (1) whether, and on which tasks, current state actually improves closed-loop control; (2) how much state history helps, and whether its benefit reflects genuine temporal variation rather than added conditioning capacity; and (3) where state should enter the model -- the vision-language backbone or the action-generation module. We answer these questions through controlled experiments on a flow-matching VLA, fixing the backbone, training data, action representation, and evaluation protocol throughout. We implement five representative interfaces -- discrete state prompt, VLM prefix, action prefix, state expert, and feature modulation -- under matched implementation details, and evaluate them on 45 atomic tasks spanning three task families plus 20 composite tasks; we then sweep the state-history length from 1 to 96 frames to examine how historical state information affects model performance. The experiments yield systematic answers to all three questions, distilled into testable design principles for state-aware VLAs.

</details>


### [363] [Passively Safe Convex Guidance for Cislunar Rendezvous and Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03060)
*Ian M. Down,Connor Plaks,Matthew Bolliger,Michael Caudill*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了用于地月空间（cislunar）轨道中被动安全脉冲式交会与临近操作（RPO）的纯凸优化程序，方法被设计为NASA CAPSTONE 02任务的基线星上制导算法，并通过高保真蒙特卡洛仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地月空间交会与临近操作面临高非线性的动力学环境，传统非凸优化求解困难且难以在星上实时执行。本文旨在开发一种可高效星上运行、同时确保被动安全的凸优化方法体系，以支持自主交会任务的实时制导需求。

Method: 论文提出纯凸优化程序来设计交会、到达及中止机动。所有机动均在一体化框架中进行设计与验证，考虑了机动执行误差和导航不确定性，并针对自主场景下的高效星上执行进行了公式化处理。

Result: 通过使用计划中的相对导航传感器套件和测量频率的高保真闭环蒙特卡洛仿真，验证了所设计机动性能满足预期要求。该方法已被采纳为NASA CAPSTONE 02任务的基线星上制导程序。

Conclusion: 所提出的纯凸优化方法能够在地月空间非线性动力学环境中实现被动安全的自主交会与临近操作，具备星上实时执行能力，并通过高保真仿真验证了其在实际任务场景中的可靠性。

Abstract: This paper presents purely convex programs for passively safe impulsive rendezvous and proximity operations in cislunar orbits. Approach, arrival, and abort maneuvers are all designed and validated in the context of maneuver execution error and navigation uncertainty, and formulated for efficient onboard execution in the autonomous scenario. The outlined methods form the baseline onboard guidance routines for NASA's CAPSTONE 02 mission planned to demonstrate autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations capabilities in the southern 9:2 synodic near rectilinear halo orbit. High fidelity closed loop Monte Carlo simulations using the planned relative navigation sensor suite and measurement cadence verify the intended maneuver design performance.

</details>


### [364] [A Hierarchical Approach to Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Requiring Time Varying Forces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03103)
*Rishabh Shukla,Adithya Santhosh,Shaili Gandhi,Samrudh Moode,Satyandra K. Gupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DPA-FTG（Diffusion Policy Augmented by Fast Trajectory Generation），一种用于接触丰富型拆卸任务的双层控制框架。高层以5Hz运行条件扩散模型生成潜在参数以选择任务原语策略，低层以60Hz运行轻量级力感知策略作为神经阻抗控制器进行实时力调节，解决了扩散策略推理延迟与开环动作分块缺乏力反馈之间的权衡问题。在双臂电池拆卸任务上超越了包括Reactive Diffusion Policy在内的最先进基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在复杂多模态机器人操作中表现优异，但应用于接触丰富的拆卸任务面临关键权衡：迭代去噪过程引入推理延迟，难以支持高频控制（如凿击、撬动等动态交互所需）；而现有的动作分块（action-chunking）技术虽缓解延迟，却采用开环执行窗口，无法感知断裂等事件引起的快速力瞬变。现有视觉-触觉方法多聚焦于位置修正，未从根本上解决规划与力调节的频带分离问题。

Method: DPA-FTG采用双层解耦架构：高层（5Hz）利用条件扩散模型预测一系列潜在参数，用于从学习到的任务原语词汇表中选择执行策略；低层（60Hz）由一个轻量级的力条件策略充当神经阻抗控制器，实时调制执行过程以维持接触稳定性。该方法将低频规划与高频力调节解耦，实现了规划灵活性与力反馈实时性的兼顾。

Result: 在双臂电池拆卸任务（涉及柔性薄片分离）上的实验评估表明，DPA-FTG优于包括Reactive Diffusion Policy（RDP）在内的最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: DPA-FTG通过将扩散策略的高层规划能力与轻量级力条件策略的高频调节能力相结合，有效解决了扩散策略在接触丰富型任务中面临的延迟-感知权衡问题，为高频动态交互场景下的扩散策略应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion policies have shown strong performance in learning complex, multi-modal behaviors for robotic manipulation. However, their application to contact-rich disassembly tasks remains limited by a key trade-off: the iterative denoising process introduces inference latencies that makes high frequency control difficult, which is essential for realizing dynamic interactions such as chiseling and prying. Recent action-chunking techniques mitigate latency but use an open-loop execution window, rendering the system blind to rapid force transients caused by fracture events. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Diffusion Policy Augmented by Fast Trajectory Generation (DPA-FTG). Compared to recent visual-tactile approaches that focus on positional correction, DPA-FTG decouples low-frequency planning from high-frequency force regulation. At the high level ($5$ Hz), a conditional diffusion model predicts a sequence of latent parameters for selecting a strategy from a learned vocabulary of task primitives. At the low level ($60$ Hz), a lightweight, force-conditioned policy acts as a neural impedance controller, modulating execution in real-time to maintain contact stability. We validate our approach on a bimanual battery disassembly task involving the separation of a compliant sheet. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that DPA-FTG outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, including Reactive Diffusion Policy (RDP).

</details>


### [365] [Shooting for Contact: Contact-Implicit Multiple Shooting for Dynamic Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03116)
*Sergio A. Esteban,Jason H. K. Siu,Derrick Mach,Junheng Li,Vince Kurtz,Joel W. Burdick,Aaron D. Ames*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DSMS框架，通过在非线性规划中嵌入可微仿真器，将运动学可行的参考轨迹转换为动力学可行的全身运动轨迹，无需预设接触时序即可处理接触、摩擦、碰撞等约束，加速运动模仿RL训练并支持零样本sim-to-real迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动重定向方法主要关注运动学相似性，忽视了全身动力学、接触一致性和执行器限制，导致生成的参考轨迹难以被强化学习策略复现，尤其在接触丰富的任务中问题突出。

Method: 提出一种接触隐式（contact-implicit）、基于直接仿真的多重打靶（DSMS）框架。该方法在非线性规划中嵌入可微仿真器，在内部求解接触、摩擦、冲击、自碰撞和关节限制等动力学问题，同时施加跟踪、执行器与任务约束，无需预先指定接触时序或引入显式接触约束。

Result: 与现有重定向方法相比，DSMS加速了运动模仿RL的训练速度，生成的策略具有更高的成功率和更低的跟踪误差，并在Unitree G1机器人上通过命令条件化的接触丰富爬行和180度跳跃转身任务实现了零样本sim-to-real迁移。

Conclusion: DSMS通过将动力学可行性直接纳入参考轨迹生成流程，弥合了运动学参考与动力学可实现执行之间的鸿沟，提升了RL训练效率与策略质量，并验证了在现实机器人上的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Motion retargeting approaches often prioritize kinematic similarity over whole-body dynamics, contact consistency, and actuation limits, yielding references that are difficult for reinforcement learning (RL) policies to reproduce, particularly for contact-rich behaviors. We present a contact-implicit, direct simulation-based multiple shooting (DSMS) framework that transforms kinematically feasible references into dynamically feasible whole-body trajectories. By embedding a differentiable simulator within a nonlinear program, DSMS resolves contact, friction, impacts, self-collision, and joint limits internally while enforcing tracking, actuation, and task constraints without prescribing a contact schedule or introducing explicit contact constraints. Compared with existing retargeting methods, DSMS accelerates motion-imitation RL training and yields policies with high success rates and low tracking error. We further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on the Unitree G1 through command-conditioned contact-rich crawling and a highly dynamic 180-degree jump-turn.

</details>


### [366] [DigitCode: Symbolic Tokenization of Hand Motion by Anatomical Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03127)
*Haoyu Gu,Haotian Lu,Jingrun Du,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DigitCode，一种基于手部符号化表示（Hand Labanotation）的分层离散手部表示方法。DigitCode通过沿解剖学层级（骨骼、手指、整只手）适配、分组和分层HL字母表，将符号表示的量化误差降低了四分之三。关键发现是"单位"而非"量化器家族"是精度杠杆：固定单位时，无训练和可学习的量化器在重建上可互换；而沿解剖层次下移才能提升精度。每个手指token可充当免训练的、可编辑的句柄，用于修复畸形生成手和重定向到机器人，并发布了HandTok可复现测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部生成、理解和机器人学习的表示（关节角、MANO参数）虽然精确但缺乏结构：手指无法作为符号被索引或编辑，也无法标记姿势是否解剖学有效。离散符号表示能提供这种结构，HL已证明手部符号化的可行性，但其底层问题——符号应跨越的解剖学单位（骨骼、手指、还是整只手）——尚未被系统回答。

Method: DigitCode通过沿手部的单位层级（unit hierarchy）适配、分组和分层HL字母表，在单一编码中整合骨骼/手指/整只手三个解剖层级。采用T×40网格（每根骨头一个固定方向符号）为基础，通过分层组织实现跨层级的统一表示。论文通过控制单位层级与量化器类型的对照实验，分离出"单位"与"量化器家族"两个变量对精度的影响。

Result: DigitCode将符号表示的量化误差降低四分之三。实验证明：在固定单位下，训练-free与可学习的强量化器在重建上可互换；真正影响精度的是沿解剖学层级下移。每个手指token作为可枚举的单位，可提供免训练的、可编辑的句柄，成功用于修复畸形生成手和将手部动作重定向到机器人。

Conclusion: 手部符号化表示的关键设计维度是符号所跨越的解剖学单位，而非量化器本身。手指作为真实可枚举的单元，为连续表示无法解决的编辑、修复和跨域重定向任务提供了结构化的解决方案。作者发布HandTok可复现测试平台，以便手部tokenizer按单位进行逐一比较。

Abstract: Hand motion carries the finest-grained information in human activity, yet the representations behind hand generation, understanding, and robot learning are overwhelmingly continuous--joint angles or MANO parameters. These are accurate but unstructured: a finger cannot be indexed or edited as a symbol, and nothing marks a pose as anatomically valid. Discrete symbolic representations supply exactly this structure, and Hand Labanotation (HL) has shown they are feasible for the hand, writing motion as a T x 40 grid of one fixed direction symbol per bone. Building on this grid, we ask the question underneath it: the anatomical unit a symbol should span--bone, finger, or whole hand. DigitCode answers it by adapting, grouping, and layering HL's alphabet along the hand's unit hierarchy within one code, cutting the symbolic representation's quantization error by three quarters. The lever is the unit, not the quantizer family: at a fixed unit, training-free and learned strong quantizers are interchangeable on reconstruction, while moving down the anatomical hierarchy is what shifts accuracy. The hierarchy also tracks what downstream tasks need. Because a finger is a genuine, enumerable unit, one per-finger token doubles as a training-free, editable handle for jobs a continuous representation cannot address--repairing malformed generated hands, and retargeting them onto robots. We release HandTok, a reproducible testbed, so hand tokenizers can be compared unit-for-unit. Project page: https://digitcode-demo.github.io.

</details>


### [367] [POMDPs for Autonomous Science Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03155)
*Daniel Guirguis,Nathan Wallace,Hanna Kurniawati,Salah Sukkarieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SHM-POMDP框架，通过基于推断的物理属性而非原始传感器数据进行分支，使科学驱动的信念空间规划在保持POMDP不确定性量化的同时变得可行。实验表明其在扩展RockSample域和Cuprite高光谱地质勘探任务中均显著优于基线和信息论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主探索任务需要在传感器不确定性和计算约束下进行决策，但将科学表征整合到POMDP规划中因高维观测空间而难以实现。信息论规划器通过假设确定性观测来规避此问题，但牺牲了POMDP提供的原理性不确定性量化。需要一个既能保持不确定性量化又具备计算可行性的科学驱动信念空间规划方法。

Method: SHM-POMDP（Science Hypothesis Map POMDP）通过以下方式实现科学驱动的信念空间规划：(1) 在推断的物理属性而非原始传感器数据上进行分支，降低决策空间维度；(2) 利用学习得到的观测模型保留完整传感器信息；(3) 联合推理导航与科学属性，在不确定性下进行规划。

Result: 在50维观测的扩展RockSample域上，SHM-POMDP相比连续观测基线获得18.6%更高的奖励，单步计算时间减少32.9%。在基于Cuprite高光谱数据的真实地质勘探中，SHM-POMDP比最优信息论基线获得2.5倍更高的信息增益，仅使用均匀先验即可达到oracle性能的80%。

Conclusion: 将层次化概率模型整合到信念空间规划中，能够实现可计算且原理性的自主科学探索，其性能同时超越传统POMDP方法和科学感知的信息论方法，为解决科学驱动自主导航中的不确定性量化与计算可行性之间的权衡提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration missions require decision-making under sensor uncertainty and computational constraints, yet integrating scientific representations into POMDP planning has remained intractable due to high-dimensional observation spaces. Information-theoretic planners overcome this by assuming deterministic observations, sacrificing the principled uncertainty quantification that POMDPs provide. We introduce the Science Hypothesis Map POMDP (SHM-POMDP), which makes science-driven belief-space planning more tractable by branching on inferred physical properties rather than raw sensor data. This preserves full sensor information through learned observation models while enabling the planner to reason jointly about navigation and scientific properties under uncertainty. On an extended RockSample domain with 50-dimensional observations, SHM-POMDP achieves 18.6\% higher rewards and 32.9\% reduced computation time per step than continuous-observation baselines. On realistic geologic exploration using Cuprite hyperspectral data, SHM-POMDP achieves 2.5$\times$ higher information gain than the best information-theoretic baseline by maintaining beliefs and replanning adaptively---reaching 80\% of oracle performance using only uniform priors. These results demonstrate that integrating hierarchical probabilistic models into belief-space planning enables tractable, principled autonomous science that outperforms both traditional POMDP methods and science-aware information-theoretic approaches.

</details>


### [368] [Accelerating Human-Aware Robot Trajectory Generation via Diffusion and Consistency Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03159)
*Byeong-Il Ham,Hyun-Bin Kim,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向人机交互（HRI）中机械臂的约束运动规划框架。针对非冗余机械臂在末端执行器位姿完全指定时难以通过零空间次级任务处理碰撞避免等约束的问题，使用RRT/RRT*算法生成碰撞感知轨迹数据集，训练扩散模型并通过引导采样生成满足约束的轨迹，同时采用一致性蒸馏减少推理时间，并在损失函数中加入关节加权加加速度正则项以提升轨迹平滑度。仿真结果表明该方法在100ms内生成150条轨迹候选，保持高任务成功率并显著降低加加速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于非冗余机械臂，当末端执行器位姿被完全指定时，碰撞避免和自碰撞避免等额外约束无法通过简单的零空间次级任务来处理，使得在人机交互环境中同时考虑安全与运动学约束时难以生成可行的关节空间轨迹。

Method: 1) 使用RRT和RRT*算法生成碰撞与自碰撞感知的轨迹数据集；2) 利用该数据集训练扩散模型，通过引导采样生成满足约束的轨迹；3) 应用一致性蒸馏（Consistency Distillation）减少迭代扩散采样的推理时间；4) 在损失函数中引入关节加权加加速度正则项（joint-weighted jerk regularization），惩罚关节加速度的突变以促进轨迹平滑。

Result: 仿真结果表明：一致性模型在不到100ms内生成150条轨迹候选，保持较高的任务成功率，并且在应用加加速度正则化后关节和末端执行器的加加速度显著降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够高效地生成满足碰撞避免和运动学约束的平滑轨迹，兼顾推理速度、任务成功率与轨迹平滑性，适用于人机交互环境下的机械臂运动规划。

Abstract: This research proposes a constrained motion planning framework for robot manipulators in human-robot interaction (HRI). For a non-redundant manipulator with a fully specified end-effector pose, additional requirements such as collision avoidance and self-collision avoidance are difficult to handle as simple null-space secondary tasks. This limitation makes it challenging to generate feasible joint-space trajectories in HRI environments where safety and kinematic constraints must be considered simultaneously. To address this limitation, collision- and self-collision-aware trajectories are generated using Rapidly-exploring Random Tree (RRT) and RRT* algorithms, and the resulting dataset is used to train a diffusion model that generates constraint-satisfying trajectories through guided sampling. To reduce the inference time required for iterative diffusion sampling, consistency distillation is applied, and a joint-weighted jerk regularization term is incorporated into the loss function to promote smoother trajectories by penalizing abrupt changes in joint acceleration. Simulation results show that the consistency model generates 150 trajectory candidates in less than 100 ms, maintains a high episode success rate, and substantially reduces joint and end-effector jerk when jerk regularization is applied.

</details>


### [369] [PFM-HR: Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03227)
*Yukang Gao,Yi Gu,Yangchen Zhou,Xingyu Chen,Zhaorui Wang,Fanghai Zhang,Hanyang Cao,Zhengyang Shen,Ji Ma,Runhan Zhang,Lei Han,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了PFM-HR（Pose Flow Matching for Humanoid Robots），一种可直接在大规模无序姿态数据上训练的可复用流匹配先验。通过引入Pose Geometry Score（PGS）量化关节坐标变化与姿态几何的一致性，并用PGS调制追踪奖励以引导策略探索向结构化姿态变化方向进行，从而提升人形机器人物理追踪任务的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的运动先验要么依赖时序先验（需要有序运动片段），要么依赖姿态先验（对策略引发的姿态转换提供的指导有限）。需要一种既能利用大规模无秩序姿态数据、又能有效引导姿态间转换的先验方法。
